# Swimmers Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Make all six swimming species (Mallard, Gadwall, American Wigeon, Northern Shoveler, American Coot, Pied-billed Grebe) detailed birds in their late-September plumages, each feeding in its species' style, with the builder features, behaviours, cast counts, and startup painting they need.

**Architecture:**
- **The builder:** the detailed builder learns to float (a traced waterline at y = 0, a pivot on the water, hidden legs that paddle when shown), plus the parts the six need: bill profiles, hooks and a frontal shield, webbed and lobed feet, no tail, a part only some plumage versions have, and sections that tilt.
- **The behaviour:** `swim` gains four foraging styles on a shared swimmer body with flocks and spacing. Divers hide under the water and drop out of targeting.
- **The cast and startup:** cast entries give exact counts per plumage version, and plumage atlases paint in a Web Worker behind the title card.
- **The birds:** the Mallard is traced first as the worked example. The other five follow in parallel, one agent per bird, each held to a test file written here from its research.

**Tech Stack:** Vite 8, TypeScript 7, Vitest 5 (`environment: 'node'`), Three.js 0.186, zod 4, Preact.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-26-swimmers-design.md`. Read it first.
- Every number and mark comes from the six research documents: `docs/research/2026-09-25-{mallard,gadwall,american-wigeon,northern-shoveler,american-coot,pied-billed-grebe}.md`. Section 9 holds the numbers for the model, 10 the test thresholds, 11 the style settings.
- The detailed builder is described in `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-24-detailed-birds-design.md`.
- The previous round's plan, `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-25-dowitcher-pectoral.md`, shows the conventions this one follows.

## How this plan was checked

- **Part 1 (Tasks 1–13)** was written in three sections, each proved in its own scratch worktree of `c588f75`, one commit per task, with `npm test && npx tsc --noEmit -p .` green at every commit:
  - Tasks 2–6 (the builder and viewer)
  - Tasks 1 and 7–12 (the cast and behaviours)
  - Task 13 (painting)

  Each section was then replayed from its own text by a script. Every edit applied verbatim, every "see it fail" step failed as written, and every commit matched.
- **The assembled Part 1 was replayed in order** from this text alone, on a fresh worktree of `c588f75`. Every edit applied verbatim, every "see it fail" step failed as written, every bite check bit, and `npm test && npx tsc --noEmit -p .` was green at every commit. Test counts in the text are the replay's, in plan order:
  - 609 at the base
  - Task 1: 618 · Task 2: 639 · Task 3: 659 · Task 4: 674 · Task 5: 677 · Task 6: 680 · Task 7: 686
  - Task 8: 717 · Task 9: 728 · Task 10: 740 · Task 11: 751 · Task 12: 759 · Task 13: 773 · Task 14: 794

  The replay found two faults, both fixed in the text:
  - Task 12's added styles broke a test that used the Coot as a bare fixture.
  - Under full-suite load the swim simulations passed the 5 s default timeout; Task 7 now gives the suite 20 s.

  `npm run build` bundles the worker (`paint.worker-*.js`, about 250 kB) and leaves the viewer out of `dist/`.
- **Task 14 (the Mallard)** was traced, painted, and checked in the viewer while planning, on top of Tasks 1–13, as the worked example for Part 2. The Mallard measures 358 mm long, with a 53.0 mm culmen and its bill widest at 0.66 of its length, 1.08 times the base. Against the photo: the crown and head within 0.8 mm, the back 0.4 mm on average, the eye within 0.5 mm, and the tail tip within 0.3 mm. Task 14 was replayed from its text on top of Tasks 1–13 to the same tree. The five Part 2 test files were typechecked against the real APIs at that point, and fail only because those species have no `detail` block yet.
- **Parts 2 and 3** are written as procedures. Each bird's test file is given in full here, from its research document's section 10, and runs red until the bird is traced. The traced `detail` block is the bird's agent's output: the user chose one agent per bird, each working from its research document and the Mallard's file.
- If a step's output differs from what the plan says, stop and find out why rather than adjusting the test.

## Decisions made while planning

Each section's own list is kept at the end of this plan ("Decisions made while planning (section A/B/C)"). The ones that change the spec or cross sections:

- **Wakes every 1.5 s, not 0.9 s** (section B, Task 12).
  - At the old rate, the whole cast (every swimmer in its style, plus the six dowitchers) peaked at 38 live rings and pushed about 290 out of the 32-ring buffer in 20 minutes, half of them wakes.
  - With wakes every 1.5 s (the grebe research's measured rhythm), dabbling rings at size 0.3, and the coot's pump ring only while it swims to feed, there are 14–16 live rings on average, peaks of 28–32, and nothing is pushed out. A test holds it.
  - `MAX_RIPPLES` stays 32. This supersedes the spec's "wake rings as today".
- **The six swimmers get their styles at the end of Part 1** (Task 12, last steps), so the block-model swimmers already feed in their species' style before Part 2 details them. Without a style every swimmer is a dabbler.
- **A positive `tailTilt` lifts the detailed builder's tail** (section A, Task 4). It used to lower it; no shipped behaviour ever set it.
- **Two files outside section B's list** were changed, for good reason:
  - `src/birds/species/mudflat.test.ts` skips swimmers, since `PECK_BOTTOM` is now keyed by probe styles only.
  - `src/player/candidates.ts` is new, so the targeting candidates can be tested in node.
- **The dowitcher's chin is retraced with tilt** (section A, Task 5). The front outline is now within 1.8 mm of the photo's (it was 8.3 mm short), with no crease and every dowitcher test holding. Culmen/head moves from 1.89 to 1.81, inside its 1.8–2.0 bound.
- **One Web Worker paints every atlas** (section C). With 20 atlases it may take 1.3–5.5 s behind the title card; two workers is the next step if that's too long.

## Global Constraints

- **Frame rate:** 60 fps on an integrated GPU in Chrome, with every bird on screen. A detailed bird is one draw call (plus its shadow pass) and about 6k triangles. The dev overlay shows each atlas's paint time.
- **Art direction:** field-guide plate. Exact structure and painted field marks; no photo textures on any mesh. Patterns come from data (`detail.plumage`, `detail.variants`).
- **Interfaces:**
  - `BirdModel` does not change.
  - `PoseState` gains exactly `paddle`, `paddlePhase`, `reachDepth`, `reachAngle` (defaults 0, 0, 0, `Math.PI / 2`). With the defaults every existing species poses exactly as before.
- **Units:**
  - Metres: every size in `detail.measurements`, `detail.profile`, `detail.eye`, `detail.wing.wrist`, `detail.probeDepth`, `detail.float`, `detail.bill.hook`, `detail.bill.shield`, `detail.lobeWidth`, and `detail.parts`.
  - Multiples of the base width and depth: `detail.bill.profile`.
  - Millimetres on the bird: every pattern size in a layer.
- **The shorebirds build exactly as before:** a geometry fingerprint of each detailed species is a test (Task 2). The dowitcher's changes once, on purpose, in Task 5.
- **Validation:** species specs are validated when the module loads. A detailed species never falls back to blocks at runtime. `validateCast` checks plumage counts and styles against the species.
- **Zones:** convex polygons. Zone tests sample `terrainSurface`.
- **Reference photos:** downloaded outside the repo and never committed. Each species' `sources` credits its photographer and licence.
- **Tests:** Vitest runs in `environment: 'node'`: no DOM, no WebGL, no Worker. Rendering is checked by eye in the viewer and the game, with Playwright screenshots where a step asks.
- **Commits:** every commit leaves `npm test` green. Never commit `node_modules`, `.env`, `dist`, `data/raw`, `.wrangler`, or `.playwright-mcp`.
- **Worktrees:**
  - `npm ci` in each worktree. **Never a symlink or junction**: a forced worktree removal on Windows deletes through one.
  - **Never `git stash`**: the stash is shared by every worktree.
- **Part 2's parallel birds:** each bird's agent edits only its own species file and its own test file. A bird that needs a shared-code change stops and reports.

## Review Focus

Inputs the spec implies that no task's own feature tests would otherwise exercise, each pinned to a test in the owning task:

1. **Swimmers crowded at a zone edge.** Spacing must never push a bird against the zone clamp so that it jumps. Task 7: every frame moves a swimmer no further than its pace, with birds spawned at the edge beside others.
2. **A bird that dives while the player is identifying it.** The identification is dropped as if the bird left the reticle; nothing throws, and it isn't scored. Task 12: the candidates test with a submerged target.
3. **No Worker** (blocked, or erroring mid-paint). Painting falls back to the main thread and Begin still enables. Task 13: the fallback test with a failing fake worker.
4. **A hand-kept viewer link from before this round.** It opens as it did. Task 6: the state test that old hashes parse unchanged.
5. **The whole cast's rings.** With every swimmer feeding and six dowitchers stitching, no ring is pushed out before it fades. Task 12: the ring-budget test.

## Interfaces between the tasks

Collected from the sections, so an implementer of one task can see the names neighbouring tasks use.

### From Tasks 2–6 (the builder and the viewer)


**`PoseState`** (`src/birds/model.ts`, Task 2), exactly as the contract gives them, `defaultPose()` setting
`paddle: 0, paddlePhase: 0, reachDepth: 0, reachAngle: Math.PI / 2`:
- `paddle` 0..1: 0 hides a swimmer's legs (bone scale), anything above 0 shows them, folded back under the body; the
  stroke's size is `paddle` × 0.5 rad.
- `paddlePhase` 0..1: the stroke; the left leg is half a stroke behind the right.
- `reachDepth` metres ≥ 0 (a negative value counts as 0): a reach aims this far below y = 0, on top of `probeDepth`.
- `reachAngle` radians 0..π/2 (clamped): the steepest the bill may point below level during a reach.
- `tailTilt`: **a positive value now lifts the detailed builder's tail**, as PoseState's doc says (Task 4; it lowered
  it before, which no shipped behaviour ever exercised).
- `lean.x` on a swimmer pitches the body about the float pivot (the group's origin, on the water).

**`DetailSpec`** (`src/birds/detail/spec.ts`), new optional fields:
- `float: { waterline: number }` (Task 2): the waterline's y in the profile frame, metres. Required for
  `posture: 'swimming'`; the builder refuses a swimmer without it and still refuses `'perched'`.
- `bill.taper` becomes optional (Task 3): a bill needs a `taper` or a `profile`.
- `bill.profile: [s, width, depth][]` (Task 3): s 0..1 from the base, width and depth multiples of
  `measurements.billWidth`/`billDepth`, linear between points, s strictly increasing; replaces `taper`.
- `bill.hook: number` (Task 3): metres the tip drops over the bill's last tenth.
- `bill.shield: { length, width, thickness }` (Task 3): metres; a plate from the top of the bill's base up the forehead.
- `tail.shape: 'none'` (Task 4): no tail geometry; the loft's rear rings hand off from the tail bone (rear end) to the
  body (tail base), so `tailTilt` cocks the stern; `anatomy.tailTip` is the rearmost loft point.
- `feet: 'webbed' | 'lobed'` (Task 4); `lobeWidth: number` (metres, the middle toe's width across its lobes; default
  0.2 × `measurements.toe`).
- `parts: { kind: 'curl'; at: string; dx?: number; height: number; versions: string[] }[]` (Task 4): at most one per
  kind; `at` a station label, `dx` metres forward along the spine, `height` metres, `versions` keys of
  `detail.variants`.
- `profile[i].tilt: number` (Task 5): degrees, −60..60, default 0; positive swings a section's lower side forward.

**`Part`** gains `'shield'` (Task 3) and `'curl'` (Task 4). Atlas regions: `shield` { x 448, y 384, w 64, h 32 },
`curl` { x 256, y 416, w 64, h 32 }. Every earlier region is where it was.

**Bones:** `BONE_NAMES` gains `'curl'` at the end (Task 4), parent `body`; no earlier bone's index moves.

**`Measures`** (`src/birds/detail/measure.ts`), all metres, at rest, group space:
- Task 2: `backAbove` (highest loft point from the tail to the neck base), `crownAbove` (highest head vertex),
  `tailTipAbove` (`anatomy.tailTip.y`), `wingTipAbove`, `breastFrontAbove` (the frontmost loft point, from the tail to
  the neck base, lower than the neck base's spine point), `draft` (depth of the lowest loft point, positive),
  `waterlineLength` (the loft's length along y = 0; 0 for a standing bird).
- Task 3: `billWidestAt` (fraction of the bill from its base where the built bill is widest; the middle of a widest
  stretch), `billWidestRatio` (that width over the width at the base).
- `measure()` now caches its result per model (the bill scan is 101 slices; the viewer asks every frame).

**New exports:**
- `measure.ts`: `tailTipNow(model)` (Task 2), `billWidthAt(model, s)` and `billDepthAt(model, s)` (Task 3: the built
  bill's width across and depth top-to-bottom at fraction `s` from the base, metres, cut square to the bill's base
  direction).
- `fixture.ts`: `TEST_SWIM_DETAIL`, `TEST_SWIM_SPEC` (code `testduck`, a Mallard-sized dabbler, waterline −0.022).
- `mesher.ts`: `Mesher.spanAtHeight(y, indexFirst, indexCount)` (Task 2); `sweep(...)`, `lens(...)` (Task 4).
- `legs.ts`: `SWIM_TIBIA`, `SWIM_TARSUS`, `LegLayout.swimming`, `legLayout(detail, profile, swimming?)`,
  `buildLegs(m, layout, feet?)` returning the feet's closed ranges (Task 2, 4).
- `bill.ts`: `billScale(bill, sigma)`, `buildShield(...)` (Task 3).
- `extras.ts` (new, Task 4): `buildCurl`, `CURL_LENGTH`, `CURL_RADIUS`, `PartSpec`, `PartResult`.
- `loft.ts`: `loftWeights(u, profile, rig, rearToTail?)`, `buildLoft(m, profile, rig, rearToTail?)`,
  `LoftRig.tailBase?` (Task 4); `worstFold` measures each fold against the ring's own, possibly tilted, plane (Task 5).
- `profile.ts`: `ProfileSample.axis` (Task 5): the section's up direction, the normal turned by the tilt.

**`Anatomy`** gains `backTopY`, `breastFront`, `waterline: { back, front }` (Task 2), `shield: { base, top } | null`
(Task 3), `parts: { kind, base, top, shown }[]` (Task 4); `closed` gains `'shield'`, `'footR'`/`'footL'` (feet with a
web or lobes), and `'curl'` entries.

**Viewer state** (`src/viewer/state.ts`, Task 6): new hash keys `depth`, `angle`, `paddle`, `sink`, `bob`, `tail`,
`float`, `see`; `lean` now ranges 0..1.6; field views `bino15` and `scope35`.

**For section C:** Tasks 3 and 4 edit `src/birds/paint/painter.ts`'s `partFrames` (the bill's frame follows a profile;
frames for `shield` and `curl`) and import `billScale` from `../detail/bill` and `CURL_LENGTH`, `CURL_RADIUS` from
`../detail/extras`. A worker that imports `painter.ts` pulls these in (both are plain TypeScript over three.js maths).

### From Tasks 1 and 7–12 (the cast and the behaviours)


```ts
// src/birds/spec.ts
export const PROBE_STYLES = ['plover', 'sandpiper', 'dowitcher'] as const
export const SWIM_STYLES = ['dabbler', 'shoveler', 'coot', 'grebe'] as const
export const FORAGING = [...PROBE_STYLES, ...SWIM_STYLES] as const
export type ProbeStyle, SwimStyle, Foraging; export const isProbeStyle, isSwimStyle   // type guards
// src/behavior/probe.ts: PECK_BOTTOM is Record<ProbeStyle, ...>

// src/behavior/types.ts
interface FlockGroup { targetX; targetZ; retargetAt; spin?: Spin }        // was an inline type on BehaviorContext.group
interface Spin { pair; cx; cz; apart; dir; rate; angle; at; ends; spinning }   // a shoveler pair's shared turn
type Neighbour = Pick<BehaviorController, 'x' | 'z' | 'heading' | 'state' | 'spacing' | 'group' | 'submerged'>
BehaviorContext.species?: string                          // the species' code; the spawner sets it for every bird
BehaviorContext.neighbours?: () => readonly Neighbour[]   // swimmers: every swimmer in the zone, itself included
BehaviorController.spacing?: number                       // swimmers: metres kept from birds outside its flock
BehaviorController.group?: FlockGroup                     // swimmers: its flock's shared record
BehaviorController.submerged?: boolean                    // divers: all the way under; the spawner hides the model
export const between, ease, wrap                          // moved here from probe.ts, plus wrap

// src/behavior/swim.ts
createSwim(ctx, x, z)            // dispatches on ctx.foraging: shoveler, coot, grebe; anything else a dabbler
companyOf(foraging?, species?)   // a swimmer's flock radius, first-patch time, spacings, start spacing: for the spawner
// src/behavior/dabbler.ts: MALLARD, GADWALL, WIGEON, DABBLERS (keyed by species code), dabblerFor(code)
// src/behavior/{shoveler,coot,grebe}.ts: SHOVELER, COOT, GREBE, and each style's create function and settings type

// src/location/validate.ts
validateCast(loc, birds, specs: CastSpecs = SPECIES)      // specs: tests may pass stand-ins
// src/player/candidates.ts (new)
targetCandidates(birds: readonly BirdInstance[]): Candidate[]   // skips hidden birds; main.tsx uses it
```

- **Task 14 and Part 2 must set each swimmer's `foraging`** in its species file: `mallar3`, `gadwal`, and `amewig`
  `'dabbler'`, `norsho` `'shoveler'`, `y00475` `'coot'`, `pibgre` `'grebe'`. This section doesn't touch the species
  files (they are not in its list); until they are set every swimmer in the game is a dabbler, and none dives.
- **A swimmer's pose** follows the contract: `y` 0 afloat, `-0.073` a grebe at back-awash, `-0.4` all the way under;
  a tip-up is `lean.x` 1.40–1.48 (the Shoveler's 0.96) with `tailTilt` and `paddle`; a head-dip is `reach` 1,
  `reachDepth` 0.15 (a coot's 0.1), `reachAngle` PI/2; surface dabbling `reach` 1, `reachDepth` 0, `reachAngle` 0.5;
  the Shoveler's skim `reach` 1, `reachDepth` 0.008 (its face 0.035 while pottering and spinning), `reachAngle` 0.47;
  the coot's pump is `headBob`, 0.17 body lengths from end to end.
- **The block models** read `lean.x` as a roll (`procedural.ts`, unchanged), so until the detailed birds land a
  tip-up rolls a block duck onto its side. Nothing here changes that.

# Part 1: the pipeline and the Mallard

### Task 1: Cast counts per plumage version

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/location/schema.ts`, `src/location/schema.test.ts`, `src/location/validate.ts`,
  `src/location/validate.test.ts`, `src/birds/spawn.ts`, `src/birds/spawn.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `parseLocation`, `validateCast`, `expandCast`, `flockEntries`, `SPECIES` (all existing).
- Produces:

```ts
// src/location/schema.ts: a cast entry's plumage
plumage?: 'male' | 'female' | 'mixed' | Record<string, number>   // counts are whole numbers of birds, at least 1

// src/location/validate.ts
/** What validateCast reads from each species' spec: its plumage versions, and how it feeds. */
export type CastSpecs = Readonly<Record<string, Pick<SpeciesSpec, 'variants' | 'foraging'>>>
export function validateCast(loc: Location, birds: readonly Bird[], specs: CastSpecs = SPECIES): string[]
// new errors: `${species} has no plumage version ${v}`, `plumage count for ${v} must be a whole number of birds, not ${n}`,
// `plumage counts add up to ${sum}, not the count of ${count}`, and on a flyover
// `a flyover flock is one version; give plumage as a single version, not counts`

// src/birds/spawn.ts: expandCast deals a record's versions out exactly, in an order shuffled with the spawner's rng;
// flockEntries gives a flock no version from a record. `male`, `female`, and `mixed` draw as before, with the same
// random numbers, so a cast without records spawns exactly as it did.
```

```ts
  describe('plumage counts', () => {
    const catalog = [bird('mallar3', ['wetland']), bird('y00475', ['wetland'])]
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run them to see them fail**

Run: `npx vitest run src/location src/birds/spawn.test.ts`
Expected: 6 tests fail.
- `takes plumage as one version, mixed, or a count of birds for each version`: a `ZodError`, "Invalid option:
  expected one of "male"|"female"|"mixed"".
- The four new `validateCast` tests that expect an error (`names a version …`, `names counts that do not add up …`,
  `rejects counts on a flyover …`, `reports a malformed record without throwing …`): each gets `[]`.
- `assigns exactly the counts a record gives …`: every variant is `undefined`.

Three new tests pass already and must keep passing: the schema's rejection of bad counts (the old enum rejects every
object), `passes counts that name the species' versions …` (nothing checked plumage), and the flyover's missing
version (`pickVariant` found no such variant).

```ts
/** Plumage given as counts per version: each a version the species has, each a whole number, adding up to the count. */
function checkCounts(entry: Location['cast'][number], i: number, spec: CastSpecs[string] | undefined): string[] {
  const counts = entry.plumage as Record<string, number>
  if (entry.behavior === 'flyover') return [`cast[${i}]: a flyover flock is one version; give plumage as a single version, not counts`]
  const errors: string[] = []
  const versions = spec ? Object.keys(spec.variants) : []
  let sum = 0
  for (const [version, n] of Object.entries(counts)) {
    if (!versions.includes(version)) errors.push(`cast[${i}]: ${entry.species} has no plumage version ${version}`)
    if (!Number.isInteger(n) || n < 1) errors.push(`cast[${i}]: plumage count for ${version} must be a whole number of birds, not ${n}`)
    else sum += n
  }
  if (errors.length === 0 && sum !== entry.count) errors.push(`cast[${i}]: plumage counts add up to ${sum}, not the count of ${entry.count}`)
  return errors
}
```

```ts
export const DEFAULT_FLOCK_EVERY: [number, number] = [120, 300]

/** One version for a bird: the one named, a random pick for `mixed`, or none (counts per version are for versionsFor). */
function pickVariant(species: string, plumage: CastEntry['plumage'], rng: Rng): string | undefined {
  if (typeof plumage === 'object') return undefined
  const variants = hasSpec(species) ? Object.keys(getSpec(species).variants) : []
```

Then replace

```ts
  return undefined

```

with

```ts
  return undefined
}

/**
 * A version for each bird of an entry. Counts per version are dealt out exactly, in a shuffled order, so no version
 * always lands on the same bird of the flock; a version the species doesn't have (validateCast names it) is none.
 */
function versionsFor(entry: CastEntry, rng: Rng): (string | undefined)[] {
  if (typeof entry.plumage !== 'object') return Array.from({ length: entry.count }, () => pickVariant(entry.species, entry.plumage, rng))
  const known = hasSpec(entry.species) ? Object.keys(getSpec(entry.species).variants) : []
  const deck = Object.entries(entry.plumage).flatMap(([v, n]) => Array.from({ length: n }, () => (known.includes(v) ? v : undefined)))
  for (let i = deck.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
    const j = Math.floor(rng() * (i + 1))
    ;[deck[i], deck[j]] = [deck[j], deck[i]]
  }
  return Array.from({ length: entry.count }, (_, n) => deck[n])
}
```

### Task 2: `PoseState` additions and the swimming builder

**Files:**
- Create: `src/birds/detail/fingerprint.test.ts`, `src/birds/detail/swim.test.ts`
- Modify: `src/birds/model.ts`, `src/birds/detail/spec.ts`, `src/birds/detail/legs.ts`, `src/birds/detail/mesher.ts`,
  `src/birds/detail/build.ts`, `src/birds/detail/measure.ts`, `src/birds/detail/fixture.ts`,
  `src/birds/detail/build.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `buildDetailedBird`, `reachDown`'s solvers `neckSwing`/`headPitchToGround` (unchanged), `TEST_SPEC`.
- Produces:

```ts
// src/birds/model.ts, PoseState:
paddle: number; paddlePhase: number; reachDepth: number; reachAngle: number   // defaults 0, 0, 0, Math.PI / 2

// detailSchema:
float: z.object({ waterline: z.number() }).optional()

// Measures gains (metres, at rest, y = 0 is the water):
backAbove: number; crownAbove: number; tailTipAbove: number; wingTipAbove: number; breastFrontAbove: number
draft: number; waterlineLength: number

export function tailTipNow(model: DetailedBirdModel): THREE.Vector3        // measure.ts
Mesher.spanAtHeight(y: number, indexFirst: number, indexCount: number): [number, number] | null
export const SWIM_TIBIA = -Math.PI / 2, SWIM_TARSUS = -2.75                  // legs.ts
export function legLayout(detail: DetailSpec, profile: Profile, swimming = false): LegLayout   // LegLayout.swimming
export const TEST_SWIM_DETAIL: DetailSpec, TEST_SWIM_SPEC: SpeciesSpec      // fixture.ts, code 'testduck'
```

The pivot is defined as **the middle of the waterline**: the loft's side view is cut by the plane y = waterline, and
the pivot is on that plane midway between the cut's rearmost and frontmost points (where the water meets the stern
and the breast). The builder shifts the bird so the pivot is the group's origin; the root and body bones sit on it.

- [ ] **Step 1: The geometry fingerprint, first, against master**

The print covers every detailed species: the rest-pose mesh (positions, texture coordinates, skin weights, skin
indices, triangles) and the bone matrices and bill tip through twelve poses (rest, walking, flying, pecks at leans 0
to 1, the dowitcher's probe). Floats are rounded to a micrometre before hashing, so a real change shows and last-bit
noise doesn't. The poses carry no `tailTilt`: Task 4 turns its sign round, and no behaviour gives a shipped bird one.

Create `src/birds/detail/fingerprint.test.ts`:

```ts
import * as THREE from 'three'
import { SPECIES } from '../species'
import { buildDetailedBird, type DetailedBirdModel } from './build'
import { billTipNow } from './measure'
import { defaultPose, type PoseState } from '../model'

/**
 * FNV-1a over 32-bit integers. Every float is rounded to a micrometre (or a millionth of a texture) first, so the
 * print sees any real change to the geometry and none of the last-bit noise a different Math library might add.
 */
function hasher() {
  let h = 2166136261
  const int = (n: number) => {
    for (let k = 0; k < 4; k++) h = Math.imul(h ^ ((n >>> (k * 8)) & 0xff), 16777619)
  }
  return {
    int,
    float(v: number) { int(Math.round(v * 1e6)) },
    hex: () => (h >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, '0'),
  }
}

/** The rest-pose mesh: vertex positions, texture coordinates, bone indices and weights, and the triangles. */
function geometryPrint(m: DetailedBirdModel): string {
  const g = m.mesh.geometry
  const h = hasher()
  for (const name of ['position', 'uv', 'skinWeight']) for (const v of (g.getAttribute(name) as THREE.BufferAttribute).array) h.float(v)
  for (const v of (g.getAttribute('skinIndex') as THREE.BufferAttribute).array) h.int(v)
  for (const v of g.getIndex()!.array) h.int(v)
  return h.hex()
}

/**
 * Poses the game uses, one after another on the same bird as the game would: rest, walking, flying, pecks, probes. No
 * tailTilt: no behaviour gives a standing bird one, and the swimmers' round turns its sign round in the detailed
 * builder (Task 4), so that a positive tailTilt lifts the tail as PoseState says.
 */
type Patch = Partial<Omit<PoseState, 'lean'>> & { leanX?: number; leanZ?: number }
const POSES: Patch[] = [
  {},
  { gait: 1, strideDistance: 0.07 },
  { gait: 0.4, strideDistance: 0.13 },
  { wingSpread: 1, wingPhase: 0.3 },
  { neckExtend: 0, headBob: 0.1, leanZ: 0.2 },
  { neckExtend: 1, headBob: -0.05 },
  { reach: 1 },
  { reach: 0.3, leanX: 0.8 },
  { reach: 0.7, leanX: 0.8, leanZ: 0.1 },
  { reach: 1, leanX: 0.8 },
  { reach: 1, neckExtend: 1, leanX: 1 },
  { reach: 0.6, neckExtend: 0.9, leanX: 0.5 },
]

/** The bones every detailed bird has had since the builder began; a bone added later, with no skin of its own on these birds, is not in the print. */
const BONES = [
  'root', 'body', 'neck', 'neckMid', 'head', 'bill', 'tail', 'wingL', 'wingR', 'wingFoldL', 'wingFoldR',
  'legs', 'tibiaL', 'tarsusL', 'toesL', 'tibiaR', 'tarsusR', 'toesR',
]

/** Every bone's matrix and the bill tip, through POSES. */
function posePrint(m: DetailedBirdModel): string {
  const h = hasher()
  const bones = BONES.map(n => m.mesh.skeleton.bones.find(b => b.name === n)!)
  for (const patch of POSES) {
    const { leanX = 0, leanZ = 0, ...rest } = patch
    const p: PoseState = { ...defaultPose(), ...rest }
    p.lean.set(leanX, 0, leanZ)
    m.pose(p)
    m.group.updateMatrixWorld(true)
    for (const b of bones) for (const e of b.matrixWorld.elements) h.float(e)
    const tip = billTipNow(m)
    h.float(tip.x); h.float(tip.y); h.float(tip.z)
  }
  return h.hex()
}

// Prints taken on master at c588f75, before the swimmers round. A change to the builder that moves any shipped
// bird's mesh, its skinning, its texture layout, or any of its poses by a micrometre changes these.
const EXPECTED: Record<string, { geometry: string; poses: string }> = {
  killde: { geometry: 'f5d077d0', poses: '4d9550b0' },
  leasan: { geometry: '83eeff1f', poses: '0cd6b93e' },
  greyel: { geometry: '7671be05', poses: '88449834' },
  lesyel: { geometry: '923592bf', poses: 'e640b069' },
  pecsan: { geometry: 'fc0011fe', poses: 'bac5bab5' },
  lobdow: { geometry: '2951d8a8', poses: 'be6320da' },
}

describe('the shipped detailed birds build exactly as before', () => {
  const detailed = Object.values(SPECIES).filter(s => s.detail)
  it('covers every detailed species', () => {
    expect(detailed.map(s => s.code).sort()).toEqual(Object.keys(EXPECTED).sort())
  })
  for (const spec of detailed) {
    it(`${spec.code}: the same mesh and the same poses`, () => {
      const m = buildDetailedBird(spec)
      expect({ geometry: geometryPrint(m), poses: posePrint(m) }).toEqual(EXPECTED[spec.code])
    })
  }
})
```

Run: `npx vitest run src/birds/detail/fingerprint.test.ts`

Create `src/birds/detail/swim.test.ts`:

```ts
import * as THREE from 'three'
import { buildDetailedBird, type DetailedBirdModel } from './build'
import { measure, billTipNow, billAngleNow, tailTipNow } from './measure'
import { createProfile } from './profile'
import { worstFold } from './loft'
import { TEST_SPEC, TEST_SWIM_SPEC, TEST_SWIM_DETAIL } from './fixture'
import { parseSpeciesSpec } from '../spec'
import { defaultPose, type PoseState } from '../model'

const WATERLINE = TEST_SWIM_DETAIL.float!.waterline
const LEGS = ['legs', 'tibiaL', 'tarsusL', 'toesL', 'tibiaR', 'tarsusR', 'toesR']

const bone = (m: DetailedBirdModel, name: string) => m.mesh.skeleton.bones.find(b => b.name === name)!
const boneAt = (m: DetailedBirdModel, name: string) => { m.group.updateMatrixWorld(true); return bone(m, name).getWorldPosition(new THREE.Vector3()) }
const posed = (patch: Partial<Omit<PoseState, 'lean'>>, leanX = 0): PoseState => {
  const p = { ...defaultPose(), ...patch }
  p.lean.x = leanX
  return p
}

/** Every vertex whose heaviest bone is one of `names`, where the model's last pose() put it, group space. */
function skinned(m: DetailedBirdModel, names: string[]): THREE.Vector3[] {
  m.group.updateMatrixWorld(true)
  const want = new Set(names.map(n => m.mesh.skeleton.bones.indexOf(bone(m, n))))
  const index = m.mesh.geometry.getAttribute('skinIndex') as THREE.BufferAttribute
  const weight = m.mesh.geometry.getAttribute('skinWeight') as THREE.BufferAttribute
  const out: THREE.Vector3[] = []
  for (let i = 0; i < index.count; i++) {
    let best = 0, bw = -1
    for (let k = 0; k < 4; k++) { const w = weight.getComponent(i, k); if (w > bw) { bw = w; best = index.getComponent(i, k) } }
    if (want.has(best)) out.push(m.mesh.getVertexPosition(i, new THREE.Vector3()))
  }
  return out
}

describe('the swimming test duck', () => {
  it('lofts without a crease', () => {
    expect(worstFold(createProfile(TEST_SWIM_DETAIL.profile), TEST_SWIM_DETAIL.rig)).toBe(0)
  })
})

describe('buildDetailedBird, swimming', () => {
  it('floats with the traced waterline on y = 0', () => {
    const m = buildDetailedBird(TEST_SWIM_SPEC)
    const profile = createProfile(TEST_SWIM_DETAIL.profile)
    m.anatomy.spine.forEach((p, k) => expect(p.y - profile.sampleAt(k / 100).point.y).toBeCloseTo(-WATERLINE, 9))
  })
  it('pivots on the water under the middle of the body: midway between where the water meets the stern and the breast', () => {
    const m = buildDetailedBird(TEST_SWIM_SPEC)
    const { back, front } = m.anatomy.waterline
    expect(front - back).toBeGreaterThan(0.2)
    expect(back + front).toBeCloseTo(0, 9)
    expect(boneAt(m, 'body').length()).toBeLessThan(1e-12)
    // where the water meets the body is where the traced belly line crosses the waterline (the loft's midline section
    // is that line), within the loft's sampling
    const profile = createProfile(TEST_SWIM_DETAIL.profile)
    const belly = Array.from({ length: 1001 }, (_, k) => {
      const s = profile.sampleAt(k / 1000)
      return s.point.clone().addScaledVector(s.normal, -s.down)
    })
    const crossings: number[] = []
    for (let k = 1; k < belly.length; k++) {
      const a = belly[k - 1]!, b = belly[k]!
      if ((a.y < WATERLINE) !== (b.y < WATERLINE)) crossings.push(a.x + ((b.x - a.x) * (WATERLINE - a.y)) / (b.y - a.y))
    }
    expect(crossings).toHaveLength(2)
    const middle = (crossings[0]! + crossings[1]!) / 2
    expect(Math.abs(back - (crossings[0]! - middle))).toBeLessThan(0.002)
    expect(Math.abs(front - (crossings[1]! - middle))).toBeLessThan(0.002)
  })
  it('tips up about the pivot: it stays put as lean.x goes to 1.5, and the stern rises out of the water', () => {
    const m = buildDetailedBird(TEST_SWIM_SPEC)
    const rest = skinned(m, ['body'])
    const tailRest = tailTipNow(m).y
    m.pose(posed({ tailTilt: 0.3 }, 1.5))
    const tipped = skinned(m, ['body'])
    expect(rest.length).toBeGreaterThan(500)
    rest.forEach((p, i) => expect(tipped[i]!.length()).toBeCloseTo(p.length(), 9))      // turned rigidly about the origin
    expect(boneAt(m, 'body').length()).toBeLessThan(1e-12)
    // the stern, where the water met the body at the back, swings up nearly its whole distance from the pivot
    const stern = new THREE.Vector3(m.anatomy.waterline.back, 0, 0).applyMatrix4(bone(m, 'body').matrixWorld)
    expect(stern.y).toBeGreaterThan(-0.99 * m.anatomy.waterline.back)
    expect(tailTipNow(m).y).toBeGreaterThan(tailRest + 0.1)
    expect(billTipNow(m).y).toBeLessThan(-0.1)                                           // the head is under
  })
  it('hides the legs by bone scale at paddle 0, inside the body and under the water', () => {
    const m = buildDetailedBird(TEST_SWIM_SPEC)
    for (const lean of [0, 0.3]) {
      m.pose(posed({}, lean))
      expect(bone(m, 'legs').scale.x).toBeLessThan(0.01)
      const legs = skinned(m, LEGS)
      expect(legs.length).toBeGreaterThan(100)
      for (const v of legs) expect(v.y).toBeLessThan(0)
    }
  })
  it('shows them at paddle 1, folded back under the body', () => {
    const m = buildDetailedBird(TEST_SWIM_SPEC)
    m.pose(posed({ paddle: 1 }))
    expect(bone(m, 'legs').scale.x).toBe(1)
    expect(boneAt(m, 'tarsusR').distanceTo(boneAt(m, 'toesR'))).toBeCloseTo(TEST_SWIM_DETAIL.measurements.tarsus, 6)
    expect(boneAt(m, 'toesR').x).toBeLessThan(boneAt(m, 'tibiaR').x - 0.02)
    expect(boneAt(m, 'toesR').y).toBeLessThan(m.anatomy.bodyLowY)
  })
  it('paddles alternately with paddlePhase, and in a tip-up the feet paddle above the water', () => {
    const m = buildDetailedBird(TEST_SWIM_SPEC)
    const feet = (phase: number, lean = 0) => {
      m.pose(posed({ paddle: 1, paddlePhase: phase }, lean))
      return [boneAt(m, 'toesR'), boneAt(m, 'toesL')] as const
    }
    const [r0, l0] = feet(0)
    for (const phase of [0.25, 0.75]) {
      const [r, l] = feet(phase)
      const dr = r.x - r0.x, dl = l.x - l0.x
      expect(Math.abs(dr), `phase ${phase}`).toBeGreaterThan(0.01)
      expect(dr * dl, `phase ${phase}`).toBeLessThan(0)
    }
    // the left foot at one phase is where the right one was half a stroke earlier, mirrored
    const right = feet(0.25)[0], left = feet(0.75)[1]
    expect(left.x).toBeCloseTo(right.x, 9); expect(left.y).toBeCloseTo(right.y, 9); expect(left.z).toBeCloseTo(-right.z, 9)
    for (const phase of [0, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75]) for (const f of feet(phase, 1.5)) expect(f.y, `phase ${phase}`).toBeGreaterThan(0)
  })
  it('keeps out-of-range swimming values to their ranges', () => {
    const m = buildDetailedBird(TEST_SWIM_SPEC)
    const bones = () => m.mesh.skeleton.bones.map(b => [...b.position.toArray(), ...b.quaternion.toArray(), ...b.scale.toArray()])
    m.pose(posed({ paddle: 3, paddlePhase: 0.2, reach: 1, reachDepth: -1, reachAngle: 4 }))
    const wild = bones()
    m.pose(posed({ paddle: 1, paddlePhase: 0.2, reach: 1, reachDepth: 0, reachAngle: Math.PI / 2 }))
    const tame = bones()
    wild.forEach((row, i) => row.forEach((v, k) => expect(v).toBeCloseTo(tame[i]![k]!, 12)))
  })
})

describe('reachDepth and reachAngle', () => {
  it('aim a reach reachDepth below the ground or the water: the bill tip goes down by its value', () => {
    const m = buildDetailedBird(TEST_SPEC)
    for (const d of [0.01, 0.02]) {
      m.pose(posed({ reach: 1, reachDepth: d }, 0.8))
      expect(billTipNow(m).y, `standing, ${d}`).toBeCloseTo(-d, 4)
    }
    // a head-dip, the body tipped forward a little: the neck swings the head down under the water until the bill
    // tip is that deep
    const duck = buildDetailedBird(TEST_SWIM_SPEC)
    for (const d of [0, 0.05, 0.1]) {
      duck.pose(posed({ reach: 1, neckExtend: 1, reachDepth: d }, 0.2))
      expect(billTipNow(duck).y, `swimming, ${d}`).toBeCloseTo(-d, 4)
    }
  })
  it('adds reachDepth to the species probeDepth', () => {
    const deep = buildDetailedBird(parseSpeciesSpec({ ...TEST_SPEC, detail: { ...TEST_SPEC.detail!, probeDepth: 0.01 } }))
    deep.pose(posed({ reach: 1, reachDepth: 0.01 }, 0.8))
    expect(billTipNow(deep).y).toBeCloseTo(-0.02, 4)
  })
  it('hold the bill no steeper than reachAngle below level, the neck swinging further to make up the depth', () => {
    const duck = buildDetailedBird(TEST_SWIM_SPEC)
    // a shoveler's skim: the bill about 25 degrees down and just under the water
    for (const reach of [0.3, 0.6, 0.8, 1]) {
      duck.pose(posed({ reach, neckExtend: 1, reachDepth: 0.005, reachAngle: 0.45 }))
      expect(billAngleNow(duck), `reach ${reach}`).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(-0.45 - 1e-9)
    }
    expect(billTipNow(duck).y).toBeCloseTo(-0.005, 4)
    expect(billAngleNow(duck)).toBeCloseTo(-0.45, 6)
    // without the cap the same reach points the bill much more steeply
    duck.pose(posed({ reach: 1, neckExtend: 1, reachDepth: 0.005 }))
    expect(billAngleNow(duck)).toBeLessThan(-0.9)
  })
})

describe('measure, floating', () => {
  const m = buildDetailedBird(TEST_SWIM_SPEC)
  const r = measure(m)
  const stations = TEST_SWIM_DETAIL.profile
  const upTo = (label: string) => stations.slice(0, stations.findIndex(s => s.label === label) + 1)
  it('takes the back, crown, and draft from the traced outline, within the loft between stations', () => {
    expect(Math.abs(r.backAbove - (Math.max(...upTo('neckBase').map(s => s.y + s.up)) - WATERLINE))).toBeLessThan(0.002)
    expect(Math.abs(r.crownAbove - (Math.max(...stations.slice(9).map(s => s.y + s.up)) - WATERLINE))).toBeLessThan(0.002)
    expect(Math.abs(r.draft - (Math.max(...stations.map(s => s.down - s.y)) + WATERLINE))).toBeLessThan(0.002)
  })
  it('reads the tail tip, the wingtip, and the breast front off the built bird', () => {
    expect(r.tailTipAbove).toBe(m.anatomy.tailTip.y)
    expect(r.wingTipAbove).toBe(m.anatomy.wingTip.y)
    expect(r.breastFrontAbove).toBe(m.anatomy.breastFront.y)
    // the breast front is ahead of the neck base, low on the breast and above the water
    expect(m.anatomy.breastFront.x).toBeGreaterThan(m.anatomy.stations.find(s => s.label === 'neckBase')!.position.x)
    expect(r.breastFrontAbove).toBeGreaterThan(0)
    expect(r.breastFrontAbove).toBeLessThan(r.backAbove / 2)
  })
  it('measures the waterline length where the water meets the body, and none for a bird standing on the ground', () => {
    expect(r.waterlineLength).toBeCloseTo(m.anatomy.waterline.front - m.anatomy.waterline.back, 12)
    expect(r.waterlineLength).toBeGreaterThan(0.2)
    expect(measure(buildDetailedBird(TEST_SPEC)).waterlineLength).toBe(0)
  })
})
```

- [ ] **Step 6: A swimmer's legs, folded back**

The tibia hangs straight down from the hip, so the bare tibia below the belly is exactly `measurements.tibia` (the
species test holds it within 15%); the tarsus and toes trail back 22.4° below level. A standing bird's legs are
built exactly as before.

- [ ] **Step 7: Where a horizontal plane cuts the mesh**

In `src/birds/detail/mesher.ts`, replace:

```ts
    return target.set(this.pos[i * 3]!, this.pos[i * 3 + 1]!, this.pos[i * 3 + 2]!)
  }
```

with:

```ts
    return target.set(this.pos[i * 3]!, this.pos[i * 3 + 1]!, this.pos[i * 3 + 2]!)
  }

  /**
   * Where the horizontal plane at height `y` cuts the triangles from index `indexFirst` for `indexCount` indices: its
   * x extent, the side view's length at that height. Null if the plane misses them.
   */
  spanAtHeight(y: number, indexFirst: number, indexCount: number): [number, number] | null {
    let lo = Infinity, hi = -Infinity
    for (let i = indexFirst; i < indexFirst + indexCount; i += 3) {
      for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) {
        const a = this.index[i + k]!, b = this.index[i + ((k + 1) % 3)]!
        const ya = this.pos[a * 3 + 1]! - y, yb = this.pos[b * 3 + 1]! - y
        if (ya < 0 === yb < 0) continue
        const xa = this.pos[a * 3]!, xb = this.pos[b * 3]!
        const x = xa + ((xb - xa) * ya) / (ya - yb)
        lo = Math.min(lo, x); hi = Math.max(hi, x)
      }
    }
    return lo <= hi ? [lo, hi] : null
  }
```

- [ ] **Step 8: The swimming builder**

The swimmer's offset puts the waterline on y = 0 and the pivot on the origin; root and body sit on the pivot; the
legs' bone sits inside the body, a quarter of the way up from the belly to the hip, so hidden legs gather under the
water. `reachDown` takes its depth and floor per call; with `reachDepth` 0 and `reachAngle` π/2 it computes the same
doubles as before (`probeDepth + 0`, `-(π/2) + tipOffAxis`), so every existing peck is bit-identical (the fingerprint
holds). While `paddle` is 0 the legs' bone is scaled to 1e-4; above 0 it shows and follows the body's lean, and the
tibia and tarsus swing ±`paddle` × 0.5 rad, the left leg opposite the right.

```ts
  breastFront: THREE.Vector3
  /** where the plane y = 0 (a swimmer's waterline) cuts the body in the side view: its rear and front x; 0, 0 if it misses */
  waterline: { back: number; front: number }
```

```ts
  const legsRest = bones.legs.position.clone()

  /** A swimmer's legs: hidden at paddle 0; shown, folded back and going wherever the body goes, paddling alternately. */
  function paddleLegs(st: PoseState) {
    const paddle = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(st.paddle, 0, 1)
    bones.legs.scale.setScalar(paddle > 0 ? 1 : HIDDEN)
    // the legs hang from root, so a standing bird's feet stay planted while it leans; a swimmer's follow its body
    bones.legs.quaternion.copy(bones.body.quaternion)
    bones.legs.position.copy(legsRest).sub(bones.body.position).applyQuaternion(bones.body.quaternion).add(bones.body.position)
    const stroke = paddle * PADDLE_SWING * Math.sin(st.paddlePhase * Math.PI * 2)
    bones.tibiaR.rotation.z = stroke; bones.tarsusR.rotation.z = stroke
    bones.tibiaL.rotation.z = -stroke; bones.tarsusL.rotation.z = -stroke
  }

  function pose(st: PoseState) {
    // lean.x pitches the nose down, lean.z rolls; the body pivots over the hips (a swimmer's on the water, mid-body)
    bones.body.rotation.set(st.lean.z, 0, -st.lean.x)
```

```ts
/**
 * Where the tail tip is now, in group space (on the water, or the ground), after the model's last pose(): the stern of
 * a tipped-up duck. For a bird with no tail, the rearmost point of its body.
 */
export function tailTipNow(model: DetailedBirdModel): THREE.Vector3 {
  model.group.updateMatrixWorld(true)
  const toGroup = model.group.matrixWorld.clone().invert()
  // the tail bone sits at the tail root and carries no rotation at rest
  return model.anatomy.tailTip.clone().sub(model.anatomy.tailRoot).applyMatrix4(model.parts.tail.matrixWorld).applyMatrix4(toGroup)
}

/** Where the bill points now, radians from level in the side view (negative is down), after the model's last pose(). */
```

### Task 3: Bill profile, hook, and frontal shield

**Files:**
- Create: `src/birds/detail/bill.test.ts`
- Modify: `src/birds/detail/spec.ts`, `src/birds/detail/bill.ts`, `src/birds/detail/build.ts`,
  `src/birds/detail/measure.ts`, `src/birds/paint/atlas.ts`, `src/birds/paint/painter.ts`,
  `src/birds/detail/fingerprint.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `TEST_SWIM_SPEC`/`TEST_SWIM_DETAIL` (Task 2), `paintAtlas`, `REGIONS`.
- Produces:

```ts
// detailSchema.bill:
taper?: number            // now optional: a bill needs a taper or a profile
profile?: [number, number, number][]    // [s, width, depth], s strictly increasing 0..1
hook?: number             // metres
shield?: { length: number; width: number; thickness: number }   // metres
// Part gains 'shield'; REGIONS.shield = { x: 448, y: 384, w: 64, h: 32 }
export function billScale(bill: DetailSpec['bill'], sigma: number): { width: number; depth: number }   // bill.ts
export function buildShield(m: Mesher, detail: DetailSpec, profile: Profile): ShieldResult | null
// Anatomy.shield: { base: THREE.Vector3; top: THREE.Vector3 } | null; closed gains 'shield'
// Measures gains: billWidestAt: number; billWidestRatio: number
export function billWidthAt(model: DetailedBirdModel, s: number): number    // measure.ts, metres
export function billDepthAt(model: DetailedBirdModel, s: number): number
```

With a profile or a hook, the bill gets a ring at every profile point (and at 0.9, 0.925, 0.95, 0.975 for a hook), so
the mesh follows the profile exactly at its points; a plain bill keeps its 16 even rings. The shield is a closed
lens: its upper face stands up to `thickness` off the head's top outline, its rim tucks 0.3 mm under the skin, and it
rides the head bone. The shield's parameters come from `docs/research/2026-09-25-american-coot.md`, sections 5.2 and
9: 9 mm up the forehead, 7 mm wide, 2.2 mm thick in late September; the callus is paint on the shield's top.

```ts
  }
```

```ts
/** Every texel of a species' painted atlas, for one plumage. */
function atlasPrint(spec: (typeof SPECIES)[string], variant?: string): string {
  const d = spec.detail!
  const px = paintAtlas({ code: spec.code, detail: d, layers: layersFor(d, variant), profile: createProfile(d.profile) })
  const h = hasher()
  for (let i = 0; i < px.length; i += 4) h.int(px[i]! | (px[i + 1]! << 8) | (px[i + 2]! << 16))
  return h.hex()
}

// Painted on master at c588f75. New atlas regions go in free space, so every shipped bird's atlas stays texel for texel
// what it was.
const EXPECTED_ATLAS: Record<string, string> = {
  killde: '3e3455e3', leasan: 'e132a9b2', greyel: '27f746a6', lesyel: '4b6f7821', pecsan: '07f03e17', lobdow: '57473eeb',
}

describe('the shipped detailed birds paint exactly as before', () => {
  const atlases = Object.values(SPECIES).filter(s => s.detail).flatMap(s => [undefined, ...Object.keys(s.variants)].map(v => [s, v] as const))
  it('covers every detailed species and plumage', () => {
    expect(atlases.map(([s, v]) => (v ? `${s.code}:${v}` : s.code)).sort()).toEqual(Object.keys(EXPECTED_ATLAS).sort())
  })
  for (const [spec, variant] of atlases) {
    const key = variant ? `${spec.code}:${variant}` : spec.code
    it(`${key}: the same atlas`, () => expect(atlasPrint(spec, variant)).toBe(EXPECTED_ATLAS[key]))
  }
})
```

```ts
import * as THREE from 'three'
import { buildDetailedBird, type DetailedBirdModel } from './build'
import { buildBill, billCenter } from './bill'
import { measure, billWidthAt, billDepthAt } from './measure'
import { createProfile } from './profile'
import { Mesher, signedVolume } from './mesher'
import { detailSchema, type DetailSpec } from './spec'
import { BONE_INDEX } from './rig'
import { TEST_SPEC, TEST_DETAIL, TEST_SWIM_SPEC, TEST_SWIM_DETAIL } from './fixture'
import { parseSpeciesSpec } from '../spec'
import { REGIONS, ATLAS_SIZE } from '../paint/atlas'
import { paintAtlas, partFrames, regionStats } from '../paint/painter'

/**
 * The Northern Shoveler's bill, docs/research/2026-09-25-northern-shoveler.md section 2.4: width (top view) and depth
 * (side view) in mm against s, a fraction of the culmen from the base, as the research recommends them.
 */
const SHOVELER_WIDTH: [number, number][] = [
  [0, 16.5], [0.1, 18.0], [0.18, 19.5], [0.3, 21.2], [0.4, 23.3], [0.5, 25.4], [0.6, 27.4], [0.7, 28.9], [0.75, 29.5],
  [0.8, 28.9], [0.85, 26.8], [0.9, 23.6], [0.93, 20.7], [0.975, 10.6], [1, 0],
]
const SHOVELER_DEPTH: [number, number][] = [
  [0, 21], [0.13, 20.5], [0.225, 19.3], [0.325, 17.5], [0.43, 16.3], [0.535, 15.6], [0.625, 15.2], [0.7, 13.2], [0.8, 10],
  [0.88, 7], [0.955, 5], [1, 2.5],
]
/** the two tables at every point of either, as multiples of the base (16.5 mm wide, 21 mm deep), linear between */
const SHOVELER_PROFILE: [number, number, number][] = [
  [0, 1, 1], [0.1, 1.0909, 0.9817], [0.13, 1.125, 0.9762], [0.18, 1.1818, 0.9461], [0.225, 1.2205, 0.919],
  [0.3, 1.2848, 0.8548], [0.325, 1.3167, 0.8333], [0.4, 1.4121, 0.7925], [0.43, 1.4503, 0.7762], [0.5, 1.5394, 0.754],
  [0.535, 1.5818, 0.7429], [0.6, 1.6606, 0.7291], [0.625, 1.6833, 0.7238], [0.7, 1.7515, 0.6286], [0.75, 1.7879, 0.5524],
  [0.8, 1.7515, 0.4762], [0.85, 1.6242, 0.3869], [0.88, 1.5079, 0.3333], [0.9, 1.4303, 0.3079], [0.93, 1.2545, 0.2698],
  [0.955, 0.9145, 0.2381], [0.975, 0.6424, 0.1852], [1, 0, 0.119],
]
/**
 * The Mallard's bill, docs/research/2026-09-25-mallard.md section 9: width as a fraction of the widest (22.4 mm; the
 * base is 0.93 of it), here over the base's, and depth as a fraction of the base's, at every point of either table.
 */
const MALLARD_PROFILE: [number, number, number][] = [
  [0, 1, 1], [0.1, 0.9624, 1], [0.2, 0.9247, 1], [0.3, 0.957, 0.92], [0.4, 1.0215, 0.81], [0.5, 1.0215, 0.7],
  [0.58, 1.0753, 0.636], [0.6, 1.0753, 0.62], [0.7, 1.0753, 0.54], [0.74, 1.0753, 0.492], [0.8, 1.0323, 0.42],
  [0.84, 0.9785, 0.372], [0.85, 0.9534, 0.36], [0.87, 0.9032, 0.316], [0.9, 0.8172, 0.25], [0.93, 0.6774, 0.25],
  [0.95, 0.6129, 0.25], [0.97, 0.4839, 0.25], [0.98, 0.4086, 0.25], [1, 0, 0.25],
]

/** The test duck with another bill. */
function duckWith(bill: Partial<DetailSpec['bill']>, measurements: Partial<DetailSpec['measurements']> = {}): DetailedBirdModel {
  const detail = { ...TEST_SWIM_DETAIL, bill: { ...TEST_SWIM_DETAIL.bill, ...bill }, measurements: { ...TEST_SWIM_DETAIL.measurements, ...measurements } }
  return buildDetailedBird(parseSpeciesSpec({ ...TEST_SWIM_SPEC, detail }))
}
const shoveler = () => duckWith({ taper: undefined, profile: SHOVELER_PROFILE }, { culmen: 0.064, billWidth: 0.0165, billDepth: 0.021 })
const mallard = () => duckWith({ taper: undefined, profile: MALLARD_PROFILE }, { culmen: 0.053, billWidth: 0.0208, billDepth: 0.0183 })

describe('bill profile', () => {
  it("follows the Shoveler's research table within 0.5 mm at each point", () => {
    const m = shoveler()
    for (const [s, mm] of SHOVELER_WIDTH) expect(Math.abs(billWidthAt(m, s) * 1000 - mm), `width at ${s}`).toBeLessThan(0.5)
    // the tip itself closes to a point, so the nail's 2.5 mm of depth at s = 1 is the one point left out
    for (const [s, mm] of SHOVELER_DEPTH.filter(([s]) => s < 1)) expect(Math.abs(billDepthAt(m, s) * 1000 - mm), `depth at ${s}`).toBeLessThan(0.5)
  })
  it("widens the Shoveler's to its spoon: widest at 0.65-0.80 of the bill, 1.6-2.0 times the base (research section 10)", () => {
    const r = measure(shoveler())
    expect(r.billWidestAt).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0.65); expect(r.billWidestAt).toBeLessThanOrEqual(0.8)
    expect(r.billWidestRatio).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1.6); expect(r.billWidestRatio).toBeLessThanOrEqual(2)
  })
  it("gives the Mallard's its widest point at 58-74% of its length, 1.02-1.10 times the base (research sections 9, 10)", () => {
    const r = measure(mallard())
    expect(r.billWidestAt).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0.58); expect(r.billWidestAt).toBeLessThanOrEqual(0.74)
    expect(r.billWidestRatio).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1.02); expect(r.billWidestRatio).toBeLessThanOrEqual(1.1)
  })
  it('measures a tapered bill too: as wide and deep as its measurements at the base, narrowing to the tip', () => {
    const m = buildDetailedBird(TEST_SPEC)
    expect(billWidthAt(m, 0) / TEST_DETAIL.measurements.billWidth).toBeCloseTo(1, 2)
    expect(billDepthAt(m, 0) / TEST_DETAIL.measurements.billDepth).toBeCloseTo(1, 2)
    expect(billWidthAt(m, 0.5)).toBeLessThan(billWidthAt(m, 0.2))
    expect(measure(m).billWidestAt).toBeLessThan(0.05)
  })
  it("sizes the bill's paint frame by the profile: the spoon is wider than the base, a tapered bill narrower", () => {
    const spoon = { ...TEST_SWIM_DETAIL, bill: { curve: 0, profile: SHOVELER_PROFILE } }
    const f = partFrames(spoon, createProfile(spoon.profile))
    expect(f.bill.widthMm(0.75)).toBeGreaterThan(1.2 * f.bill.widthMm(0))
    const g = partFrames(TEST_DETAIL, createProfile(TEST_DETAIL.profile))
    expect(g.bill.widthMm(0.75)).toBeLessThan(0.6 * g.bill.widthMm(0))
  })
  it('needs a taper or a profile, and profile points that run from the base to the tip', () => {
    expect(() => detailSchema.parse({ ...TEST_DETAIL, bill: { curve: 0 } })).toThrow(/taper or a profile/)
    expect(() => detailSchema.parse({ ...TEST_DETAIL, bill: { curve: 0, profile: [[0.5, 1, 1], [0.2, 1, 1]] } })).toThrow(/base to tip/)
  })
})

describe('bill hook', () => {
  const profile = createProfile(TEST_DETAIL.profile)
  const hooked = { ...TEST_DETAIL, bill: { ...TEST_DETAIL.bill, hook: 0.0015 } }
  it('drops the tip by its value, and only over the last tenth of the bill', () => {
    const plain = buildBill(new Mesher(), TEST_DETAIL, profile), hook = buildBill(new Mesher(), hooked, profile)
    expect(plain.tip.clone().sub(hook.tip).dot(plain.up)).toBeCloseTo(0.0015, 9)
    for (const s of [0, 0.5, 0.9]) expect(billCenter(hooked, profile, s).p.distanceTo(billCenter(TEST_DETAIL, profile, s).p), `${s}`).toBeLessThan(1e-12)
    // a grebe's hooked tip reads as a lower tip on the built bird
    const m = buildDetailedBird(parseSpeciesSpec({ ...TEST_SPEC, detail: hooked }))
    expect(measure(m).billTipRise - measure(buildDetailedBird(TEST_SPEC)).billTipRise).toBeCloseTo(-0.0015, 6)
  })
  it('keeps the hooked bill closed and outward-facing', () => {
    const m = new Mesher()
    const b = buildBill(m, hooked, profile)
    expect(signedVolume(m.geometry(), b.indexFirst, b.indexCount)).toBeGreaterThan(0)
  })
})

describe('frontal shield', () => {
  // the American Coot's in late September: 9 mm up the forehead, 7 mm wide, 2.2 mm thick
  // (docs/research/2026-09-25-american-coot.md, sections 5.2 and 9)
  const COOT_SHIELD = { length: 0.009, width: 0.007, thickness: 0.0022 }
  const coot = () => duckWith({ shield: COOT_SHIELD })
  /** how many vertices map into the shield's atlas region */
  const inShield = (m: DetailedBirdModel) => {
    const uv = m.mesh.geometry.getAttribute('uv') as THREE.BufferAttribute
    const r = REGIONS.shield
    let n = 0
    for (let i = 0; i < uv.count; i++) {
      const x = uv.getX(i) * ATLAS_SIZE, y = uv.getY(i) * ATLAS_SIZE
      if (x > r.x && x < r.x + r.w && y > r.y && y < r.y + r.h) n++
    }
    return n
  }
  it('is built only when the bill has one', () => {
    const plain = buildDetailedBird(TEST_SWIM_SPEC), shielded = coot()
    expect(inShield(plain)).toBe(0)
    expect(plain.anatomy.shield).toBeNull()
    expect(plain.anatomy.closed.map(c => c.name)).toEqual(['loft', 'bill'])
    expect(inShield(shielded)).toBeGreaterThan(50)
    expect(shielded.anatomy.closed.map(c => c.name)).toEqual(['loft', 'bill', 'shield'])
  })
  it('is closed and faces outward, and moves with the head', () => {
    const m = coot()
    const c = m.anatomy.closed.find(x => x.name === 'shield')!
    expect(signedVolume(m.mesh.geometry, c.indexFirst, c.indexCount)).toBeGreaterThan(0)
    const uv = m.mesh.geometry.getAttribute('uv') as THREE.BufferAttribute
    const bones = m.mesh.geometry.getAttribute('skinIndex') as THREE.BufferAttribute
    const r = REGIONS.shield
    for (let i = 0; i < uv.count; i++) {
      const x = uv.getX(i) * ATLAS_SIZE, y = uv.getY(i) * ATLAS_SIZE
      if (x > r.x && x < r.x + r.w && y > r.y && y < r.y + r.h) expect(bones.getX(i)).toBe(BONE_INDEX.head)
    }
  })
  it('runs from the top of the bill base up the forehead for its length', () => {
    const m = coot()
    const { base, top } = m.anatomy.shield!
    const a = m.anatomy
    const billTop = a.billBase.clone().addScaledVector(a.billUp, TEST_SWIM_DETAIL.profile.at(-1)!.up)
    expect(base.distanceTo(billTop)).toBeLessThan(0.001)
    expect(top.y).toBeGreaterThan(base.y)
    expect(top.x).toBeLessThan(base.x)
    // measured along the curving forehead, so the straight line from end to end is a little shorter
    expect(base.distanceTo(top)).toBeGreaterThan(0.8 * COOT_SHIELD.length)
    expect(base.distanceTo(top)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(COOT_SHIELD.length + 1e-9)
  })
  it('stands off the head by its thickness at the middle', () => {
    const m = coot()
    const c = m.anatomy.closed.find(x => x.name === 'shield')!
    const pos = m.mesh.geometry.getAttribute('position') as THREE.BufferAttribute
    const idx = m.mesh.geometry.getIndex()!
    const v = new THREE.Vector3()
    let far = 0
    const { base, top } = m.anatomy.shield!
    // the plate's rise off the line from its base to its top, square to that line in the side view
    const along = top.clone().sub(base).normalize(), out = new THREE.Vector3(along.y, -along.x, 0)
    if (out.dot(new THREE.Vector3(1, 1, 0)) < 0) out.negate()
    for (let i = c.indexFirst; i < c.indexFirst + c.indexCount; i++) far = Math.max(far, v.fromBufferAttribute(pos, idx.getX(i)).sub(base).dot(out))
    expect(far).toBeGreaterThan(COOT_SHIELD.thickness * 0.8)
  })
  it('paints in its own atlas region, and nothing else', () => {
    const px = paintAtlas({ code: 'testduck', detail: TEST_SWIM_DETAIL, layers: [{ kind: 'fill', where: { part: 'shield' }, color: '#ffffff' }], profile: createProfile(TEST_SWIM_DETAIL.profile) })
    expect(regionStats(px, 'shield', [0, 1], [0, 1]).mean).toBeCloseTo(1, 2)
    expect(regionStats(px, 'bill', [0, 1], [0, 1]).mean).toBeCloseTo(0.5, 1)
    expect(regionStats(px, 'eye', [0, 1], [0, 1]).mean).toBeLessThan(0.5)
  })
})
```

In `src/birds/detail/bill.ts`, replace:

```ts
/**

 * spine's final tangent, lifted over the distal half by `curve` x length at the tip.

```

with:

```ts
/**
 * The bill's width and depth at sigma (0 base, 1 tip) as multiples of measurements.billWidth and billDepth: its
 * profile, linear between points, or 1 − 0.85 σ^taper. The root inside the head (sigma < 0) keeps the base's.
 */
export function billScale(bill: DetailSpec['bill'], sigma: number): { width: number; depth: number } {
  const s = Math.max(0, sigma)
  const p = bill.profile
  if (!p) {
    const f = 1 - 0.85 * s ** bill.taper!
    return { width: f, depth: f }
  }
  if (s <= p[0]![0]) return { width: p[0]![1], depth: p[0]![2] }
  for (let i = 1; i < p.length; i++) {
    const [s1, w1, d1] = p[i]!, [s0, w0, d0] = p[i - 1]!
    if (s <= s1) { const k = (s - s0) / (s1 - s0); return { width: w0 + (w1 - w0) * k, depth: d0 + (d1 - d0) * k } }
  }
  const last = p[p.length - 1]!
  return { width: last[1], depth: last[2] }
}

/**
 * The bill's centreline in the side view for sigma in [-ROOT, 1] (0 = base, 1 = tip): straight along the
 * spine's final tangent, lifted over the distal half by `curve` x length at the tip, and dropped over the last tenth
 * by `hook`.
 */
```

```ts
  const dq = sigma > 0.5 ? (2 * q) / 0.5 : 0      // d(q^2)/dsigma
  const tangent = end.tangent.clone().multiplyScalar(L).addScaledVector(end.normal, k * dq).normalize()
```

with:

```ts
  const q = Math.max(0, (sigma - 0.5) / 0.5)
  const dq = sigma > 0.5 ? (2 * q) / 0.5 : 0      // d(q^2)/dsigma
  let lift = k * q * q, slope = k * dq
  const hook = detail.bill.hook
  if (hook) {
    const h = Math.max(0, (sigma - (1 - HOOK)) / HOOK)
    lift -= hook * h * h
    slope -= sigma > 1 - HOOK ? hook * ((2 * h) / HOOK) : 0
  }
  const p = end.point.clone().addScaledVector(end.tangent, sigma * L).addScaledVector(end.normal, lift)
  const tangent = end.tangent.clone().multiplyScalar(L).addScaledVector(end.normal, slope).normalize()
  return { p, tangent }
}

/**
 * Where the bill's rings sit, sigma from the root inside the head toward the tip (the tip itself is a point): evenly,
 * plus one at every profile point so the mesh follows the profile exactly, and closer over a hook's bend.
 */
function ringSigmas(bill: DetailSpec['bill']): number[] {
  const even = Array.from({ length: RINGS }, (_, i) => -ROOT + ((1 + ROOT) * i) / RINGS)   // the last stops short of the tip
  if (!bill.profile && !bill.hook) return even
  const extra = [...(bill.profile ?? []).map(([s]) => s), ...(bill.hook ? [0.9, 0.925, 0.95, 0.975] : [])]
  const all = [...even, ...extra.filter(s => s < 1)].sort((a, b) => a - b)
  return all.filter((s, i) => i === 0 || s - all[i - 1]! > 1e-6)
}
```

```ts
export interface ShieldResult {
  /** where the shield leaves the top of the bill's base, and its top, on the midline (profile frame) */
  base: THREE.Vector3
  top: THREE.Vector3
  indexFirst: number
  indexCount: number
}

/**
 * A frontal shield (`bill.shield`): a lens-shaped plate on the forehead, from the top of the bill's base up and back
 * along the head's top outline for `length`, `width` across, standing `thickness` off the head at its middle and
 * thinning to nothing at its rim, which sits just inside the skin so it blends into the head. A closed mesh on the
 * head bone, painted as 'shield': s runs up the forehead, t across it.
 */
export function buildShield(m: Mesher, detail: DetailSpec, profile: Profile): ShieldResult | null {
  const sh = detail.bill.shield
  if (!sh) return null
  const indexFirst = m.indexCount
  // the head's top outline, from the bill base back over the forehead, and its arclength
  const N = 400
  const top = Array.from({ length: N + 1 }, (_, k) => {
    const s = profile.sampleAt(1 - (k / N) * 0.5)
    return { s, p: s.point.clone().addScaledVector(s.normal, s.up) }
  })
  const along = [0]
  for (let k = 1; k <= N; k++) along.push(along[k - 1]! + top[k]!.p.distanceTo(top[k - 1]!.p))
  if (along[N]! < sh.length) throw new Error(`the shield (${sh.length} m) is longer than the forehead`)
  /** the head's section where the outline is `a` metres from the bill base */
  const sectionAt = (a: number) => {
    let k = 1
    while (along[k]! < a) k++
    const w = (a - along[k - 1]!) / (along[k]! - along[k - 1]!)
    return profile.sampleAt(1 - ((k - 1 + w) / N) * 0.5)
  }
  const point = (a: number, b: number, lift: number) => {
    const s = sectionAt(a * sh.length)
    // on the head's surface at z across the top: the superellipse section's upper side
    const z = Math.min(Math.abs(b) * sh.width / 2, s.halfWidth * 0.95)
    const n = s.up * (1 - (z / s.halfWidth) ** SQUARENESS) ** (1 / SQUARENESS)
    return new THREE.Vector3(s.point.x + s.normal.x * (n + lift), s.point.y + s.normal.y * (n + lift), Math.sign(b) * z)
  }
  const faces: number[][][] = []
  for (const face of ['top', 'under'] as const) {
    const grid: number[][] = []
    for (let i = 0; i < SHIELD_S; i++) {
      const a = i / (SHIELD_S - 1)
      const row: number[] = []
      for (let j = 0; j < SHIELD_T; j++) {
        const b = (j / (SHIELD_T - 1)) * 2 - 1
        // a lens: thickest in the middle, nothing at the rim; rounded across and along
        const bulge = face === 'top' ? sh.thickness * Math.sqrt(Math.max(0, 1 - b * b)) * Math.sin(Math.PI * a) ** 0.6 : 0
        row.push(m.vertex(point(a, b, bulge - SHIELD_INSET), 'shield', a, (b + 1) / 2, [['head', 1]]))
      }
      grid.push(row)
    }
    faces.push(grid)
  }
  const [upper, under] = faces as [number[][], number[][]]
  // a runs up the forehead (back along the head), b toward +z; the upper face turns outward, the under face in
  for (let i = 0; i < SHIELD_S - 1; i++) {
    for (let j = 0; j < SHIELD_T - 1; j++) {
      m.quad(upper[i]![j]!, upper[i + 1]![j]!, upper[i + 1]![j + 1]!, upper[i]![j + 1]!)
      m.quad(under[i]![j]!, under[i]![j + 1]!, under[i + 1]![j + 1]!, under[i + 1]![j]!)
    }
  }
  return {
    base: point(0, 0, 0), top: point(1, 0, 0),
    indexFirst, indexCount: m.indexCount - indexFirst,
```

```ts
/**
 * The built bill's cross-section at rest, at fraction `s` of its length from the base (0) to the tip (1): the points
 * where the plane square to the bill's base direction, that far along, cuts the bill's triangles.
 */
function billSection(model: DetailedBirdModel, s: number): THREE.Vector3[] {
  const a = model.anatomy
  const g = model.mesh.geometry
  const pos = g.getAttribute('position') as THREE.BufferAttribute
  const index = g.getIndex()!
  const bill = a.closed.find(c => c.name === 'bill')!
  const along = s * a.billTip.clone().sub(a.billBase).dot(a.billDir) + a.billBase.dot(a.billDir)
  const p = new THREE.Vector3(), q = new THREE.Vector3()
  const out: THREE.Vector3[] = []
  for (let i = bill.indexFirst; i < bill.indexFirst + bill.indexCount; i += 3) {
    for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) {
      p.fromBufferAttribute(pos, index.getX(i + k))
      q.fromBufferAttribute(pos, index.getX(i + ((k + 1) % 3)))
      const dp = p.dot(a.billDir) - along, dq = q.dot(a.billDir) - along
      if (dp < 0 === dq < 0) continue
      out.push(p.clone().lerp(q, dp / (dp - dq)))
    }
  }
  return out
}

/** The built bill's width (across, z) at rest, at fraction `s` of its length from the base (0) to the tip (1), metres. */
export function billWidthAt(model: DetailedBirdModel, s: number): number {
  const pts = billSection(model, s)
  return pts.length ? Math.max(...pts.map(v => v.z)) - Math.min(...pts.map(v => v.z)) : 0
}

/** The built bill's depth (top to bottom, square to the bill) at rest, at fraction `s` of its length from the base, metres. */
export function billDepthAt(model: DetailedBirdModel, s: number): number {
  const a = model.anatomy
  const h = billSection(model, s).map(v => v.clone().sub(a.billBase).dot(a.billUp))
  return h.length ? Math.max(...h) - Math.min(...h) : 0
}
```

### Task 4: Webbed and lobed feet, no tail, and parts only some versions have

**Files:**
- Create: `src/birds/detail/extras.ts`, `src/birds/detail/feet.test.ts`, `src/birds/detail/extras.test.ts`
- Modify: `src/birds/detail/spec.ts`, `src/birds/detail/rig.ts`, `src/birds/detail/mesher.ts`,
  `src/birds/detail/legs.ts`, `src/birds/detail/tail.ts`, `src/birds/detail/loft.ts`, `src/birds/detail/build.ts`,
  `src/birds/paint/atlas.ts`, `src/birds/paint/painter.ts`, `src/birds/species/species.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 2's swimmer legs and fixture, Task 3's `REGIONS.shield`.
- Produces:

```ts
// detailSchema:
tail: { shape: 'square' | 'rounded' | 'wedge' | 'notched' | 'none'; angle?: number }
feet?: 'webbed' | 'lobed'
lobeWidth?: number        // metres
parts?: { kind: 'curl'; at: string; dx?: number; height: number; versions: string[] }[]
// Part gains 'curl'; REGIONS.curl = { x: 256, y: 416, w: 64, h: 32 }; BONE_NAMES gains 'curl' (last), parent 'body'
export function buildLegs(m: Mesher, layout: LegLayout, feet?: FeetOptions): { name: string; indexFirst: number; indexCount: number }[]
export function loftWeights(u: number, profile: Profile, rig: LoftRig, rearToTail = false): Weights
export function buildLoft(m: Mesher, profile: Profile, rig: LoftRig, rearToTail = false): LoftResult
export function sweep(m, points, radius, part, weights, radial?): { indexFirst: number; indexCount: number }   // mesher.ts
export function lens(m, S, T, at, bulge, n, part, weights): { indexFirst: number; indexCount: number }
export function buildCurl(m: Mesher, part: PartSpec, profile: Profile): PartResult                             // extras.ts
// Anatomy.parts: { kind, base, top, shown }[]; closed gains 'footR', 'footL' (with feet), 'curl'
// a positive PoseState.tailTilt lifts the detailed builder's tail
```

Webs and lobes are thin closed lenses (0.6 mm at the middle) in the plane the front toes spread in: the ground for a
standing bird, the plane of the trailing tarsus for a swimmer. A web spans each pair of neighbouring toes, its free
edge dipping back 15% between their tips. Lobes are one rounded flap either side of each toe segment, notched to
nothing at the joints: 2 on the inner toe, 3 on the middle, 4 on the outer
(`docs/research/2026-09-25-american-coot.md`, sections 5.5 and 9; the inner and outer counts are its estimate). The
Mallard drake's curl (`docs/research/2026-09-25-mallard.md`, sections 4.1, 5, and 9: two black loops about 20 mm
high on the rump) is two closed tubes, 1.2 mm thick and 3.2 mm apart, each turning 300° up, back, over, and down in
front, on its own `curl` bone. It is built on every bird of the species and scaled to 1e-4 on the versions its
`versions` don't name (set once, after binding, so the bind pose is the whole bird); it is painted as the part
`'curl'`, in its own region.
