Create `src/birds/detail/feet.test.ts`:

```ts
import * as THREE from 'three'
import { buildDetailedBird, type DetailedBirdModel } from './build'
import { signedVolume } from './mesher'
import { TEST_SPEC, TEST_DETAIL, TEST_SWIM_SPEC, TEST_SWIM_DETAIL } from './fixture'
import { parseSpeciesSpec, type SpeciesSpec } from '../spec'
import type { DetailSpec } from './spec'

const withDetail = (spec: SpeciesSpec, patch: Partial<DetailSpec>) => buildDetailedBird(parseSpeciesSpec({ ...spec, detail: { ...spec.detail!, ...patch } }))

/**
 * Whether the triangles in an index range close up: every edge (between distinct points) is met once each way. Points
 * are matched by position, so separate pieces that share a rim, as a lens's two faces do, count as joined.
 */
function isClosed(g: THREE.BufferGeometry, first: number, count: number): boolean {
  const pos = g.getAttribute('position') as THREE.BufferAttribute
  const idx = g.getIndex()!
  const key = (i: number) => [pos.getX(i), pos.getY(i), pos.getZ(i)].map(v => Math.round(v * 1e7)).join(',')
  const edges = new Map<string, number>()
  for (let t = first; t < first + count; t += 3) {
    for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) {
      const a = key(idx.getX(t + k)), b = key(idx.getX(t + ((k + 1) % 3)))
      if (a !== b) edges.set(`${a}|${b}`, (edges.get(`${a}|${b}`) ?? 0) + 1)
    }
  }
  for (const [e, n] of edges) {
    const [a, b] = e.split('|')
    if ((edges.get(`${b}|${a}`) ?? 0) !== n) return false
  }
  return true
}
const feet = (m: DetailedBirdModel) => m.anatomy.closed.filter(c => c.name === 'footR' || c.name === 'footL')
/** the points of one foot's range, in the plane its toes spread in: along the middle toe and across it */
function spread(m: DetailedBirdModel, name: string) {
  const c = m.anatomy.closed.find(x => x.name === name)!
  const pos = m.mesh.geometry.getAttribute('position') as THREE.BufferAttribute
  const idx = m.mesh.geometry.getIndex()!
  const pts: THREE.Vector3[] = []
  for (let t = c.indexFirst; t < c.indexFirst + c.indexCount; t++) pts.push(new THREE.Vector3().fromBufferAttribute(pos, idx.getX(t)))
  return pts
}

describe('feet', () => {
  it('are plain toes without a feet type: no web, no lobes', () => {
    expect(feet(buildDetailedBird(TEST_SPEC))).toHaveLength(0)
    expect(feet(buildDetailedBird(TEST_SWIM_SPEC))).toHaveLength(0)
  })
  for (const kind of ['webbed', 'lobed'] as const) {
    it(`builds ${kind} feet as closed, outward-facing meshes, standing or swimming`, () => {
      for (const spec of [TEST_SPEC, TEST_SWIM_SPEC]) {
        const m = withDetail(spec, { feet: kind })
        expect(feet(m).map(c => c.name)).toEqual(['footR', 'footL'])
        for (const c of feet(m)) {
          expect(isClosed(m.mesh.geometry, c.indexFirst, c.indexCount), `${spec.code} ${c.name}`).toBe(true)
          expect(signedVolume(m.mesh.geometry, c.indexFirst, c.indexCount), `${spec.code} ${c.name}`).toBeGreaterThan(0)
        }
      }
    })
  }
  it('webs the three front toes: the membrane covers the ground between them, most of the way to their tips', () => {
    const toe = TEST_DETAIL.measurements.toe
    /** whether any triangle of the right foot covers the point (x, z) of the ground, seen from above */
    const covers = (m: DetailedBirdModel, x: number, z: number) => {
      const c = m.anatomy.closed.find(e => e.name === 'footR')!
      const pos = m.mesh.geometry.getAttribute('position') as THREE.BufferAttribute, idx = m.mesh.geometry.getIndex()!
      for (let t = c.indexFirst; t < c.indexFirst + c.indexCount; t += 3) {
        const [a, b, d] = [0, 1, 2].map(k => new THREE.Vector2(pos.getX(idx.getX(t + k)), pos.getZ(idx.getX(t + k))))
        const side = (p: THREE.Vector2, q: THREE.Vector2) => (q.x - p.x) * (z - p.y) - (q.y - p.y) * (x - p.x)
        const s1 = side(a!, b!), s2 = side(b!, d!), s3 = side(d!, a!)
        if ((s1 >= 0 && s2 >= 0 && s3 >= 0) || (s1 <= 0 && s2 <= 0 && s3 <= 0)) return true
      }
      return false
    }
    // standing, the right foot's toes spread over the ground 0.45 rad apart: points midway between two toes, half and
    // seven-tenths of a toe out, are under the web and clear of any lobe
    const webbed = withDetail(TEST_SPEC, { feet: 'webbed' }), lobed = withDetail(TEST_SPEC, { feet: 'lobed' })
    for (const angle of [-0.225, 0.225]) {
      for (const out of [0.5, 0.7]) {
        const x = webbed.anatomy.foot.x + Math.cos(angle) * out * toe, z = webbed.anatomy.foot.z + Math.sin(angle) * out * toe
        expect(covers(webbed, x, z), `webbed ${angle} ${out}`).toBe(true)
        expect(covers(lobed, x, z), `lobed ${angle} ${out}`).toBe(false)
      }
    }
  })
  it("lobes each toe to the given width: the Coot's middle toe 10 mm across its lobes (research sections 5.5 and 9)", () => {
    const m = withDetail(TEST_SPEC, { feet: 'lobed', lobeWidth: 0.01 })
    const foot = m.anatomy.foot
    // out along the middle toe (straight forward from the foot, standing), past the shorter toes' tips, how far either
    // side its last lobe reaches
    const middle = spread(m, 'footR').filter(p => p.x > foot.x + 0.78 * TEST_DETAIL.measurements.toe)
    const width = Math.max(...middle.map(p => p.z)) - Math.min(...middle.map(p => p.z))
    expect(width).toBeGreaterThan(0.0095)
    expect(width).toBeLessThan(0.0105)
  })
})
```

Create `src/birds/detail/extras.test.ts`:

```ts
import * as THREE from 'three'
import { buildDetailedBird, type DetailedBirdModel } from './build'
import { measure, tailTipNow } from './measure'
import { loftWeights } from './loft'
import { createProfile } from './profile'
import { signedVolume } from './mesher'
import { detailSchema, type Part } from './spec'
import { TEST_SPEC, TEST_SWIM_SPEC, TEST_SWIM_DETAIL } from './fixture'
import { parseSpeciesSpec } from '../spec'
import { defaultPose } from '../model'
import { REGIONS, ATLAS_SIZE } from '../paint/atlas'
import { paintAtlas, regionStats } from '../paint/painter'

/** the vertices mapped into a part's atlas region */
function inRegion(m: DetailedBirdModel, part: Part): number[] {
  const uv = m.mesh.geometry.getAttribute('uv') as THREE.BufferAttribute
  const r = REGIONS[part]
  const out: number[] = []
  for (let i = 0; i < uv.count; i++) {
    const x = uv.getX(i) * ATLAS_SIZE, y = uv.getY(i) * ATLAS_SIZE
    if (x > r.x && x < r.x + r.w && y > r.y && y < r.y + r.h) out.push(i)
  }
  return out
}
/** where the model's last pose() put these vertices, group space */
function skinnedAt(m: DetailedBirdModel, vertices: number[]): THREE.Vector3[] {
  m.group.updateMatrixWorld(true)
  return vertices.map(i => m.mesh.getVertexPosition(i, new THREE.Vector3()))
}

describe('tailTilt', () => {
  it('lifts the tail when positive, as PoseState says, standing or swimming', () => {
    for (const spec of [TEST_SPEC, TEST_SWIM_SPEC]) {
      const m = buildDetailedBird(spec)
      const rest = tailTipNow(m).y
      m.pose({ ...defaultPose(), tailTilt: 0.3 })
      expect(tailTipNow(m).y, spec.code).toBeGreaterThan(rest + 0.01)
    }
  })
})

describe("tail 'none'", () => {
  const detail = { ...TEST_SWIM_DETAIL, tail: { shape: 'none' as const } }
  const tailless = () => buildDetailedBird(parseSpeciesSpec({ ...TEST_SWIM_SPEC, detail }))
  it('builds no tail', () => {
    const tailed = buildDetailedBird(TEST_SWIM_SPEC), m = tailless()
    expect(inRegion(tailed, 'tail').length).toBeGreaterThan(0)
    expect(inRegion(m, 'tail')).toHaveLength(0)
    expect(inRegion(m, 'tailUnder')).toHaveLength(0)
    // the tail's two faces, 6 x 7 vertices each, are all that is missing
    expect(m.mesh.geometry.getAttribute('position').count).toBe(tailed.mesh.geometry.getAttribute('position').count - 84)
  })
  it('makes the rear of the body the stern: the tail tip is its rearmost point', () => {
    const m = tailless()
    const loft = m.anatomy.closed.find(c => c.name === 'loft')!
    const pos = m.mesh.geometry.getAttribute('position') as THREE.BufferAttribute, idx = m.mesh.geometry.getIndex()!
    let rear = Infinity
    for (let t = loft.indexFirst; t < loft.indexFirst + loft.indexCount; t++) rear = Math.min(rear, pos.getX(idx.getX(t)))
    expect(m.anatomy.tailTip.x).toBeCloseTo(rear, 6)
    expect(measure(m).tailTipAbove).toBe(m.anatomy.tailTip.y)
    expect(measure(m).length).toBeCloseTo(m.anatomy.billTip.distanceTo(m.anatomy.tailTip), 12)
  })
  it('hands the rear of the body from the tail bone to the body over the rump', () => {
    const profile = createProfile(detail.profile)
    const kT = profile.uOf('tailBase')
    expect(loftWeights(0, profile, detail.rig, true)).toEqual([['tail', 1]])
    const mid = loftWeights(kT / 2, profile, detail.rig, true)
    expect(mid.map(([b]) => b)).toEqual(['tail', 'body'])
    expect(mid[0]![1]).toBeCloseTo(0.5, 9)
    expect(loftWeights(kT, profile, detail.rig, true)).toEqual([['body', 1]])
    expect(loftWeights(0, profile, detail.rig)).toEqual([['body', 1]])          // a tailed bird's rear is all body
  })
  it('cocks the stern with tailTilt', () => {
    const m = tailless()
    const rear = inRegion(m, 'skin').filter(i => m.mesh.getVertexPosition(i, new THREE.Vector3()).distanceTo(m.anatomy.tailTip) < 1e-6)
    expect(rear.length).toBeGreaterThan(0)
    const rest = tailTipNow(m)
    m.pose({ ...defaultPose(), tailTilt: 0.4 })
    const cocked = tailTipNow(m)
    expect(cocked.y - rest.y).toBeGreaterThan(0.005)
    for (const v of skinnedAt(m, rear)) expect(v.distanceTo(cocked)).toBeLessThan(1e-6)
  })
})

describe('version-only parts', () => {
  // the Mallard drake's curl: two loops about 20 mm high on the rump, on the male only
  // (docs/research/2026-09-25-mallard.md, sections 4.1, 5, and 9)
  const CURL = { kind: 'curl' as const, at: 'rump', height: 0.02, versions: ['male'] }
  const detail = { ...TEST_SWIM_DETAIL, parts: [CURL], variants: { male: TEST_SWIM_DETAIL.plumage, female: TEST_SWIM_DETAIL.plumage } }
  const spec = parseSpeciesSpec({ ...TEST_SWIM_SPEC, variants: { male: {}, female: {} }, detail })
  const curlBone = (m: DetailedBirdModel) => m.mesh.skeleton.bones.find(b => b.name === 'curl')!
  it('is built on every bird of the species, whatever its version, and on no other species', () => {
    for (const v of [undefined, 'male', 'female']) expect(inRegion(buildDetailedBird(spec, v), 'curl').length, `${v}`).toBeGreaterThan(100)
    expect(inRegion(buildDetailedBird(TEST_SWIM_SPEC), 'curl')).toHaveLength(0)
  })
  it('shows on the versions it names and hides by bone scale on the rest, whatever the pose', () => {
    const male = buildDetailedBird(spec, 'male'), female = buildDetailedBird(spec, 'female'), plain = buildDetailedBird(spec)
    const height = (m: DetailedBirdModel) => {
      const ys = skinnedAt(m, inRegion(m, 'curl')).map(v => v.y)
      return Math.max(...ys) - Math.min(...ys)
    }
    for (const m of [male, female, plain]) {
      const p = defaultPose(); p.lean.x = 1.2; p.tailTilt = 0.3; p.paddle = 1
      m.pose(p)
      m.pose(defaultPose())
    }
    expect(curlBone(male).scale.x).toBe(1)
    expect(male.anatomy.parts[0]!.shown).toBe(true)
    expect(height(male)).toBeGreaterThan(0.018)
    for (const m of [female, plain]) {
      expect(curlBone(m).scale.x).toBeLessThan(0.01)
      expect(m.anatomy.parts[0]!.shown).toBe(false)
      expect(height(m)).toBeLessThan(0.0001)
    }
  })
  it('rises its height from the back at its station, and goes where the body goes', () => {
    const m = buildDetailedBird(spec, 'male')
    const { base, top } = m.anatomy.parts[0]!
    const rump = m.anatomy.stations.find(s => s.label === 'rump')!.position
    const s = TEST_SWIM_DETAIL.profile.find(x => x.label === 'rump')!
    expect(base.distanceTo(rump.clone().add(new THREE.Vector3(0, s.up, 0)))).toBeLessThan(0.002)
    expect(top.distanceTo(base)).toBeCloseTo(CURL.height, 3)
    const rest = skinnedAt(m, inRegion(m, 'curl'))
    const p = defaultPose(); p.lean.x = 1.5
    m.pose(p)
    const tipped = skinnedAt(m, inRegion(m, 'curl'))
    rest.forEach((v, i) => expect(tipped[i]!.length()).toBeCloseTo(v.length(), 9))     // turned with the body about the pivot
    expect(Math.min(...tipped.map(v => v.y))).toBeGreaterThan(Math.max(...rest.map(v => v.y)))
  })
  it('is a closed mesh, painted as its own part', () => {
    const m = buildDetailedBird(spec, 'male')
    const c = m.anatomy.closed.find(x => x.name === 'curl')!
    expect(signedVolume(m.mesh.geometry, c.indexFirst, c.indexCount)).toBeGreaterThan(0)
    const px = paintAtlas({ code: 'testduck', detail, layers: [{ kind: 'fill', where: { part: 'curl' }, color: '#101010' }], profile: createProfile(detail.profile) })
    expect(regionStats(px, 'curl', [0, 1], [0, 1]).mean).toBeLessThan(0.1)
    expect(regionStats(px, 'shield', [0, 1], [0, 1]).mean).toBeCloseTo(0.5, 1)
  })
  it('names versions the species paints, a station it has, and one part of each kind', () => {
    expect(() => detailSchema.parse({ ...detail, parts: [{ ...CURL, versions: ['eclipse'] }] })).toThrow(/part version eclipse/)
    expect(() => detailSchema.parse({ ...detail, parts: [{ ...CURL, at: 'uppertail' }] })).toThrow(/label: uppertail/)
    expect(() => detailSchema.parse({ ...detail, parts: [CURL, CURL] })).toThrow(/more than one curl/)
  })
})
```

- [ ] **Step 4: A swept tube and a closed lens**

In `src/birds/detail/mesher.ts`, replace:

```ts
/** Signed volume of a closed triangle mesh; positive when every face points outward. */
```

with:

```ts
/**
 * A closed tube swept along `points` (at least two), `radius` at each point, capped at both ends, every face outward.
 * Its rings turn with the path, so it can curl. Texture s runs along it (0..1) at t 0.5.
 */
export function sweep(m: Mesher, points: THREE.Vector3[], radius: number[], part: Part, weights: Weights, radial = 6): { indexFirst: number; indexCount: number } {
  const indexFirst = m.indexCount
  const rings: number[][] = []
  let u1 = new THREE.Vector3()
  points.forEach((c, i) => {
    const w = points[Math.min(i + 1, points.length - 1)]!.clone().sub(points[Math.max(i - 1, 0)]!).normalize()
    // carry the ring's frame along the path so it never twists
    if (i === 0) u1 = w.clone().cross(Math.abs(w.z) < 0.9 ? new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, 1) : new THREE.Vector3(1, 0, 0)).normalize()
    else u1.addScaledVector(w, -u1.dot(w)).normalize()
    const u2 = w.clone().cross(u1)
    const row: number[] = []
    for (let j = 0; j < radial; j++) {
      const th = (j / radial) * Math.PI * 2
      const p = c.clone().addScaledVector(u1, Math.cos(th) * radius[i]!).addScaledVector(u2, Math.sin(th) * radius[i]!)
      row.push(m.vertex(p, part, i / (points.length - 1), 0.5, weights))
    }
    rings.push(row)
  })
  for (let i = 0; i < rings.length - 1; i++) {
    for (let j = 0; j < radial; j++) {
      const j1 = (j + 1) % radial
      m.quad(rings[i]![j]!, rings[i]![j1]!, rings[i + 1]![j1]!, rings[i + 1]![j]!)
    }
  }
  const ca = m.vertex(points[0]!, part, 0, 0.5, weights), cb = m.vertex(points.at(-1)!, part, 1, 0.5, weights)
  for (let j = 0; j < radial; j++) {
    const j1 = (j + 1) % radial
    m.tri(ca, rings[0]![j1]!, rings[0]![j]!)
    m.tri(cb, rings.at(-1)![j]!, rings.at(-1)![j1]!)
  }
  return { indexFirst, indexCount: m.indexCount - indexFirst }
}

/**
 * A thin closed lens over a grid of a (along) by b (across), both 0..1: two faces, each `bulge(a, b)` off the middle
 * surface `at(a, b)` along the unit normal `n`, which meet wherever the bulge is 0 (it must be, all round the grid's
 * edge). Every face outward.
 */
export function lens(
  m: Mesher, S: number, T: number, at: (a: number, b: number) => THREE.Vector3, bulge: (a: number, b: number) => number,
  n: THREE.Vector3, part: Part, weights: Weights,
): { indexFirst: number; indexCount: number } {
  const indexFirst = m.indexCount
  // the winding that faces +n: which way the grid turns about n
  const da = at(0.6, 0.5).sub(at(0.4, 0.5)), db = at(0.5, 0.6).sub(at(0.5, 0.4))
  const turn = da.cross(db).dot(n) > 0
  for (const side of [1, -1]) {
    const grid: number[][] = []
    for (let i = 0; i < S; i++) {
      const row: number[] = []
      for (let j = 0; j < T; j++) {
        const a = i / (S - 1), b = j / (T - 1)
        row.push(m.vertex(at(a, b).addScaledVector(n, side * bulge(a, b)), part, a, b, weights))
      }
      grid.push(row)
    }
    for (let i = 0; i < S - 1; i++) {
      for (let j = 0; j < T - 1; j++) {
        const q = [grid[i]![j]!, grid[i + 1]![j]!, grid[i + 1]![j + 1]!, grid[i]![j + 1]!] as const
        if (turn === side > 0) m.quad(q[0], q[1], q[2], q[3])
        else m.quad(q[0], q[3], q[2], q[1])
      }
    }
  }
  return { indexFirst, indexCount: m.indexCount - indexFirst }
}

/** Signed volume of a closed triangle mesh; positive when every face points outward. */
```

```ts
    if (!feet.kind) continue

        const at = (t: number, u: number) => {

        lens(m, 5, 5, at, (t, u) => (MEMBRANE / 2) * Math.sin(Math.PI * u) * Math.sin(Math.PI * t) ** 0.5, n, 'legs', [[B.toes, 1]])
      }

      const half = (feet.lobeWidth ?? 0.2 * layout.toe) / 2
      TOES.forEach((toe, k) => {
        const tip = tips[k]!, dir = tip.clone().sub(foot).normalize()

        for (let g = 0; g < toe.lobes; g++) {
          const at = (a: number, b: number) => foot.clone().lerp(tip, (g + a) / toe.lobes)
            .addScaledVector(across, (2 * b - 1) * half * Math.sin(Math.PI * a) ** 0.6)

        }
      })
    }
```


with:

```ts
 * Skin weights along the spine: body until just before the neck base, then a linear hand-off
 * body -> neck -> neckMid -> head between the labelled stations. Always sums to 1. With `rearToTail` (a bird with no
 * tail), the rear of the body hands off from the tail bone at the rear end to the body at the tail base, so cocking
 * the tail cocks the stern.
 */
export function loftWeights(u: number, profile: Profile, rig: LoftRig, rearToTail = false): Weights {
  if (rearToTail && rig.tailBase) {
    const kT = profile.uOf(rig.tailBase)
    if (u <= 0) return [['tail', 1]]
    if (u < kT) return [['tail', 1 - u / kT], ['body', u / kT]]
  }
  const kNB = profile.uOf(rig.neckBase), kNM = profile.uOf(rig.neckMid), kH = profile.uOf(rig.head)
```

- [ ] **Step 7: The curl**

Create `src/birds/detail/extras.ts`:

```ts
import * as THREE from 'three'
import type { Profile } from './profile'
import type { DetailSpec } from './spec'
import { sweep, type Mesher } from './mesher'

/** a curl's loop turns this far round, radians: up and back from the back, over the top, and down in front */
const CURL_SWEEP = (5 / 3) * Math.PI
/** the length of a curl's feather along its loop, as a multiple of its height (a circle of that diameter, CURL_SWEEP round) */
export const CURL_LENGTH = CURL_SWEEP / 2
/** the curled feathers' thickness (a tube's radius), and how far apart the two loops sit, metres */
export const CURL_RADIUS = 0.0012
const CURL_GAP = 0.0016

export type PartSpec = NonNullable<DetailSpec['parts']>[number]

export interface PartResult {
  kind: PartSpec['kind']
  /** where it leaves the body, profile frame: its bone's joint */
  base: THREE.Vector3
  /** its highest point, profile frame */
  top: THREE.Vector3
  indexFirst: number
  indexCount: number
}

/**
 * The Mallard drake's curl: his two central tail coverts, curled into loops `height` high that rise from the back
 * midline, turn back and over, and come down in front (docs/research/2026-09-25-mallard.md, sections 4.1, 5, and 9:
 * two black loops about 20 mm high on the rump). Two closed tubes side by side, on the curl bone.
 */
export function buildCurl(m: Mesher, part: PartSpec, profile: Profile): PartResult {
  const indexFirst = m.indexCount
  const s = profile.sampleAt(profile.uOf(part.at) + (part.dx ?? 0) / profile.length)
  const up = new THREE.Vector3(s.normal.x, s.normal.y, 0), fwd = new THREE.Vector3(s.tangent.x, s.tangent.y, 0)
  // a little inside the back, so the loops grow out of the feathers
  const base = new THREE.Vector3(s.point.x, s.point.y, 0).addScaledVector(up, s.up - CURL_RADIUS)
  const r = part.height / 2
  let top = base.clone()
  for (const side of [1, -1]) {
    const points: THREE.Vector3[] = [], radius: number[] = []
    for (let k = 0; k <= 24; k++) {
      const phi = -Math.PI / 2 - (CURL_SWEEP * k) / 24
      const p = base.clone().addScaledVector(up, r).addScaledVector(fwd, Math.cos(phi) * r).addScaledVector(up, Math.sin(phi) * r)
      p.z = side * CURL_GAP
      points.push(p)
      radius.push(CURL_RADIUS * (1 - 0.4 * (k / 24)))
      if (p.clone().sub(base).dot(up) > top.clone().sub(base).dot(up)) top = p.clone().setZ(0)
    }
    sweep(m, points, radius, 'curl', [['curl', 1]])
  }
  return { kind: part.kind, base, top, indexFirst, indexCount: m.indexCount - indexFirst }
}
```

### Task 5: Sections that tilt, and the dowitcher's throat retraced with them

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/birds/detail/spec.ts`, `src/birds/detail/profile.ts`, `src/birds/detail/loft.ts`,
  `src/birds/detail/bill.ts`, `src/birds/detail/extras.ts`, `src/birds/species/lobdow.ts`,
  `src/birds/detail/profile.test.ts`, `src/birds/detail/loft.test.ts`, `src/birds/species/species.test.ts`,
  `src/birds/detail/fingerprint.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `createProfile`, `buildLoft`, `worstFold`, `eyePlacement`, `buildShield`, `buildCurl`.
- Produces:

```ts
// stationSchema gains:
tilt: z.number().min(-60).max(60).optional()      // degrees; positive swings the section's lower side forward
// ProfileSample gains:
axis: THREE.Vector2     // the section's up direction: the normal turned back by the (interpolated) tilt; = normal untilted
// worstFold measures each fold against the ring's own plane (square to `axis`), which is square to the spine untilted
```

A section's offsets run along `axis` instead of `normal`: in the loft, the eye's placement, the shield, and the curl.
Tilt is interpolated along the spine like `up`, `down`, and `halfWidth`. Untilted, `axis` is `normal.clone()`, so
every shipped bird builds bit for bit as before (the fingerprint holds through Step 4).

A tilted ring's plane is not square to the spine, so a crease is now measured as a ring reaching back behind the plane
of the ring before it (`t = (axis.y, −axis.x)`, which is the tangent exactly when untilted). Measured against the
spine's tangent instead, a tilted ring would count as folded even where no two rings cross; the retraced dowitcher
reads 0 by its own planes and creased by the tangent. An independent check while planning, counting loft triangles
turned to face into the body, found the retraced dowitcher like the old one: a handful of triangles at the concave
corner under the chin, the worst turned 0.13 (old 0.10) against 0.59 for a real 1.8 mm crease and 0.98 for a 7.8 mm
one. In the viewer, from the side and from three-quarters in front, the throat shows no crease.

```ts
    expect(s.axis.dot(s.tangent)).toBeLessThan(0)
    expect(s.axis.length()).toBeCloseTo(1, 12)
    // untilted, a section stays square to the spine
    const plain = profile.sampleAt(profile.uOf('hip'))
    expect(plain.axis.equals(plain.normal)).toBe(true)
  })
```

```ts
describe('Long-billed Dowitcher: the throat under its resting head', () => {
  // The front of the bird in its tracing photo (iNaturalist 199669733; see lobdow.ts), read off the photo's silhouette
  // at 0.145 mm per pixel with the hip at (760, 720): mm forward of the hip, at every mm of height from 10 mm below the
  // hip (the breast) to 41 mm above it (the chin, under the bill)
  const PHOTO_FRONT = [
    63.50, 64.05, 64.80, 65.25, 65.95, 66.10, 66.40, 66.80, 67.10, 67.85, 68.40, 68.85, 69.30,
    69.85, 70.30, 70.60, 70.85, 71.60, 71.90, 72.20, 72.30, 72.45, 72.60, 72.75, 73.05, 73.05,
    73.20, 73.20, 73.05, 73.05, 73.20, 72.90, 72.75, 72.60, 72.45, 72.05, 71.75, 71.30, 70.75,
    70.15, 69.55, 68.85, 68.25, 67.55, 66.80, 66.10, 65.35, 64.50, 63.65, 63.35, 63.95, 65.65,
  ]
  it('follows the photo from the breast to the chin within 2 mm, without creasing', () => {
    const d = getSpec('lobdow').detail!
    const profile = createProfile(d.profile)
    const m = new Mesher()
    const loft = buildLoft(m, profile, d.rig)
    PHOTO_FRONT.forEach((x, k) => {
      const span = m.spanAtHeight((k - 10) / 1000, loft.indexFirst, loft.indexCount)!
      expect(Math.abs(span[1] * 1000 - x), `${k - 10} mm above the hip`).toBeLessThan(2)
    })
    expect(worstFold(profile, d.rig)).toBe(0)
  })
})
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Retrace the dowitcher's throat**

The seven stations from the neck base to the lores get tilts, and new ups and downs; the nape and crown (not rig
joints) move under 1 mm. The rig joints (neck base, mid-neck, occiput, hip, tail base) and the bill do not move, so
the stitch is untouched. The values were found by a seeded search over these 23 numbers, rounded to 0.1° and 0.1 mm,
that minimised the front outline's error against the photo from 10 mm below the hip to the chin while holding: no
crease; the back of the neck and head within 1 mm of the old trace and never outside the photo's; the eye within
0.4 mm of the photo's pupil (it was 0.39); culmen/head at least 1.81; and the outline under the chin rising steadily to
the bill (an earlier result 0.7 mm from the photo left a 2.6 mm hook under the chin, visible in the viewer). Results:
- Front outline: within 0.8 mm of the photo from the breast up the throat, 1.1–1.8 mm short under the chin (y 34–41
  mm), 1.80 mm at worst (was 8.3 mm), rms 0.66 mm.
- Eye 0.38 mm from the photo's pupil; culmen/head 1.81 (was 1.89; bounds 1.7–2.4, the brief's 1.8–2.0).
- Unchanged: length 219.7 mm, joint 35.7 mm, belly 52.1 mm, wing low 61.2 mm (9.1 above the belly; the deep water's
  55 mm still 1.2 mm under the wing low − 5), stitch bottom 27.0 mm, feet planted.

### Task 6: The viewer: float mode, see-through water, and swimming poses

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/viewer/state.ts`, `src/viewer/state.test.ts`, `src/viewer/main.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the `PoseState` fields (Task 2), `Measures`' floating and bill measures (Tasks 2, 3).
- Produces:

```ts
// ViewerState gains (hash keys, each clamped to its range):
depth: number      // reachDepth, metres, 0..0.3
angle: number      // reachAngle, radians, 0..π/2 (default π/2)
paddle: number     // 0..1; the viewer advances paddlePhase at 2.4 strokes a second
sink: number       // metres, 0..0.15: both birds lowered by it
bob: number        // headBob, −0.2..0.2
tail: number       // tailTilt, radians, −0.5..1
float: boolean     // the lake: opaque water at y = 0 (a swimmer's waterline), the ground dropped 1 m, no grid
see: boolean       // float mode's water see-through (opacity 0.35), to show the hull
// lean's range becomes 0..1.6 (a tip-up is about 1.5); FIELD_VIEWS gains bino15 and scope35
```

The viewer already picks a plumage version for each bird. The readout adds the floating measures for a bird whose
waterline length is above 0, and the bill's widest point.

```ts

```

with:

```ts
function applyLook(): void {
  // Float mode is the lake as the game draws it: opaque water at y = 0, where a swimmer's waterline sits, and no ground
  // near enough to see. See-through lets the hull show under it. Otherwise, a wader's shallow water over the ground.
  water.visible = state.float || state.water > 0
  water.position.y = state.float ? 0 : state.water
  waterMaterial.opacity = state.float && !state.see ? 1 : state.float ? 0.35 : 0.55
  waterMaterial.transparent = waterMaterial.opacity < 1
  waterMaterial.color.set(state.float ? '#4d6a73' : '#6f8a93')
  waterMaterial.needsUpdate = true
  ground.position.y = state.float ? -1 : 0
  grid.visible = !state.float && ground.visible
  spine.visible = state.stations
```

### Task 7: The swim styles scaffold: the foraging split, a shared swimmer body, swim flocks, spacing

**Files:**
- Create: `src/behavior/flock.ts` (`createFlock`, moved out of `probe.ts`), `src/behavior/swimmer.ts` (the shared
  swimmer body), `src/behavior/dabbler.ts` (the first style: cruise, loaf, preen, alert)
- Modify: `src/birds/spec.ts`, `src/birds/spec.test.ts`, `src/location/validate.ts`, `src/location/validate.test.ts`,
  `src/behavior/types.ts`, `src/behavior/probe.ts`, `src/behavior/probe.test.ts`, `src/behavior/swim.ts` (rewritten),
  `src/behavior/swim.test.ts` (rewritten), `src/birds/spawn.ts`, `src/birds/spawn.test.ts`,
  `src/birds/species/mudflat.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `validateCast(loc, birds, specs)` (Task 1); `createFlock`, `between`, `ease` (existing, private in
  `probe.ts`); `randomPointInPolygon`, `pointInPolygon` (existing).
- Produces:

```ts
// src/birds/spec.ts
export const PROBE_STYLES = ['plover', 'sandpiper', 'dowitcher'] as const
export const SWIM_STYLES = ['dabbler', 'shoveler', 'coot', 'grebe'] as const
export const FORAGING = [...PROBE_STYLES, ...SWIM_STYLES] as const
export type ProbeStyle = (typeof PROBE_STYLES)[number]
export type SwimStyle = (typeof SWIM_STYLES)[number]
export type Foraging = (typeof FORAGING)[number]
export const isProbeStyle: (f: Foraging | undefined) => f is ProbeStyle
export const isSwimStyle: (f: Foraging | undefined) => f is SwimStyle

// src/behavior/probe.ts
export const PECK_BOTTOM: Record<ProbeStyle, { lean: number; neckExtend: number; reach: number }>

// src/location/validate.ts: new errors
// `${species} feeds as a ${style}, a probing style, so it cannot swim` and `…, a swimming style, so it cannot probe`

// src/behavior/types.ts
export interface FlockGroup { targetX: number; targetZ: number; retargetAt: number }
export type Neighbour = Pick<BehaviorController, 'x' | 'z' | 'spacing' | 'group'>
BehaviorContext.group?: FlockGroup
BehaviorContext.species?: string
BehaviorContext.neighbours?: () => readonly Neighbour[]
BehaviorController.spacing?: number
BehaviorController.group?: FlockGroup
export const between: (rng, [lo, hi]) => number
export const ease: (t: number) => number   // smoothstep
export const wrap: (a: number) => number   // into -PI..PI

// src/behavior/flock.ts
export interface FlockSettings { radius: number; retargetSeconds: number; drift?: number }
export function createFlock(ctx, zone, x, z, settings: FlockSettings)   // unchanged from probe.ts

// src/behavior/swimmer.ts
export interface SwimSettings {
  cruise: { speed: number; seconds: readonly [number, number] }
  turn: number
  flock: FlockSettings & { drift: number; whenFarther: number }
  spacing: { flock: number; other: number }
  wake: { every: number; size: number; above: number }
}
export interface Company { radius: number; retargetSeconds: number; spacing: SwimSettings['spacing'] }
export const ACCEL = 1, AVOID = 0.12, DRIFT = 0.08
export function createSwimmer(ctx, x, z, S: SwimSettings)   // swim, swimTo, float, clearAt, spot, others, spacingTo, …
export function pickByShare<K extends string>(rng, table: Record<K, { share: number; mean: number }>): K
export const mid: (range: readonly [number, number]) => number
export function startSpot(zone, rng, group: FlockGroup, company: Company, placed: readonly Neighbour[]): [number, number]

// src/behavior/dabbler.ts
export interface DabblerSettings extends SwimSettings { cruising; loaf; preen; alert }
export const MALLARD: DabblerSettings
export function createDabbler(ctx, x, z, S = MALLARD): BehaviorController   // states: 'cruise' | 'loaf' | 'preen' | 'alert'

// src/behavior/swim.ts
export function createSwim(ctx, x, z): BehaviorController   // every style a dabbler until its own task
export function companyOf(foraging?: Foraging, species?: string): Company

// src/birds/spawn.ts
export function speciesTraits(code: string): Pick<BehaviorContext, 'species' | 'wingbeatHz' | 'foraging'>   // + species
// Swimmers get a flock group (one per cast entry, as probers), seeded at a point in their zone and kept there for its
// first retargetSeconds; they start near it, clear of the swimmers already placed (startSpot); every swimmer's
// ctx.neighbours is the list of swimmer controllers in its zone; every bird's ctx.species is its code.
```

The shared body works like this, so that nothing ever needs the zone clamp:
- **Every move goes through `swim`.** A step that would leave the zone is halved until it stays in (a convex zone
  always lets some of it through), so the clamp is never called and a bird never jumps.
- **It pivots to turn about.** Its speed is its wanted speed times `max(0, cos(off))`, `off` being how far round it
  still has to turn, and it gathers way or slows at `ACCEL`, 1 m/s².
- **Spacing is soft and hard.**
  - Inside its spacing plus 0.1 m, the part of its motion toward a neighbour is taken out, and its aim is turned the
    way that doesn't close on it, so it steers round rather than crabbing.
  - Inside the spacing itself, it paddles away at up to `AVOID`, 0.12 m/s.
  - No step ever takes it further inside a neighbour's spacing than it already is.
  - Flockmates keep the style's flock spacing. Any other pair keeps the larger of the two birds' own spacings, so
    each keeps at least its own.

```ts
import { createSwim, companyOf } from './swim'
import { MALLARD } from './dabbler'
import { AVOID, DRIFT, startSpot } from './swimmer'
import { defaultPose } from '../birds/model'
import { makeRng } from '../catalog/rng'
import { pointInPolygon } from '../location/zones'
import type { BehaviorContext, BehaviorController, FlockGroup } from './types'
import type { Foraging } from '../birds/spec'

type Pt = [number, number]
const zone = { kind: 'polygon' as const, points: [[-10, -10], [10, -10], [10, 10], [-10, 10]] as Pt[] }
const WIND = { dirX: 1, dirZ: 0, effective: 0.5 }
const ctx = (seed: number, more: Partial<BehaviorContext> = {}): BehaviorContext => ({
  zone, rng: makeRng(seed), wind: () => WIND, ground: () => -2, ...more,
})

interface Bird { seed: number; x: number; z: number; group?: FlockGroup; foraging?: Foraging; species?: string }
/** Swimmers sharing a zone, each seeing all the others, as the spawner wires them. */
function crowd(birds: Bird[], area: Pt[] = zone.points): BehaviorController[] {
  const list: BehaviorController[] = []
  for (const b of birds) {
    list.push(createSwim(ctx(b.seed, { zone: { kind: 'polygon', points: area }, group: b.group, foraging: b.foraging, species: b.species, neighbours: () => list }), b.x, b.z))
  }
  return list
}
/** Flocks started as the spawner starts them: each bird near its flock's target, clear of the birds placed before it. */
function formed(area: Pt[], flocks: { group: FlockGroup; count: number; foraging?: Foraging; species?: string }[], seed: number): BehaviorController[] {
  const rng = makeRng(seed), list: BehaviorController[] = [], zone = { kind: 'polygon' as const, points: area }
  for (const f of flocks) {
    for (let n = 0; n < f.count; n++) {
      const [x, z] = startSpot(zone, rng, f.group, companyOf(f.foraging, f.species), list)
      list.push(createSwim(ctx(seed * 100 + list.length, { zone, group: f.group, foraging: f.foraging, species: f.species, neighbours: () => list }), x, z))
    }
  }
  return list
}
/** Each pair's distance less the spacing it must keep: a flock's own between flockmates, else the smaller of the two. */
function pairGaps(birds: BehaviorController[]): number[] {
  const out: number[] = []
  for (let a = 0; a < birds.length; a++) {
    for (let c = a + 1; c < birds.length; c++) {
      const A = birds[a]!, C = birds[c]!
      const keep = A.group === C.group ? companyOf(undefined, undefined).spacing.flock : Math.min(A.spacing!, C.spacing!)
      out.push(Math.hypot(A.x - C.x, A.z - C.z) - keep)
    }
  }
  return out
}
const shortestGap = (birds: BehaviorController[]) => Math.min(...pairGaps(birds))
const run = (birds: BehaviorController[], seconds: number, each?: (frame: number) => void) => {
  const poses = birds.map(() => defaultPose())
  for (let i = 0; i < seconds * 30; i++) {
    birds.forEach((b, k) => b.update(1 / 30, poses[k]!))
    each?.(i)
  }
}
/** The most a dabbler moves in a frame: its cruise, plus paddling away from a neighbour, plus the wind. */
const PACE = (MALLARD.cruise.speed + AVOID + DRIFT * WIND.effective) / 30 + 1e-9

describe('swim', () => {
  it('swims as a dabbler when the species has no style', () => {
    const trace = (c: BehaviorController) => {
      const out: string[] = []
      run([c], 60, i => { if (i % 30 === 0) out.push(`${c.state} ${c.x.toFixed(6)} ${c.z.toFixed(6)}`) })
      return out
    }
    expect(trace(createSwim(ctx(1), 0, 0))).toEqual(trace(createSwim(ctx(1, { foraging: 'dabbler' }), 0, 0)))
  })
  it('stays in its zone, on the water, and no frame moves it further than its pace', () => {
    // two flocks of four crowded into a patch 6 m by 4 m, so they keep meeting each other and the edge
    const patch: Pt[] = [[0, 0], [6, 0], [6, 4], [0, 4]]
    for (let seed = 1; seed <= 3; seed++) {
      const rng = makeRng(seed), g1 = { targetX: 1.5, targetZ: 2, retargetAt: 0 }, g2 = { targetX: 4.5, targetZ: 2, retargetAt: 0 }
      const birds = crowd(Array.from({ length: 8 }, (_, k) => ({ seed: seed * 100 + k, x: 0.2 + rng() * 5.6, z: 0.2 + rng() * 3.6, group: k < 4 ? g1 : g2 })), patch)
      let last = birds.map(b => [b.x, b.z])
      run(birds, 120, i => {
        birds.forEach((b, k) => {
          if (!pointInPolygon(b.x, b.z, patch) || b.y !== 0) expect.fail(`seed ${seed}, bird ${k}, frame ${i}: at ${b.x}, ${b.y}, ${b.z}`)
          const moved = Math.hypot(b.x - last[k]![0]!, b.z - last[k]![1]!)
          if (moved > PACE) expect.fail(`seed ${seed}, bird ${k}, frame ${i}: moved ${moved.toFixed(4)} m`)
        })
        last = birds.map(b => [b.x, b.z])
      })
    }
  })
  it('keeps its distance: started apart as the spawner starts them, no two swimmers come closer than the smaller of their spacings', () => {
    // two flocks of five whose patches overlap, in a pond 8 m by 5 m; the tolerance is 1 cm
    const patch: Pt[] = [[0, 0], [8, 0], [8, 5], [0, 5]]
    let worst = Infinity
    for (let seed = 1; seed <= 3; seed++) {
      const birds = formed(patch, [{ group: { targetX: 2, targetZ: 2.5, retargetAt: 60 }, count: 5 }, { group: { targetX: 6, targetZ: 2.5, retargetAt: 60 }, count: 5 }], seed)
      run(birds, 180, () => { worst = Math.min(worst, shortestGap(birds)) })
    }
    expect(worst).toBeGreaterThan(-0.01)
  })
  it('parts swimmers started too close: a pair inside its spacing never closes further, and all are clear within 30 s', () => {
    const patch: Pt[] = [[0, 0], [8, 0], [8, 5], [0, 5]]
    for (let seed = 1; seed <= 3; seed++) {
      const rng = makeRng(seed), g1 = { targetX: 2, targetZ: 2.5, retargetAt: 0 }, g2 = { targetX: 6, targetZ: 2.5, retargetAt: 0 }
      const birds = crowd(Array.from({ length: 10 }, (_, k) => ({ seed: seed * 100 + k, x: 0.2 + rng() * 7.6, z: 0.2 + rng() * 4.6, group: k < 5 ? g1 : g2 })), patch)
      let gaps = pairGaps(birds), late = Infinity
      run(birds, 60, i => {
        const now = pairGaps(birds)
        now.forEach((g, p) => { if (g < -0.01 && g < gaps[p]! - 1e-9) expect.fail(`seed ${seed}, frame ${i}: a pair ${-g} m inside closed further`) })
        if (i >= 30 * 30) late = Math.min(late, ...now)
        gaps = now
      })
      expect(late, `seed ${seed}`).toBeGreaterThan(-0.01)
    }
  })
  it('keeps with its flock: birds far from the shared target swim to it', () => {
    const group = { targetX: 5, targetZ: 5, retargetAt: 1e9 }
    const birds = crowd([{ seed: 11, x: -8, z: -8, group }, { seed: 12, x: 8, z: -8, group }])
    run(birds, 90)
    for (const b of birds) expect(Math.hypot(b.x - group.targetX, b.z - group.targetZ)).toBeLessThan(MALLARD.flock.whenFarther)
  })
  it('never jumps when it starts at the zone edge in a crowd: it paddles clear inside the zone, never clamped back in', () => {
    // A strip 0.8 m wide with birds started a few centimetres apart against its edge: a push straight out of the zone
    // would have to be clamped back, jumping the bird; instead a step that would leave is shortened.
    const strip: Pt[] = [[0, 0], [6, 0], [6, 0.8], [0, 0.8]]
    for (let seed = 1; seed <= 20; seed++) {
      const group = { targetX: 3, targetZ: 0.4, retargetAt: 0 }
      const birds = crowd([
        { seed, x: 0.05, z: 0.05, group }, { seed: seed + 50, x: 0.15, z: 0.05, group },
        { seed: seed + 100, x: 0.05, z: 0.15 }, { seed: seed + 150, x: 5.95, z: 0.75 },
      ], strip)
      let last = birds.map(b => [b.x, b.z])
      run(birds, 60, i => {
        birds.forEach((b, k) => {
          if (!pointInPolygon(b.x, b.z, strip)) expect.fail(`seed ${seed}, bird ${k}, frame ${i}: left the strip at ${b.x}, ${b.z}`)
          const moved = Math.hypot(b.x - last[k]![0]!, b.z - last[k]![1]!)
          if (moved > PACE) expect.fail(`seed ${seed}, bird ${k}, frame ${i}: moved ${moved.toFixed(4)} m`)
        })
        last = birds.map(b => [b.x, b.z])
      })
    }
  })
  it('drifts downwind while it loafs', () => {
    const c = createSwim(ctx(4), 0, 0)
    const pose = defaultPose()
    let guard = 0
    while (c.state !== 'loaf' && guard++ < 20000) c.update(1 / 30, pose)
    for (let i = 0; i < 60 && c.state === 'loaf'; i++) c.update(1 / 30, pose)   // come to a stop first
    const x0 = c.x
    for (let i = 0; i < 30 && c.state === 'loaf'; i++) c.update(1 / 30, pose)
    expect(c.x).toBeGreaterThan(x0)
  })
  it('makes a wake as it swims, a full-size ring no oftener than its wake allows', () => {
    const rings: { t: number; size?: number }[] = []
    let t = 0
    const c = createSwim(ctx(3, { onRipple: (_x, _z, size) => rings.push({ t, size }) }), 0, 0)
    const pose = defaultPose()
    for (let i = 0; i < 120 * 30; i++) { t = i / 30; c.update(1 / 30, pose) }
    expect(rings.length).toBeGreaterThan(10)
    const wake = rings.filter(r => r.size === MALLARD.wake.size)
    expect(wake.length).toBeGreaterThan(10)
    for (let i = 1; i < wake.length; i++) expect(wake[i]!.t - wake[i - 1]!.t).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(MALLARD.wake.every - 1e-9)
  })
  it('is deterministic per seed', () => {
    const a = createSwim(ctx(9), 1, 1), b = createSwim(ctx(9), 1, 1)
    for (let i = 0; i < 500; i++) { a.update(1 / 30, defaultPose()); b.update(1 / 30, defaultPose()) }
    expect([a.x, a.z, a.state]).toEqual([b.x, b.z, b.state])
  })
})
```

```ts
describe('spawnCast swimmers', () => {
  const pond = (cast: Location['cast'], w = 20, d = 12): Location => ({ ...loc(cast), zones: { w: { kind: 'polygon', points: [[0, 0], [w, 0], [w, d], [0, d]] } } })
  const deps = (seed: number) => ({ registry: new BirdRegistry(), wind: new Wind({ direction: 0, strength: 0.3, gustiness: 0.5, seed: 1 }), ground: () => -1, ripples: new RippleBuffer(), seed })
  /** the closest any two birds come, less the spacing they keep: a flock's own between flockmates, else the other */
  function shortestGap(birds: readonly { species: string; position: { x: number; z: number } }[]): number {
    let gap = Infinity
    for (let a = 0; a < birds.length; a++) {
      for (let c = a + 1; c < birds.length; c++) {
        const A = birds[a]!, C = birds[c]!
        const keep = A.species === C.species ? MALLARD.spacing.flock : MALLARD.spacing.other
        gap = Math.min(gap, Math.hypot(A.position.x - C.position.x, A.position.z - C.position.z) - keep)
      }
    }
    return gap
  }

    let gap = Infinity
    for (let i = 0; i < 60 * 30; i++) { world.update(1 / 30, d.wind); gap = Math.min(gap, shortestGap(d.registry.all())) }
    expect(gap).toBeGreaterThan(-0.01)
  })
})
```

```ts
export const SWIM_STYLES = ['dabbler', 'shoveler', 'coot', 'grebe'] as const
```

Create `src/behavior/flock.ts`:

```ts
import type { BehaviorContext } from './types'
import { randomPointInPolygon, clampToPolygon, pointInPolygon } from '../location/zones'
import type { PolygonZone } from '../location/schema'

/** How a flock holds together: its birds keep within `radius` of a target that moves on every `retargetSeconds`. */
export interface FlockSettings {
  radius: number
  retargetSeconds: number
  /** at most this many metres each time the target moves on; unset, it moves anywhere in the zone */
  drift?: number
}

/**
 * A flock's shared target, which moves on now and then (anywhere in the zone, or, with `drift`, at most that many
 * metres), and spots near it for one bird. A target outside the zone (the spawner seeds a probing flock's target at
 * the vantage) goes straight to a point in it: the drift is how a flock moves on within its patch, not how it finds
 * the patch.
 */
export function createFlock(ctx: BehaviorContext, zone: PolygonZone, x: number, z: number, settings: FlockSettings) {
  const group = ctx.group ?? { targetX: x, targetZ: z, retargetAt: Infinity }
  return {
    group,
    /** a spot within `radius` of the shared target, moving the target on first if it is due */
    near(clock: number): [number, number] {
      if (clock >= group.retargetAt) {
        const [px, pz] = randomPointInPolygon(zone.points, ctx.rng)
        const d = Math.hypot(px - group.targetX, pz - group.targetZ)
        if (settings.drift === undefined || d <= settings.drift || !pointInPolygon(group.targetX, group.targetZ, zone.points)) {
          ;[group.targetX, group.targetZ] = [px, pz]
        } else {
          const k = settings.drift / d
          ;[group.targetX, group.targetZ] = [group.targetX + (px - group.targetX) * k, group.targetZ + (pz - group.targetZ) * k]
        }
        group.retargetAt = clock + settings.retargetSeconds
      }
      const a = ctx.rng() * Math.PI * 2, r = ctx.rng() * settings.radius
      return clampToPolygon(group.targetX + Math.cos(a) * r, group.targetZ + Math.sin(a) * r, zone.points)
    },
    /** how far (px, pz) is from the shared target */
    distance(px: number, pz: number): number { return Math.hypot(group.targetX - px, group.targetZ - pz) },
  }
}
```

```ts
const between = (rng: () => number, [lo, hi]: [number, number]) => lo + rng() * (hi - lo)
/** smoothstep: 0 to 1, starting and ending at zero speed */
const ease = (t: number) => t * t * (3 - 2 * t)

/**
 * A flock's shared target, which moves on now and then (anywhere in the zone, or, with `drift`, at most that many
 * metres), and spots near it for one bird. A target outside the zone (the spawner seeds every group at the vantage)
 * goes straight to a point in it: the drift is how a flock moves on within its patch, not how it finds the patch.
 */
function createFlock(ctx: BehaviorContext, zone: PolygonZone, x: number, z: number, settings: { radius: number; retargetSeconds: number; drift?: number }) {
  const group = ctx.group ?? { targetX: x, targetZ: z, retargetAt: Infinity }
  return {
    group,
    /** a spot within `radius` of the shared target, moving the target on first if it is due */
    near(clock: number): [number, number] {
      if (clock >= group.retargetAt) {
        const [px, pz] = randomPointInPolygon(zone.points, ctx.rng)
        const d = Math.hypot(px - group.targetX, pz - group.targetZ)
        if (settings.drift === undefined || d <= settings.drift || !pointInPolygon(group.targetX, group.targetZ, zone.points)) {
          ;[group.targetX, group.targetZ] = [px, pz]
        } else {
          const k = settings.drift / d
          ;[group.targetX, group.targetZ] = [group.targetX + (px - group.targetX) * k, group.targetZ + (pz - group.targetZ) * k]
        }
        group.retargetAt = clock + settings.retargetSeconds
      }
      const a = ctx.rng() * Math.PI * 2, r = ctx.rng() * settings.radius
      return clampToPolygon(group.targetX + Math.cos(a) * r, group.targetZ + Math.sin(a) * r, zone.points)
    },
    /** how far (px, pz) is from the shared target */
    distance(px: number, pz: number): number { return Math.hypot(group.targetX - px, group.targetZ - pz) },
  }
```

Create `src/behavior/swimmer.ts`:

```ts
import type { BehaviorContext, FlockGroup, Neighbour } from './types'
import { turnToward, polygonOf, wrap } from './types'
import { createFlock, type FlockSettings } from './flock'
import { pointInPolygon, randomPointInPolygon } from '../location/zones'
import type { PolygonZone } from '../location/schema'
import type { Rng } from '../catalog/types'

/** What every swim style's settings share. */
export interface SwimSettings {
  /** swimming from spot to spot near the flock: speed in m/s, and how long a cruise lasts */
  cruise: { speed: number; seconds: readonly [number, number] }
  /** the fastest it turns, rad/s */
  turn: number
  /**
   * its flock: the birds keep within `radius` of a target that drifts at most `drift` metres every `retargetSeconds`,
   * and one farther than `whenFarther` from it swims back
   */
  flock: FlockSettings & { drift: number; whenFarther: number }
  /** metres kept from a flockmate, and from any other swimmer */
  spacing: { flock: number; other: number }
  /** a ring on the water every `every` seconds while swimming faster than `above` m/s: the wake, as `swim` always made */
  wake: { every: number; size: number; above: number }
}

/**
 * How a swimmer keeps company, for placing it at the start: its flock's radius (Infinity: anywhere in the zone), how
 * long the flock stays on its first patch, and the spacings it keeps.
 */
export interface Company { radius: number; retargetSeconds: number; spacing: SwimSettings['spacing'] }

/** how fast a swimmer gathers way or slows, m/s² */
export const ACCEL = 1
/** how fast a swimmer paddles away from a neighbour that is too close, at most, m/s */
export const AVOID = 0.12
/** wind drift while not swimming under way, m/s per unit of effective wind (as `swim` always drifted) */
export const DRIFT = 0.08
/** a neighbour this far beyond the spacing is close enough that the swimmer stops closing on it, metres */
const MARGIN = 0.1

/**
 * Position, heading, and the moves every swim style shares: swimming ahead or toward a spot, pivoting to turn about,
 * drifting with the wind, and keeping clear of the other swimmers. Every move goes through `swim`, which never takes
 * the bird out of its zone: a step that would leave it is shortened until it stays in, so nothing is ever clamped.
 */
export function createSwimmer(ctx: BehaviorContext, x: number, z: number, S: SwimSettings) {
  const zone = polygonOf(ctx)
  const flock = createFlock(ctx, zone, x, z, S.flock)
  const body = {
    zone, flock, x, z, heading: ctx.rng() * Math.PI * 2,
    /** forward speed, m/s, eased toward what the style asks for */
    speed: 0,
    /** this bird's own controller, so it can tell itself from its neighbours; the style sets it */
    self: null as Neighbour | null,

    /** how close this bird lets `n` come: its flock spacing for a flockmate, else the larger of the two birds' own */
    spacingTo(n: Neighbour): number {
      return n.group !== undefined && n.group === flock.group ? S.spacing.flock : Math.max(S.spacing.other, n.spacing ?? 0)
    },
    /** the other swimmers in the zone */
    others(): Neighbour[] {
      return (ctx.neighbours?.() ?? []).filter(n => n !== body.self)
    },
    /** true when no other swimmer is closer to (px, pz) than this bird keeps, with `extra` metres to spare */
    clearAt(px: number, pz: number, extra = 0): boolean {
      return body.others().every(n => Math.hypot(n.x - px, n.z - pz) >= body.spacingTo(n) + extra)
    },
    /** a spot near the flock's target, clear of the other swimmers if one of a few tries is */
    spot(clock: number): [number, number] {
      let best = flock.near(clock)
      for (let i = 0; i < 8 && !body.clearAt(best[0], best[1], MARGIN); i++) best = flock.near(clock)
      return best
    },

    /**
     * One frame: swim ahead at up to `want` m/s, turning toward `aim` (radians, or null to hold the heading) no faster
     * than the style turns. It swims slower the further round it still has to turn, so it pivots on the spot to turn
     * about. With `drift` the wind carries it. It keeps clear of its neighbours: it never swims closer to one inside
     * the spacing's margin, and paddles away from one inside the spacing.
     */
    swim(dt: number, want: number, aim: number | null, drift = false): void {
      const crowd = body.crowding()
      if (aim !== null && want > 0 && crowd.close.length) {
        // steer round a close neighbour: aim the way that doesn't close on it, slower the more of the way it blocks
        const [dx, dz] = body.unclose(Math.cos(aim), -Math.sin(aim), crowd.close)
        const len = Math.hypot(dx, dz)
        if (len > 1e-3) aim = Math.atan2(-dz, dx)
        want *= len
      }
      if (aim !== null) body.heading = turnToward(body.heading, aim, S.turn * dt)
      const off = aim === null ? 0 : Math.abs(wrap(aim - body.heading))
      const target = want * Math.max(0, Math.cos(off))
      body.speed += Math.max(-ACCEL * dt, Math.min(ACCEL * dt, target - body.speed))
      let vx = Math.cos(body.heading) * body.speed, vz = -Math.sin(body.heading) * body.speed
      if (drift) {
        const w = ctx.wind()
        vx += w.dirX * DRIFT * w.effective
        vz += w.dirZ * DRIFT * w.effective
      }
      ;[vx, vz] = body.unclose(vx + crowd.px, vz + crowd.pz, crowd.close)
      body.moveBy(vx * dt, vz * dt)
    },
    /** Swim toward (tx, tz) at up to `want` m/s; returns the distance left. */
    swimTo(tx: number, tz: number, want: number, dt: number): number {
      const d = Math.hypot(tx - body.x, tz - body.z)
      // slow into the spot rather than overshoot it
      body.swim(dt, Math.min(want, Math.sqrt(2 * ACCEL * d)), d > 1e-6 ? Math.atan2(-(tz - body.z), tx - body.x) : null)
      return Math.hypot(tx - body.x, tz - body.z)
    },
    /** Sit on the water: slow to a stop and drift with the wind. */
    float(dt: number): void { body.swim(dt, 0, null, true) },

    /**
     * The neighbours it is close to: the direction away from each one inside the spacing's margin, and the velocity of
     * a paddle away from any inside the spacing itself, the full AVOID a quarter of the spacing inside.
     */
    crowding(): { close: [number, number][]; px: number; pz: number } {
      let px = 0, pz = 0
      const close: [number, number][] = []
      for (const n of body.others()) {
        const s = body.spacingTo(n)
        const dx = body.x - n.x, dz = body.z - n.z, d = Math.hypot(dx, dz)
        if (d >= s + MARGIN) continue
        // right on top of each other (only ever at a hand-made start): part sideways
        const [ux, uz] = d > 1e-9 ? [dx / d, dz / d] : [Math.sin(body.heading), Math.cos(body.heading)]
        close.push([ux, uz])
        if (d < s) { px += ux * (s - d) / s; pz += uz * (s - d) / s }
      }
      const p = Math.hypot(px, pz)
      const k = p > 0 ? (AVOID * Math.min(1, 4 * p)) / p : 0
      return { close, px: px * k, pz: pz * k }
    },
    /** (vx, vz) with the part toward any close neighbour taken out */
    unclose(vx: number, vz: number, close: readonly [number, number][]): [number, number] {
      for (let pass = 0; pass < 2; pass++) {
        for (const [ux, uz] of close) {
          const closing = -(vx * ux + vz * uz)
          if (closing > 0) { vx += closing * ux; vz += closing * uz }
        }
      }
      return [vx, vz]
    },
    /**
     * Move by (dx, dz), shortened as far as it takes to stay in the zone and never to come inside a neighbour's spacing
     * any further than it already is. A convex zone always lets some of a step through; a crowd may not, and then it
     * waits.
     */
    moveBy(dx: number, dz: number): void {
      const others = body.others()
      const ok = (nx: number, nz: number) => pointInPolygon(nx, nz, zone.points) && others.every(n => {
        const now = Math.hypot(nx - n.x, nz - n.z)
        return now >= body.spacingTo(n) || now >= Math.hypot(body.x - n.x, body.z - n.z)
      })
      for (let k = 1; k > 1 / 64; k /= 2) {
        const nx = body.x + dx * k, nz = body.z + dz * k
        if (ok(nx, nz)) { body.x = nx; body.z = nz; return }
      }
    },
  }
  return body
}

/**
 * Pick the next activity so that over time each takes its `share` of the bird's time: a spell of mean length `mean`
 * seconds is chosen in proportion to share / mean. Shares need not sum to 1.
 */
export function pickByShare<K extends string>(rng: Rng, table: Readonly<Record<K, { share: number; mean: number }>>): K {
  const keys = Object.keys(table) as K[]
  const weights = keys.map(k => table[k].share / table[k].mean)
  let r = rng() * weights.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0)
  for (let i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
    r -= weights[i]!
    if (r < 0) return keys[i]!
  }
  return keys.at(-1)!
}

/** The mean of a uniform range. */
export const mid = ([lo, hi]: readonly [number, number]) => (lo + hi) / 2

/**
 * Where a swimmer starts: near its flock's target (anywhere in the zone for a bird that keeps a patch of its own), and
 * no closer to the swimmers already placed than either keeps. The spawner calls it, so flocks start formed.
 */
export function startSpot(zone: PolygonZone, rng: Rng, group: FlockGroup, company: Company, placed: readonly Neighbour[]): [number, number] {
  // the target itself is in the zone (the spawner seeds it there), so it will do if nothing better turns up
  let spot: [number, number] = [group.targetX, group.targetZ]
  for (let i = 0; i < 200; i++) {
    let [sx, sz] = [0, 0]
    if (company.radius === Infinity) [sx, sz] = randomPointInPolygon(zone.points, rng)
    else {
      const a = rng() * Math.PI * 2, r = Math.sqrt(rng()) * company.radius
      ;[sx, sz] = [group.targetX + Math.cos(a) * r, group.targetZ + Math.sin(a) * r]
      if (!pointInPolygon(sx, sz, zone.points)) continue
    }
    spot = [sx, sz]
    const keep = (n: Neighbour) => (n.group === group ? company.spacing.flock : Math.max(company.spacing.other, n.spacing ?? 0))
    if (placed.every(n => Math.hypot(n.x - sx, n.z - sz) >= keep(n) + MARGIN)) return spot
  }
  return spot
}
```

Create `src/behavior/dabbler.ts`:

```ts
import type { BehaviorContext, BehaviorController } from './types'
import { approach, between } from './types'
import { createSwimmer, pickByShare, mid, type SwimSettings } from './swimmer'
import type { PoseState } from '../birds/model'

/**
 * A dabbling duck's settings. The numbers are the Mallard's, from docs/research/2026-09-25-mallard.md, section 11
 * (and section 7): most are estimates from a few birds on video, and the time budget is winter's.
 */
export interface DabblerSettings extends SwimSettings {
  /** shares of the bird's time (Gray et al. 1996, open water; the 3% courting, fighting and flying left out) */
  loaf: { share: number; seconds: readonly [number, number] }
  preen: { share: number; seconds: readonly [number, number] }
  alert: { share: number; seconds: readonly [number, number] }
  /** cruising's share of the bird's time; how long a cruise lasts is `cruise.seconds` */
  cruising: { share: number }
}

export const MALLARD: DabblerSettings = {
  // 0.48 m/s sourced (Prange & Schmidt-Nielsen 1970); bouts of 4-14 s are the old swim's, an estimate
  cruise: { speed: 0.48, seconds: [4, 14] },
  // 0.8-1.2 rad/s estimated from video; the old swim's 1.2
  turn: 1.2,
  // a loose group of four within about 3-5 m, flock radius 2-3 m (estimates); the target drifting 3 m a minute is ours
  flock: { radius: 2.5, retargetSeconds: 60, drift: 3, whenFarther: 5 },
  // nearest neighbour 0.3-2 m while feeding: 0.4, just over the 0.36 m traced length, so two never overlap; 0.8 from
  // birds of other flocks, as the Gadwall's unpaired birds keep 0.3-1.2 m (gadwall section 7.6)
  spacing: { flock: 0.4, other: 0.8 },
  // the old swim's wake: a full-size ring every 0.9 s while under way
  wake: { every: 0.9, size: 1, above: 0.3 },
  cruising: { share: 0.311 },
  loaf: { share: 0.068, seconds: [5, 20] },
  preen: { share: 0.065, seconds: [5, 15] },
  alert: { share: 0.057, seconds: [1, 4] },
}

type State = 'cruise' | 'loaf' | 'preen' | 'alert'

/** neck extension for each state: loafing drawn in, alert stretched up */
const NECK: Record<State, number> = { cruise: 0.6, loaf: 0.15, preen: 0.3, alert: 1 }

/**
 * The `dabbler` style: cruise from spot to spot near the flock, loaf and drift with the wind, preen, and look about.
 * A bird that finds itself farther from the flock than `whenFarther` swims back to it first.
 */
export function createDabbler(ctx: BehaviorContext, startX: number, startZ: number, S: DabblerSettings = MALLARD): BehaviorController {
  const b = createSwimmer(ctx, startX, startZ, S)
  let state: State = 'loaf'
  let timer = 0
  let clock = 0
  let following = false
  let sinceWake = 0
  let preen = 0
  let [tx, tz] = [b.x, b.z]

  function next(): void {
    following = b.flock.distance(b.x, b.z) > S.flock.whenFarther
    if (following) {
      state = 'cruise'
      ;[tx, tz] = b.spot(clock)
      // a guard, in case a neighbour or the zone's edge keeps it from getting there
      timer = Math.hypot(tx - b.x, tz - b.z) / S.cruise.speed + 10
      return
    }
    state = pickByShare(ctx.rng, {
      cruise: { share: S.cruising.share, mean: mid(S.cruise.seconds) },
      loaf: { share: S.loaf.share, mean: mid(S.loaf.seconds) },
      preen: { share: S.preen.share, mean: mid(S.preen.seconds) },
      alert: { share: S.alert.share, mean: mid(S.alert.seconds) },
    })
    timer = between(ctx.rng, state === 'cruise' ? S.cruise.seconds : S[state].seconds)
    if (state === 'cruise') [tx, tz] = b.spot(clock)
  }

  const self: BehaviorController = {
    name: 'swim',
    get state() { return state },
    get x() { return b.x }, get y() { return 0 }, get z() { return b.z }, get heading() { return b.heading },
    spacing: S.spacing.other,
    group: b.flock.group,
    update(dt: number, pose: PoseState) {
      clock += dt
      timer -= dt
      if (state === 'cruise') {
        if (b.swimTo(tx, tz, S.cruise.speed, dt) < 0.05) {
          if (following || timer < 1) next()
          else [tx, tz] = b.spot(clock)
        } else if (timer <= 0) next()
      } else {
        b.float(dt)
        if (timer <= 0) next()
      }
      // the wake, while it is under way
      sinceWake += dt
      if (b.speed > S.wake.above && sinceWake >= S.wake.every) { sinceWake = 0; ctx.onRipple?.(b.x, b.z, S.wake.size) }
      // preening works one side and then the other: a slow roll, eased in and out with the state
      preen = approach(preen, state === 'preen' ? 1 : 0, 2, dt)
      pose.lean.z = preen * 0.1 * Math.sin(clock * Math.PI * 0.8)
      pose.neckExtend = approach(pose.neckExtend, NECK[state], 3, dt)
      pose.lean.x = approach(pose.lean.x, 0, 4, dt)
      pose.headBob = approach(pose.headBob, 0, 4, dt)
      pose.tailTilt = approach(pose.tailTilt, 0, 4, dt)
    },
  }
  b.self = self
  next()
  return self
}
```

Replace the whole of `src/behavior/swim.ts` with:

```ts
import type { BehaviorContext, BehaviorController } from './types'
import { createDabbler, MALLARD } from './dabbler'
import type { Company } from './swimmer'
import type { Foraging } from '../birds/spec'

/**
 * Swim in the species' style. No style, or a probing one (validateCast rejects that), swims as a dabbler. Until each
 * style has its own state machine, it swims as a dabbler too.
 */
export function createSwim(ctx: BehaviorContext, startX: number, startZ: number): BehaviorController {
  switch (ctx.foraging) {
    default: return createDabbler(ctx, startX, startZ)
  }
}

/** How a swimmer of this style and species keeps company, for the spawner to start it among its flock. */
export function companyOf(_foraging?: Foraging, _species?: string): Company {
  const S = MALLARD
  return { radius: S.flock.radius, retargetSeconds: S.flock.retargetSeconds, spacing: S.spacing }
}
```

```ts
const STANDING: Behavior[] = ['wade', 'probe']

/** What a species brings to its behavior: its wingbeat, and how it feeds. A species with no spec flaps at 5 Hz. */
export function speciesTraits(code: string): Pick<BehaviorContext, 'wingbeatHz' | 'foraging'> {

  const spec = getSpec(code)
  return { wingbeatHz: spec.wingbeatHz, foraging: spec.foraging }
}
```

- Produces:

```ts
// src/behavior/dabbler.ts
export interface DabblerSettings extends SwimSettings {
  cruising: { share }; loaf, preen, alert: { share; seconds }
  feeding: { share; split: { surface; headDip; tipUp } }       // shares of feeding time, by bout
  surface: { seconds; speed; reachAngle; lean }
  headDip: { dips; seconds; gap; depth; lean; tail }
  tipUp: { count; seconds; gap; lean; tail; strokes; down; up }
  rings: { tipUp; headDip; dabble; dabbleEvery; ahead }
}
export const MALLARD: DabblerSettings, GADWALL: DabblerSettings, WIGEON: DabblerSettings
/** keyed by species code; any other dabbler, or none named, swims as a Mallard */
export const DABBLERS: Readonly<Record<string, DabblerSettings>>   // { mallar3, gadwal, amewig }
export function dabblerFor(species?: string): DabblerSettings
export function createDabbler(ctx, x, z, S = MALLARD): BehaviorController
// states: 'cruise' | 'loaf' | 'preen' | 'alert' | 'surfaceDabble' | 'headDip' | 'tipUp'
// a 'headDip' or 'tipUp' state is a whole bout: a pause, then a dip or tip-up, `dips` or `count` times over

// src/behavior/swim.ts: a dabbler (or no style) gets dabblerFor(ctx.species); companyOf reads the same settings
```

How a dabbler spends its time:
- **What it does next** is picked by `pickByShare`: an activity with mean length `m` and share `s` is chosen with
  weight `s / m`, so over time each takes its share of the bird's time. The shares are the research's time budget
  and feeding split.
- **A feeding bout's share counts its pauses,** as Szijj counted birds by how they were feeding: the level spells
  between tip-ups count as tip-up time, and the bill at the surface between head-dips as head-dip time.
- **Every posture eases.** The bill, the head under, and the tilt each move at a steady rate and are shaped with
  smoothstep: the head in 0.3 s, the tilt over in `tipUp.down` and back in `tipUp.up`.
- **A tip-up's `seconds` is the time the body is past half-way up.** The phase is lengthened by half the difference
  between the tilt times to allow for them.

Create `src/behavior/dabbler.test.ts`:

```ts
import { createDabbler, MALLARD, GADWALL, WIGEON, dabblerFor, type DabblerSettings } from './dabbler'
import { createSwim } from './swim'
import { defaultPose } from '../birds/model'
import { makeRng } from '../catalog/rng'
import type { BehaviorContext } from './types'

type Pt = [number, number]
const zone = { kind: 'polygon' as const, points: [[-20, -20], [20, -20], [20, 20], [-20, 20]] as Pt[] }
const ctx = (seed: number, more: Partial<BehaviorContext> = {}): BehaviorContext => ({
  zone, rng: makeRng(seed), wind: () => ({ dirX: 1, dirZ: 0, effective: 0.3 }), ground: () => -2, ...more,
})

interface Frame { t: number; state: string; lean: number; tail: number; reach: number; depth: number; angle: number; paddle: number; phase: number; neck: number; bob: number }
interface Ring { t: number; size: number }

/** A bird feeding on its own for `seconds`, one record per frame, under the state it was in as the frame began. */
function record(S: DabblerSettings, seed: number, seconds: number, fps = 30) {
  const rings: Ring[] = []
  let t = 0
  const c = createDabbler(ctx(seed, { onRipple: (_x, _z, size) => rings.push({ t, size: size ?? 1 }) }), 0, 0, S)
  const pose = defaultPose(), frames: Frame[] = []
  for (let i = 0; i < seconds * fps; i++) {
    t = i / fps
    const state = c.state
    c.update(1 / fps, pose)
    frames.push({ t, state, lean: pose.lean.x, tail: pose.tailTilt, reach: pose.reach, depth: pose.reachDepth, angle: pose.reachAngle, paddle: pose.paddle, phase: pose.paddlePhase, neck: pose.neckExtend, bob: pose.headBob })
  }
  return { frames, rings }
}

/** Runs of consecutive frames where `test` holds: start and end times. */
function runs(frames: Frame[], test: (f: Frame) => boolean): { from: number; to: number; frames: Frame[] }[] {
  const out: { from: number; to: number; frames: Frame[] }[] = []
  let cur: Frame[] = []
  for (const f of frames) {
    if (test(f)) cur.push(f)
    else if (cur.length) { out.push({ from: cur[0]!.t, to: f.t, frames: cur }); cur = [] }
  }
  return out
}

const share = (frames: Frame[], states: string[]) => frames.filter(f => states.includes(f.state)).length / frames.length
const mean = (xs: number[]) => xs.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / xs.length

/**
 * What each species must show, from its research's sections 7 and 11: the feeding split as a share of feeding time,
 * and tip-up and head-dip lengths in seconds.
 */
const SPECIES = [
  // mallard section 11: tip-up / head-dip / surface 45 / 45 / 10 (Szijj 1965: 42 / 44 / 7); tip-ups 1.5-6 s, mean 4;
  // head-dips 0.5-3 s
  { name: 'Mallard', code: 'mallar3', S: MALLARD, split: { tipUp: [0.35, 0.55], headDip: [0.35, 0.55], surface: [0.04, 0.16] }, tipUp: { range: [1.5, 6], mean: [3.6, 4.4] }, headDip: [0.5, 3] },
  // gadwall section 11: 50 / 38 / 12, an estimate between Szijj (1 / 71 / 13) and McKnight & Hepp in October
  // (93 / 5 / 2); tip-ups 1.1-4.5 s, mean 3.1 (video, n = 23); head-dips 1-3 s
  { name: 'Gadwall', code: 'gadwal', S: GADWALL, split: { tipUp: [0.06, 0.18], headDip: [0.28, 0.48], surface: [0.4, 0.6] }, tipUp: { range: [1.1, 4.5], mean: [2.8, 3.4] }, headDip: [1, 3] },
  // american-wigeon section 11: tip-ups at most 10% of feeding, 2-4 s; head-dips 1-2 s
  { name: 'Wigeon', code: 'amewig', S: WIGEON, split: { tipUp: [0.03, 0.1], headDip: [0.36, 0.56], surface: [0.36, 0.56] }, tipUp: { range: [2, 4], mean: [2.7, 3.3] }, headDip: [1, 2] },
] as const

for (const sp of SPECIES) {
  describe(`dabbler, ${sp.name}`, () => {
    const S = sp.S
    // eight birds for an hour each, at 10 frames a second: enough bouts for the shares to settle
    const long = [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8].map(seed => record(S, seed, 3600, 10))
    const frames = long.flatMap(r => r.frames)
    // For the motion itself, one bird for ten minutes at 60 frames a second, busier feeding than the species: every
    // kind of bout as often as the others, so each shows up many times.
    const busy: DabblerSettings = { ...S, feeding: { share: S.feeding.share * 3, split: { surface: 1, headDip: 1, tipUp: 1 } } }
    const fine = record(busy, 9, 600, 60)

    it('spends its time as the research says: each activity within 0.05 of its share', () => {
      const total = S.cruising.share + S.loaf.share + S.preen.share + S.alert.share + S.feeding.share
      const want: Record<string, number> = {
        cruise: S.cruising.share / total, loaf: S.loaf.share / total, preen: S.preen.share / total, alert: S.alert.share / total,
        surfaceDabble: (S.feeding.share * S.feeding.split.surface) / total, headDip: (S.feeding.share * S.feeding.split.headDip) / total,
        tipUp: (S.feeding.share * S.feeding.split.tipUp) / total,
      }
      for (const [state, w] of Object.entries(want)) expect(share(frames, [state]), state).toBeCloseTo(w, 1)
      for (const [state, w] of Object.entries(want)) expect(Math.abs(share(frames, [state]) - w), state).toBeLessThan(0.05)
    })
    it('splits its feeding between surface dabbling, head-dips, and tip-ups within the research ranges', () => {
      const feeding = frames.filter(f => ['surfaceDabble', 'headDip', 'tipUp'].includes(f.state))
      const of = (state: string) => feeding.filter(f => f.state === state).length / feeding.length
      for (const [state, [lo, hi]] of [['tipUp', sp.split.tipUp], ['headDip', sp.split.headDip], ['surfaceDabble', sp.split.surface]] as const) {
        expect(of(state), state).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(lo)
        expect(of(state), state).toBeLessThanOrEqual(hi)
      }
    })
    it('tips up for the research lengths: the body past half-way up for each, the mean near the research mean', () => {
      // at 10 frames a second a run's measured length is within a frame and a half of the true one
      const q = 0.15
      const ups = long.flatMap(r => runs(r.frames, f => f.lean > S.tipUp.lean / 2)).map(u => u.to - u.from)
      expect(ups.length).toBeGreaterThan(20)
      for (const d of ups) {
        expect(d).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(sp.tipUp.range[0] - q)
        expect(d).toBeLessThanOrEqual(sp.tipUp.range[1] + q)
      }
      expect(mean(ups)).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(sp.tipUp.mean[0])
      expect(mean(ups)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(sp.tipUp.mean[1])
    })
    it('tips up in bouts, level for the gap between tip-ups', () => {
      // inside a tip-up bout, the body is below half-way between tip-ups for its gap, give or take the tilt times
      for (const r of long) {
        for (const bout of runs(r.frames, f => f.state === 'tipUp')) {
          const level = runs(bout.frames, f => f.lean <= S.tipUp.lean / 2).filter(l => l.from > bout.from && l.to < bout.to)
          // the level spell is the gap plus half the tilt over less half the tilt back (0.05 s), measured to 0.15 s
          for (const l of level) {
            expect(l.to - l.from).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(S.tipUp.gap[0] - 0.15)
            expect(l.to - l.from).toBeLessThanOrEqual(S.tipUp.gap[1] + 0.2)
          }
        }
      }
    })
    it('dips its head for the research lengths, the head and upper neck under', () => {
      const dips = long.flatMap(r => runs(r.frames, f => f.depth > S.headDip.depth / 2)).map(u => u.to - u.from)
      expect(dips.length).toBeGreaterThan(50)
      for (const d of dips) {
        expect(d).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(sp.headDip[0] - 0.15)
        expect(d).toBeLessThanOrEqual(sp.headDip[1] + 0.15)
      }
      // fully under: reaching all the way, the bill straight down, the body at the head-dip's lean
      const deep = frames.filter(f => f.depth > S.headDip.depth - 1e-9)
      expect(deep.length).toBeGreaterThan(0)
      const off = deep.find(f => Math.abs(f.reach - 1) > 1e-6 || Math.abs(f.angle - Math.PI / 2) > 1e-6 || Math.abs(f.lean - S.headDip.lean) > 1e-6)
      expect(off).toBeUndefined()
    })
    it('upends at full tilt: the body 70-90 degrees nose-down, the tail up, the feet out and paddling at its stroke rate', () => {
      const full = fine.frames.filter(f => f.paddle > 0.999)
      expect(full.length).toBeGreaterThan(60)
      const degrees = full.map(f => (f.lean * 180) / Math.PI)
      expect(Math.min(...degrees)).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(70)
      expect(Math.max(...degrees)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(90)
      for (const f of full) expect(f.tail).toBeCloseTo(S.tipUp.tail, 2)
      expect(Math.max(...full.map(f => f.reach))).toBeLessThan(0.01)
      // the stroke cycle advances strokes / fps each frame, wrapping at 1
      for (let i = 1; i < fine.frames.length; i++) {
        const d = (fine.frames[i]!.phase - fine.frames[i - 1]!.phase + 1) % 1
        if (Math.abs(d - S.tipUp.strokes / 60) > 1e-9) expect.fail(`the stroke advanced ${d} at ${fine.frames[i]!.t} s`)
      }
    })
    it('eases over in 0.2-0.5 s and back in 0.2-0.3 s, starting and ending gently', () => {
      const { frames: f } = fine
      let overs = 0
      for (let i = 1; i < f.length; i++) {
        // going over: from the first frame off level to the first at 99% of full tilt
        if (f[i - 1]!.paddle === 0 && f[i]!.paddle > 0) {
          overs++
          expect(f[i]!.lean - f[i - 1]!.lean).toBeLessThan(0.02)
          let j = i
          while (j < f.length && f[j]!.paddle < 0.99) j++
          if (j < f.length) { expect(f[j]!.t - f[i]!.t).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0.2); expect(f[j]!.t - f[i]!.t).toBeLessThanOrEqual(0.5) }
        }
        // coming back: from the first frame off full tilt to the first below 1%
        if (f[i - 1]!.paddle === 1 && f[i]!.paddle < 1) {
          let j = i
          while (j < f.length && f[j]!.paddle > 0.01) j++
          if (j < f.length) { expect(f[j]!.t - f[i]!.t).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0.15); expect(f[j]!.t - f[i]!.t).toBeLessThanOrEqual(0.3) }
        }
      }
      expect(overs).toBeGreaterThan(3)
    })
    it('moves smoothly: at 60 fps no part of the pose jumps in a frame', () => {
      // A tip-up coming back level in 0.25 s along smoothstep moves at most 1.5 / 0.25 / 60 = 0.1 of its range in a
      // frame; the limits allow for a head-dip easing at the same time. A snap would move the whole range at once.
      const most = { lean: 0.15, tail: 0.05, reach: 0.15, depth: 0.02, angle: 0.1, paddle: 0.1, neck: 0.1, bob: 0.01 }
      const { frames: f } = fine
      for (let i = 1; i < f.length; i++) {
        for (const [k, limit] of Object.entries(most) as [keyof typeof most, number][]) {
          const d = Math.abs(f[i]![k] - f[i - 1]![k])
          if (d > limit) expect.fail(`${k} changed by ${d.toFixed(4)} at ${f[i]!.t.toFixed(2)} s (${f[i]!.state})`)
        }
      }
    })
    it('rings the water at the start and end of each tip-up and head-dip, and every 1-2 s while dabbling', () => {
      const { frames: f, rings } = fine
      const at = (t: number, size: number) => rings.some(r => Math.abs(r.t - t) < 1e-9 && r.size === size)
      const ups = runs(f, x => x.paddle > 0), dips = runs(f, x => x.depth > 0)
      expect(ups.length).toBeGreaterThan(3)
      expect(dips.length).toBeGreaterThan(10)
      // a phase rings in the frame its posture starts to move
      for (const u of ups) expect(at(u.from, S.rings.tipUp), `tip-up at ${u.from}`).toBe(true)
      for (const d of dips) expect(at(d.from, S.rings.headDip), `head-dip at ${d.from}`).toBe(true)
      expect(rings.filter(r => r.size === S.rings.tipUp).length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(2 * ups.length - 1)
      expect(rings.filter(r => r.size === S.rings.headDip).length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(2 * dips.length - 1)
      // while dabbling at the surface, a small ring every 1-2 s
      const dabbling = runs(f, x => x.state === 'surfaceDabble')
      let gaps = 0
      for (const d of dabbling) {
        const inside = rings.filter(r => r.size === S.rings.dabble && r.t >= d.from && r.t < d.to).map(r => r.t)
        for (let i = 1; i < inside.length; i++) {
          gaps++
          expect(inside[i]! - inside[i - 1]!).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(S.rings.dabbleEvery[0] - 1e-9)
          expect(inside[i]! - inside[i - 1]!).toBeLessThanOrEqual(S.rings.dabbleEvery[1] + 1 / 60 + 1e-9)
        }
      }
      expect(gaps).toBeGreaterThan(3)
    })
    it(`is what swim gives a species coded ${sp.code}`, () => {
      const trace = (make: () => ReturnType<typeof createSwim>) => {
        const c = make(), pose = defaultPose(), out: string[] = []
        for (let i = 0; i < 60 * 30; i++) { c.update(1 / 30, pose); if (i % 30 === 0) out.push(`${c.state} ${c.x.toFixed(6)} ${pose.lean.x.toFixed(6)}`) }
        return out
      }
      expect(trace(() => createSwim(ctx(8, { species: sp.code }), 0, 0))).toEqual(trace(() => createDabbler(ctx(8), 0, 0, S)))
      expect(dabblerFor(sp.code)).toBe(S)
    })
  })
}

describe('dabbler settings', () => {
  it('fall back to the Mallard for a dabbler with none of its own', () => {
    expect(dabblerFor('nosuchduck')).toBe(MALLARD)
    expect(dabblerFor(undefined)).toBe(MALLARD)
    expect(dabblerFor('constructor')).toBe(MALLARD)
  })
})
```
