Replace the whole of `src/behavior/dabbler.ts` with:

```ts
import type { BehaviorContext, BehaviorController } from './types'
import { approach, between, ease } from './types'
import { createSwimmer, pickByShare, mid, type SwimSettings } from './swimmer'
import type { PoseState } from '../birds/model'

type Range = readonly [number, number]

/** A dabbling duck's settings: the shared swimmer's, its time budget, and how it feeds. */
export interface DabblerSettings extends SwimSettings {
  /** shares of the bird's time; they need not sum to 1. How long a cruise lasts is `cruise.seconds`. */
  cruising: { share: number }
  loaf: { share: number; seconds: Range }
  preen: { share: number; seconds: Range }
  alert: { share: number; seconds: Range }
  /**
   * feeding's share of the bird's time, and how it splits between bouts of surface dabbling, head-dips, and tip-ups.
   * A bout's share counts its pauses: the level spells between tip-ups, the bill at the surface between head-dips.
   */
  feeding: { share: number; split: { surface: number; headDip: number; tipUp: number } }
  /** surface dabbling: the bill at the water, swimming slowly, a small ring every 1-2 s where the bill works */
  surface: { seconds: Range; speed: number; reachAngle: number; lean: number }
  /**
   * head-dips, in bouts of `dips`, each `seconds` long with the head and upper neck `depth` metres under, and `gap`
   * seconds with the bill back at the surface between them; the body tipped `lean` and the tail cocked `tail`
   */
  headDip: { dips: Range; seconds: Range; gap: Range; depth: number; lean: number; tail: number }
  /**
   * tip-ups, in bouts of `count`, each `seconds` long with the body past half-way up (`lean` radians nose-down at
   * full tilt, the tail cocked `tail`, the feet out and paddling `strokes` a second), `gap` seconds level between;
   * `down` and `up` are how long it takes to tilt over and to come back level, eased at both ends
   */
  tipUp: { count: Range; seconds: Range; gap: Range; lean: number; tail: number; strokes: number; down: number; up: number }
  /** ring sizes: at the start and end of each tip-up and head-dip, and while dabbling; `ahead` is where the bill works */
  rings: { tipUp: number; headDip: number; dabble: number; dabbleEvery: Range; ahead: number }
}

/**
 * The Mallard, from docs/research/2026-09-25-mallard.md, section 11 (behaviour in section 7). Sourced: the cruise
 * speed (Prange & Schmidt-Nielsen 1970), the time budget (Gray et al. 1996, open water, both sexes, winter; the 3%
 * courting, fighting, and flying left out), the feeding split (Szijj 1965: 42 / 44 / 7 in proportion), the tip-up's
 * 4.2 s mean and 5.8 s maximum (Szijj 1965) and near-vertical body, and the 2.4 strokes a second (Prange &
 * Schmidt-Nielsen 1970). The rest are estimates from a few birds on video.
 */
export const MALLARD: DabblerSettings = {
  // 0.48 m/s (0.45-0.51); cruise bouts of 4-14 s are the old swim's, an estimate
  cruise: { speed: 0.48, seconds: [4, 14] },
  // 0.8-1.2 rad/s estimated from video; the old swim's 1.2
  turn: 1.2,
  // a loose group of four within about 3-5 m, flock radius 2-3 m (estimates); the target drifting 3 m a minute is ours
  flock: { radius: 2.5, retargetSeconds: 60, drift: 3, whenFarther: 5 },
  // nearest neighbour 0.3-2 m while feeding: 0.4, just over the 0.36 m traced length, so two never overlap; 0.8 from
  // birds of other flocks, as the Gadwall's unpaired birds keep 0.3-1.2 m (gadwall section 7.6)
  spacing: { flock: 0.4, other: 0.8 },
  // the old swim's wake: a full-size ring every 0.9 s while under way
  wake: { every: 0.9, size: 1, above: 0.3 },
  cruising: { share: 0.311 },
  loaf: { share: 0.068, seconds: [5, 20] },
  preen: { share: 0.065, seconds: [5, 15] },
  alert: { share: 0.057, seconds: [1, 4] },
  feeding: { share: 0.468, split: { surface: 0.1, headDip: 0.45, tipUp: 0.45 } },
  // bouts of 5-25 s (the lab's shallow feeding bouts were 22.2 s), swimming at 0.05-0.15 m/s; the bill about 30 degrees
  // down and the body tipped 5 degrees, from the Wigeon's measured surface-picking pose (american-wigeon section 11)
  surface: { seconds: [5, 25], speed: 0.1, reachAngle: 0.5, lean: 0.09 },
  // 0.5-3 s a dip, mean about 2 (so 1-3 here); the head and part of the neck under, the body level or tipped 0-15
  // degrees (7.5 here), the tail up about 10 degrees. Dips a bout and the pauses are the Gadwall's (gadwall section 11:
  // 3-8, about 1 s up), shortened to 2-6 for a bird that tips up as much as it dips. The head goes 0.15 m under, where
  // the Gadwall's head-dips find plants 10-30 cm down (gadwall section 7.3).
  headDip: { dips: [2, 6], seconds: [1, 3], gap: [0.5, 1.5], depth: 0.15, lean: 0.13, tail: 0.17 },
  // mean 4 s (1.5-6): 2-6 here; 2-4 in a bout with 1-5 s level between; 80-90 degrees (85 here, 1.48 rad); 0.2-0.5 s
  // over and 0.2-0.3 s back (0.35 and 0.25 here); the tail's cock at full tilt is an estimate
  tipUp: { count: [2, 4], seconds: [2, 6], gap: [1, 5], lean: 1.48, tail: 0.4, strokes: 2.4, down: 0.35, up: 0.25 },
  // ring sizes are estimates: a tip-up's swirl larger than a head-dip's, a working bill's the dowitcher's bill ring
  rings: { tipUp: 0.7, headDip: 0.5, dabble: 0.4, dabbleEvery: [1, 2], ahead: 0.18 },
}

/**
 * The Gadwall, from docs/research/2026-09-25-gadwall.md, section 11: it dips and dabbles more and tips up less than
 * the Mallard, in shorter tip-ups. Its cruise speed and time budget are estimates (the budget from Paulus 1984's 44%
 * feeding in October and Gray et al. 1996); its tip-ups were timed on video (n = 23).
 */
export const GADWALL: DabblerSettings = {
  ...MALLARD,
  // 0.40 m/s (0.35-0.48), a smaller hull than the Mallard's
  cruise: { speed: 0.4, seconds: [4, 14] },
  // a pair plus one: the pair 0.1-0.45 m apart (0.35 here, over its 0.325 m length), others 0.3-1.2 m; radius 1.5 m
  // for three birds is ours
  flock: { radius: 1.5, retargetSeconds: 60, drift: 3, whenFarther: 3 },
  spacing: { flock: 0.35, other: 0.8 },
  cruising: { share: 0.2 },
  loaf: { share: 0.2, seconds: [10, 60] },
  alert: { share: 0.1, seconds: [1, 3] },
  preen: { share: 0.05, seconds: [5, 15] },
  feeding: { share: 0.45, split: { surface: 0.5, headDip: 0.38, tipUp: 0.12 } },
  // the bill and face in the water for 2-10 s at about 0.05 m/s
  surface: { seconds: [2, 10], speed: 0.05, reachAngle: 0.5, lean: 0.09 },
  // 1-3 s under, about 1 s up between, 3-8 in a bout; the body tipped 10-30 degrees (20 here), the tail not raised
  headDip: { dips: [3, 8], seconds: [1, 3], gap: [0.5, 1.5], depth: 0.15, lean: 0.35, tail: 0 },
  // 3.1 s (1.1-4.5, most 2-4): 1.7-4.5 here, mean 3.1; 5-15 in a row, 2-4 s level between; tilting in and out in
  // 0.25-0.5 s; about 2.5 strokes a second
  tipUp: { count: [5, 15], seconds: [1.7, 4.5], gap: [2, 4], lean: 1.48, tail: 0.4, strokes: 2.5, down: 0.35, up: 0.25 },
}

/**
 * The American Wigeon, from docs/research/2026-09-25-american-wigeon.md, section 11: a dabbler with its own settings.
 * Its open-water feeding is surface picking and head-dips, a dip about every 6 s (Knapton & Knudsen 1978, computed),
 * with rare tip-ups. Everything but the dip rate is an estimate.
 */
export const WIGEON: DabblerSettings = {
  ...MALLARD,
  // 0.4 m/s (0.3-0.5)
  cruise: { speed: 0.4, seconds: [4, 14] },
  // nearest neighbour 0.3-1.0 m (0.35 here, over its 0.33 m length); radius 1.5 m for five birds is ours
  flock: { radius: 1.5, retargetSeconds: 60, drift: 3, whenFarther: 3 },
  spacing: { flock: 0.35, other: 0.8 },
  // feed 50%, cruise 15-20% (17.5 here), loaf 25%, preen 10%, alert 2%
  cruising: { share: 0.175 },
  loaf: { share: 0.25, seconds: [10, 60] },
  preen: { share: 0.1, seconds: [5, 15] },
  alert: { share: 0.02, seconds: [1, 3] },
  // tip-ups at most 10% of feeding (8 here); the rest split evenly between surface picking and dip bouts, which the
  // research doesn't divide
  feeding: { share: 0.5, split: { surface: 0.46, headDip: 0.46, tipUp: 0.08 } },
  // surface picking in bouts of 5-20 s at 0.1-0.2 m/s, the body pitched 5-10 degrees bow-down
  surface: { seconds: [5, 20], speed: 0.15, reachAngle: 0.5, lean: 0.13 },
  // a dip of 1-2 s about every 6 s: 3-6 s of picking at the surface between; 3-8 in a bout (ours); the body pitched
  // 10-15 degrees
  headDip: { dips: [3, 8], seconds: [1, 2], gap: [3, 6], depth: 0.15, lean: 0.22, tail: 0 },
  // 2-4 s, 70-90 degrees (80 here, 1.40 rad); two or three together (ours), paddling as the Gadwall does
  tipUp: { count: [2, 3], seconds: [2, 4], gap: [1, 5], lean: 1.4, tail: 0.4, strokes: 2.5, down: 0.35, up: 0.25 },
}

/** A species' dabbler settings, keyed by its code; any other dabbler (or none named) swims as a Mallard. */
export const DABBLERS: Readonly<Record<string, DabblerSettings>> = { mallar3: MALLARD, gadwal: GADWALL, amewig: WIGEON }
export const dabblerFor = (species?: string): DabblerSettings => (species !== undefined && Object.hasOwn(DABBLERS, species) ? DABBLERS[species]! : MALLARD)

type State = 'cruise' | 'loaf' | 'preen' | 'alert' | 'surfaceDabble' | 'headDip' | 'tipUp'

/** neck extension for each state: loafing drawn in, alert and feeding stretched */
const NECK: Record<State, number> = { cruise: 0.6, loaf: 0.15, preen: 0.3, alert: 1, surfaceDabble: 0.8, headDip: 1, tipUp: 1 }
/** how long the head takes to go under, or come up, s */
const DIP = 0.3
/** A whole number from lo to hi, each equally likely. */
const count = (rng: () => number, [lo, hi]: Range) => lo + Math.floor(rng() * (hi - lo + 1))
/** Move `v` toward `want` by at most `by`. */
const step = (v: number, want: number, by: number) => (v < want ? Math.min(want, v + by) : Math.max(want, v - by))

/**
 * The `dabbler` style: cruise from spot to spot near the flock, loaf and drift with the wind, preen, look about, and
 * feed in bouts: dabbling at the surface as it swims slowly, dipping its head, or tipping up. A bird that finds itself
 * farther from the flock than `whenFarther` swims back to it first.
 */
export function createDabbler(ctx: BehaviorContext, startX: number, startZ: number, S: DabblerSettings = MALLARD): BehaviorController {
  const b = createSwimmer(ctx, startX, startZ, S)
  let state: State = 'loaf'
  let timer = 0
  let clock = 0
  let following = false
  let [tx, tz] = [b.x, b.z]
  // a feeding bout: how many dips or tip-ups are left, and whether the bird is in one (under or up) or between
  let left = 0
  let active = false
  // eased posture: how far the bill is toward the water (0 head up .. 1 at the surface), how far the head is under,
  // and how far the body is tipped up, each 0..1 moving at a steady rate and shaped with smoothstep
  let bill = 0, under = 0, tilt = 0
  let preen = 0
  let sinceWake = 0, nextDabble = 0
  let paddlePhase = 0

  const TABLE = {
    cruise: { share: S.cruising.share, mean: mid(S.cruise.seconds) },
    loaf: { share: S.loaf.share, mean: mid(S.loaf.seconds) },
    preen: { share: S.preen.share, mean: mid(S.preen.seconds) },
    alert: { share: S.alert.share, mean: mid(S.alert.seconds) },
    surfaceDabble: { share: S.feeding.share * S.feeding.split.surface, mean: mid(S.surface.seconds) },
    // a bout is a pause and a dip (or a tip-up), `dips` (or `count`) times over
    headDip: { share: S.feeding.share * S.feeding.split.headDip, mean: mid(S.headDip.dips) * (mid(S.headDip.seconds) + mid(S.headDip.gap)) },
    tipUp: { share: S.feeding.share * S.feeding.split.tipUp, mean: mid(S.tipUp.count) * (mid(S.tipUp.seconds) + mid(S.tipUp.gap)) },
  }

  const ring = (size: number, ahead: number) => ctx.onRipple?.(b.x + Math.cos(b.heading) * ahead, b.z - Math.sin(b.heading) * ahead, size)

  function next(): void {
    following = b.flock.distance(b.x, b.z) > S.flock.whenFarther
    if (following) {
      state = 'cruise'
      ;[tx, tz] = b.spot(clock)
      // a guard, in case a neighbour or the zone's edge keeps it from getting there
      timer = Math.hypot(tx - b.x, tz - b.z) / S.cruise.speed + 10
      return
    }
    state = pickByShare(ctx.rng, TABLE)
    // a bout opens with a pause, so a dip or tip-up never runs straight on from the last bout's
    if (state === 'headDip') { left = count(ctx.rng, S.headDip.dips); active = false; timer = between(ctx.rng, S.headDip.gap) }
    else if (state === 'tipUp') { left = count(ctx.rng, S.tipUp.count); active = false; timer = between(ctx.rng, S.tipUp.gap) }
    else if (state === 'surfaceDabble') { timer = between(ctx.rng, S.surface.seconds); [tx, tz] = b.spot(clock) }
    else {
      timer = between(ctx.rng, state === 'cruise' ? S.cruise.seconds : S[state].seconds)
      if (state === 'cruise') [tx, tz] = b.spot(clock)
    }
  }
  function startDip(): void {
    active = true; left--
    timer = between(ctx.rng, S.headDip.seconds)
    ring(S.rings.headDip, S.rings.ahead)
  }
  function startTip(): void {
    active = true; left--
    // the phase runs from starting over to starting back; the body is past half-way for exactly the drawn time
    timer = between(ctx.rng, S.tipUp.seconds) + (S.tipUp.down - S.tipUp.up) / 2
    ring(S.rings.tipUp, 0)
  }
  /** the end of a dip or tip-up: a ring, then the pause before the next, or the end of the bout */
  function endPhase(): void {
    active = false
    ring(state === 'tipUp' ? S.rings.tipUp : S.rings.headDip, state === 'tipUp' ? 0 : S.rings.ahead)
    if (left <= 0) next()
    else timer = between(ctx.rng, state === 'tipUp' ? S.tipUp.gap : S.headDip.gap)
  }

  const self: BehaviorController = {
    name: 'swim',
    get state() { return state },
    get x() { return b.x }, get y() { return 0 }, get z() { return b.z }, get heading() { return b.heading },
    spacing: S.spacing.other,
    group: b.flock.group,
    update(dt: number, pose: PoseState) {
      clock += dt
      timer -= dt
      if (state === 'cruise') {
        if (b.swimTo(tx, tz, S.cruise.speed, dt) < 0.05) {
          if (following || timer < 1) next()
          else [tx, tz] = b.spot(clock)
        } else if (timer <= 0) next()
      } else if (state === 'surfaceDabble') {
        // pottering toward a spot near the flock, the bill working the surface and ringing it every 1-2 s (the count
        // runs on from one dabbling bout into the next)
        if (b.swimTo(tx, tz, S.surface.speed, dt) < 0.05) [tx, tz] = b.spot(clock)
        nextDabble -= dt
        if (nextDabble <= 0 && bill > 0.99) { nextDabble = between(ctx.rng, S.rings.dabbleEvery); ring(S.rings.dabble, S.rings.ahead) }
        if (timer <= 0) next()
      } else if (state === 'headDip' || state === 'tipUp') {
        b.float(dt)
        if (timer <= 0) {
          if (active) endPhase()
          else if (state === 'headDip') startDip()
          else startTip()
        }
      } else {
        b.float(dt)
        if (timer <= 0) next()
      }
      // the wake, while it is under way
      sinceWake += dt
      if (b.speed > S.wake.above && sinceWake >= S.wake.every) { sinceWake = 0; ctx.onRipple?.(b.x, b.z, S.wake.size) }

      // posture: each part moves at a steady rate toward where this moment wants it, shaped with smoothstep
      const dipping = state === 'headDip'
      const wantBill = state === 'surfaceDabble' || dipping ? 1 : 0
      const wantUnder = dipping && active ? 1 : 0
      const wantTilt = state === 'tipUp' && active ? 1 : 0
      bill = step(bill, wantBill, dt / DIP)
      under = step(under, wantUnder, dt / DIP)
      tilt = step(tilt, wantTilt, dt / (wantTilt > tilt ? S.tipUp.down : S.tipUp.up))
      const kBill = ease(bill), kUnder = ease(under), kTilt = ease(tilt)
      // the head-dip's lean and tail, the surface pose's lean, blended as far as the bill is down
      const lean = kUnder * S.headDip.lean + (1 - kUnder) * kBill * S.surface.lean
      pose.lean.x = lean + (S.tipUp.lean - lean) * kTilt
      pose.tailTilt = kUnder * S.headDip.tail + (S.tipUp.tail - kUnder * S.headDip.tail) * kTilt
      // in a tip-up the body carries the head down; the reach is the head-dip's and the dabbling bill's
      pose.reach = kBill * (1 - kTilt)
      pose.reachDepth = kUnder * S.headDip.depth
      pose.reachAngle = S.surface.reachAngle + (Math.PI / 2 - S.surface.reachAngle) * kUnder
      pose.paddle = kTilt
      paddlePhase = (paddlePhase + S.tipUp.strokes * dt) % 1
      pose.paddlePhase = paddlePhase
      // preening works one side and then the other: a slow roll, eased in and out with the state
      preen = approach(preen, state === 'preen' ? 1 : 0, 2, dt)
      pose.lean.z = preen * 0.1 * Math.sin(clock * Math.PI * 0.8)
      pose.neckExtend = approach(pose.neckExtend, NECK[state], 3, dt)
      pose.headBob = approach(pose.headBob, 0, 4, dt)
    },
  }
  b.self = self
  next()
  return self
}
```

In `src/behavior/swim.ts`, replace

```ts
import type { BehaviorContext, BehaviorController } from './types'
import { createDabbler, MALLARD } from './dabbler'
import type { Company } from './swimmer'
```

with

```ts
import type { BehaviorContext, BehaviorController } from './types'
import { createDabbler, dabblerFor } from './dabbler'
import type { Company } from './swimmer'
```

Then replace

```ts
/**
 * Swim in the species' style. No style, or a probing one (validateCast rejects that), swims as a dabbler. Until each
 * style has its own state machine, it swims as a dabbler too.
 */
```

with

```ts
/**
 * Swim in the species' style. No style, or a probing one (validateCast rejects that), swims as a dabbler, with the
 * species' own dabbler settings if it has them. Until each style has its own state machine, it swims as a dabbler too.
 */
```

Then replace

```ts
  switch (ctx.foraging) {
    default: return createDabbler(ctx, startX, startZ)
  }
```

with

```ts
  switch (ctx.foraging) {
    default: return createDabbler(ctx, startX, startZ, dabblerFor(ctx.species))
  }
```

Then replace

```ts
/** How a swimmer of this style and species keeps company, for the spawner to start it among its flock. */
export function companyOf(_foraging?: Foraging, _species?: string): Company {
  const S = MALLARD
  return { radius: S.flock.radius, retargetSeconds: S.flock.retargetSeconds, spacing: S.spacing }
```

with

```ts
/** How a swimmer of this style and species keeps company, for the spawner to start it among its flock. */
export function companyOf(_foraging?: Foraging, species?: string): Company {
  const S = dabblerFor(species)
  return { radius: S.flock.radius, retargetSeconds: S.flock.retargetSeconds, spacing: S.spacing }
```

### Task 9: `shoveler`, with the pair spin

**Files:**
- Create: `src/behavior/shoveler.ts`, `src/behavior/shoveler.test.ts`
- Modify: `src/behavior/types.ts`, `src/behavior/swimmer.ts`, `src/behavior/swim.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the shared body and `pickByShare` (Task 7); `wrap`, `turnToward`, `ease` (Task 7 / existing).
- Produces:

```ts
// src/behavior/types.ts
export interface FlockGroup { targetX; targetZ; retargetAt; spin?: Spin }
export interface Spin {
  readonly pair: readonly [Neighbour, Neighbour]   // the first advances the turn, the second takes its place opposite
  readonly cx: number; readonly cz: number        // the midpoint they turn about
  readonly apart: number                          // metres between their centres
  readonly dir: 1 | -1                            // counter-clockwise seen from above, or clockwise, for the bout
  readonly rate: number                           // radians a second
  angle: number; at: number                       // the first bird's bearing from the midpoint, and its clock then
  ends: number                                    // the first bird's clock when the bout ends (while gathering: gives up)
  spinning: boolean                               // false while the two swim to their places
}
export type Neighbour = Pick<BehaviorController, 'x' | 'z' | 'heading' | 'state' | 'spacing' | 'group'>

// src/behavior/swimmer.ts: body.partner, a neighbour it may come as close to as it likes (its spin partner)

// src/behavior/shoveler.ts
export interface ShovelerSettings extends SwimSettings { cruising; loaf; preen; alert; skim; circle; tipUp }
export const SHOVELER: ShovelerSettings
export function createShoveler(ctx, x, z, S = SHOVELER): BehaviorController
// states: 'cruise' | 'loaf' | 'preen' | 'alert' | 'skim' | 'circle' | 'tipUp'

// src/behavior/swim.ts: 'shoveler' dispatches to createShoveler; companyOf('shoveler') reads SHOVELER
```

How the pair spin works:
- **Asking.** A shoveler that picks `circle` asks the nearest flockmate within 3 m that is skimming, loafing,
  preening, or looking about. There must be room: the circle in the zone, and no other swimmer inside what it keeps
  from the circle. It writes the spin record into their flock's group.
- **Joining.** The partner sees itself named in `group.spin` on its next update and joins, whatever it was doing.
- **Gathering.** Each exempts the other from spacing (`body.partner`) and swims to its place, the two 0.25 m apart
  about their midpoint. Each turns on its place to face along the circle; the first starts the turn once both are
  there.
- **Turning as one.** The first bird advances the shared angle. The second takes the angle forward to its own clock,
  so the pair turns as one whichever of them updates first in a frame.
- **Ending.** When the bout is up, or if the pair can't gather within 12 s, the first bird clears the record and
  both go back to pottering side by side.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

Create `src/behavior/shoveler.test.ts`:

```ts
import { createShoveler, SHOVELER, type ShovelerSettings } from './shoveler'
import { createSwim, companyOf } from './swim'
import { startSpot } from './swimmer'
import { defaultPose, type PoseState } from '../birds/model'
import { makeRng } from '../catalog/rng'
import type { BehaviorContext, BehaviorController, FlockGroup, Spin } from './types'

type Pt = [number, number]
const pond = { kind: 'polygon' as const, points: [[0, 0], [20, 0], [20, 12], [0, 12]] as Pt[] }
const ctx = (seed: number, more: Partial<BehaviorContext> = {}): BehaviorContext => ({
  zone: pond, rng: makeRng(seed), wind: () => ({ dirX: 1, dirZ: 0, effective: 0.3 }), ground: () => -2, foraging: 'shoveler', ...more,
})

interface Frame { t: number; states: string[]; xs: number[]; zs: number[]; headings: number[]; reach: number[]; poses: PoseState[]; spin?: Spin & { snapshot: true } }
interface Ring { t: number; size: number }

/** A flock of three shovelers, started as the spawner starts them, recorded every frame. */
function flock(seed: number, seconds: number, fps: number, S: ShovelerSettings = SHOVELER, keepPoses = false) {
  const rng = makeRng(seed), group: FlockGroup = { targetX: 10, targetZ: 6, retargetAt: 60 }
  const birds: BehaviorController[] = [], rings: Ring[] = []
  let t = 0
  for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) {
    const [x, z] = startSpot(pond, rng, group, companyOf('shoveler'), birds)
    birds.push(createShoveler(ctx(seed * 10 + k, { group, neighbours: () => birds, onRipple: (_x, _z, size) => rings.push({ t, size: size ?? 1 }) }), x, z, S))
  }
  const poses = birds.map(() => defaultPose()), frames: Frame[] = []
  for (let i = 0; i < seconds * fps; i++) {
    t = i / fps
    const states = birds.map(b => b.state)
    birds.forEach((b, k) => b.update(1 / fps, poses[k]!))
    frames.push({
      t, states, xs: birds.map(b => b.x), zs: birds.map(b => b.z), headings: birds.map(b => b.heading), reach: poses.map(p => p.reach),
      poses: keepPoses ? poses.map(p => ({ ...p, lean: p.lean.clone() })) : [],
      spin: group.spin ? { ...group.spin, snapshot: true } : undefined,
    })
  }
  return { frames, rings, birds, group }
}

const wrap = (a: number) => Math.atan2(Math.sin(a), Math.cos(a))
const reachOf = (_h: unknown, f: Frame, k: number) => f.reach[k]!

describe('shoveler', () => {
  // four flocks of three for an hour each, at 10 frames a second
  const hours = [1, 2, 3, 4].map(seed => flock(seed, 3600, 10))
  // one flock for ten minutes at 60 frames a second
  const fine = flock(7, 600, 60, SHOVELER, true)

  it('is what swim gives a shoveler', () => {
    const c = createSwim(ctx(1), 5, 5)
    const pose = defaultPose(), states = new Set<string>()
    for (let i = 0; i < 600 * 10; i++) { c.update(0.1, pose); states.add(c.state) }
    expect(states).toContain('skim')
  })
  it('spends its time as the research says: skim 38%, circle 5%, cruise 22%, loaf and preen 34%', () => {
    const all = hours.flatMap(h => h.frames.flatMap(f => f.states))
    const share = (s: string) => all.filter(x => x === s).length / all.length
    const total = SHOVELER.cruising.share + SHOVELER.loaf.share + SHOVELER.preen.share + SHOVELER.alert.share + SHOVELER.skim.share + SHOVELER.circle.share
    expect(Math.abs(share('skim') - SHOVELER.skim.share / total)).toBeLessThan(0.06)
    expect(Math.abs(share('cruise') - SHOVELER.cruising.share / total)).toBeLessThan(0.05)
    expect(Math.abs(share('loaf') + share('preen') - (SHOVELER.loaf.share + SHOVELER.preen.share) / total)).toBeLessThan(0.05)
    // a pair spins only when two are skimming close together, so circling is looser: 2-10% of the time
    expect(share('circle')).toBeGreaterThan(0.02)
    expect(share('circle')).toBeLessThan(0.1)
  })
  it('skims with the bill in the surface film at 20-35 degrees, travelling at 0.32 m/s or pottering nearly on the spot', () => {
    const skims = fine.frames.flatMap(f => f.poses.map((p, k) => ({ p, k, state: f.states[k] }))).filter(x => x.state === 'skim' && x.p.reach > 0.999)
    expect(skims.length).toBeGreaterThan(600)
    for (const { p } of skims) {
      expect((p.reachAngle * 180) / Math.PI).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(20)
      expect((p.reachAngle * 180) / Math.PI).toBeLessThanOrEqual(35)
      expect(p.reachDepth).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(SHOVELER.skim.film - 1e-9)
      expect(p.reachDepth).toBeLessThanOrEqual(SHOVELER.skim.face + 1e-9)
    }
    // a skimming bird never moves faster than the travelling skim, nor sweeps its bill side to side
    const { frames } = fine
    for (let i = 1; i < frames.length; i++) {
      for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) {
        if (frames[i]!.states[k] !== 'skim' || frames[i - 1]!.states[k] !== 'skim') continue
        const v = Math.hypot(frames[i]!.xs[k]! - frames[i - 1]!.xs[k]!, frames[i]!.zs[k]! - frames[i - 1]!.zs[k]!) * 60
        expect(v).toBeLessThanOrEqual(SHOVELER.skim.travel.speed + 0.13)
      }
    }
  })
  it('turns the pair as one: the two centres 0.25 m apart about a fixed midpoint, one direction a bout, once round every 3.4 s', () => {
    let bouts = 0
    for (const h of [...hours, fine]) {
      let cur: Frame[] = []
      const done = (bout: Frame[]) => {
        if (bout.length < 20) return
        bouts++
        const s0 = bout[0]!.spin!
        const [a, c] = s0.pair.map(p => h.birds.indexOf(p as BehaviorController))
        expect(a).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0)
        expect(c).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0)
        let turned = 0
        for (let i = 0; i < bout.length; i++) {
          const f = bout[i]!
          const [ax, az, cx, cz] = [f.xs[a!]!, f.zs[a!]!, f.xs[c!]!, f.zs[c!]!]
          expect(Math.hypot(ax - cx, az - cz)).toBeCloseTo(SHOVELER.circle.apart, 6)
          expect((ax + cx) / 2).toBeCloseTo(s0.cx, 6)
          expect((az + cz) / 2).toBeCloseTo(s0.cz, 6)
          expect(f.states[a!]).toBe('circle')
          expect(f.states[c!]).toBe('circle')
          if (i > 0) {
            const prev = bout[i - 1]!
            const step = wrap(Math.atan2(-(az - s0.cz), ax - s0.cx) - Math.atan2(-(prev.zs[a!]! - s0.cz), prev.xs[a!]! - s0.cx))
            expect(Math.sign(step)).toBe(s0.dir)
            turned += Math.abs(step)
          }
        }
        const seconds = bout.at(-1)!.t - bout[0]!.t
        expect(turned / seconds).toBeCloseTo((2 * Math.PI) / SHOVELER.circle.revolution, 2)
      }
      // from the second frame of each turn: in the first, the second bird is still stepping onto its place
      let turning = 0
      for (const f of h.frames) {
        turning = f.spin?.spinning ? turning + 1 : 0
        if (turning > 1) cur.push(f)
        else if (cur.length) { done(cur); cur = [] }
      }
    }
    expect(bouts).toBeGreaterThan(10)
  })
  it('spins for 4-8 s a bout, now and then a minute or two', () => {
    const lengths: number[] = []
    for (const h of hours) {
      let start = -1
      for (const f of h.frames) {
        if (f.spin?.spinning && start < 0) start = f.t
        if (!f.spin?.spinning && start >= 0) { lengths.push(f.t - start); start = -1 }
      }
    }
    expect(lengths.length).toBeGreaterThan(10)
    // the bout's clock starts when the pair is asked, so the turning part is a little shorter than the drawn length
    for (const d of lengths) expect((d >= 1 && d <= 8.1) || (d >= 50 && d <= 120.1), `${d} s`).toBe(true)
  })
  it('points both birds along the circle once turning, nose to tail', () => {
    const { frames, birds } = fine
    let checked = 0, turning = 0
    for (const f of frames) {
      const s = f.spin
      turning = s?.spinning ? turning + 1 : 0
      // a second into the turn, each heading lies along the circle in the turning direction
      if (!s || turning <= 60) continue
      const [a, c] = s.pair.map(p => birds.indexOf(p as BehaviorController))
      for (const k of [a!, c!]) {
        const bearing = Math.atan2(-(f.zs[k]! - s.cz), f.xs[k]! - s.cx)
        expect(Math.abs(wrap(f.headings[k]! - (bearing + (s.dir * Math.PI) / 2)))).toBeLessThan(0.02)
      }
      expect(Math.abs(wrap(f.headings[a!]! - f.headings[c!]! - Math.PI))).toBeLessThan(0.02)
      checked++
    }
    expect(checked).toBeGreaterThan(60)
  })
  /**
   * Bird k's runs of consecutive frames where `test` holds, and whether each ended with the bird still in `state` (the
   * state it began the next frame in: a frame is recorded under the state it began in)
   */
  function spells(frames: Frame[], k: number, test: (f: Frame) => boolean, state: string) {
    const out: { from: number; to: number; within: boolean }[] = []
    let from = -1
    frames.forEach((f, i) => {
      if (test(f)) { if (from < 0) from = f.t }
      else if (from >= 0) { out.push({ from, to: f.t, within: frames[i + 1]?.states[k] === state }); from = -1 }
    })
    return out
  }
  it("skims in bouts: the travelling head down 30-60 s, the potterer's face down 0.75-2.75 s and up 0.25-1 s", () => {
    const q = 0.15
    const down: number[] = [], up: number[] = []
    for (const h of hours) {
      for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) {
        const skimming = (f: Frame) => f.states[k] === 'skim'
        // the bill down (reach past half-way) and up, inside skimming, for spells the skim itself ended
        for (const r of spells(h.frames, k, f => skimming(f) && reachOf(h, f, k) > 0.5, 'skim')) if (r.within) down.push(r.to - r.from)
        for (const r of spells(h.frames, k, f => skimming(f) && reachOf(h, f, k) <= 0.5, 'skim')) if (r.within) up.push(r.to - r.from)
      }
    }
    expect(down.length).toBeGreaterThan(100)
    for (const d of down) expect((d >= 0.75 - q && d <= 2.75 + q) || (d >= 30 - q && d <= 60 + q), `down ${d} s`).toBe(true)
    for (const d of up) expect((d >= 0.25 - q && d <= 1 + q) || Math.abs(d - SHOVELER.skim.travel.up) <= q, `up ${d} s`).toBe(true)
    expect(down.filter(d => d >= 30).length).toBeGreaterThan(5)
  })
  it('wanders its heading a few degrees as it skims, never sweeping more than 8 degrees', () => {
    // A bird on its own, so no neighbour turns it aside: under way, it swims the way its body faces, and the heading it
    // shows is that plus the wander.
    const c = createShoveler(ctx(12), 10, 6)
    const pose = defaultPose()
    let most = 0, px = c.x, pz = c.z, n = 0
    for (let i = 0; i < 1200 * 60; i++) {
      c.update(1 / 60, pose)
      const dx = c.x - px, dz = c.z - pz
      if (c.state === 'skim' && pose.reach > 0.999 && Math.hypot(dx, dz) * 60 > 0.25) {
        most = Math.max(most, Math.abs(wrap(c.heading - Math.atan2(-dz, dx))))
        n++
      }
      ;[px, pz] = [c.x, c.z]
    }
    expect(n).toBeGreaterThan(1000)
    expect((most * 180) / Math.PI).toBeGreaterThan(5)
    expect((most * 180) / Math.PI).toBeLessThan(8)
  })
  it('tips up rarely, about once in 10 minutes of feeding, 1-2.5 s at no more than 60 degrees', () => {
    let tips = 0, feeding = 0
    for (const h of hours) {
      feeding += h.frames.reduce((n, f) => n + f.states.filter(x => x === 'skim' || x === 'circle').length, 0) / 10
      for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) tips += spells(h.frames, k, f => f.states[k] === 'tipUp', 'x').length
    }
    // Poisson: 1 per 600 s of feeding, within about two and a half of its standard deviations
    const expected = feeding / 600
    expect(Math.abs(tips - expected)).toBeLessThan(2.5 * Math.sqrt(expected))
    const full = fine.frames.flatMap(f => f.poses.filter(p => p.paddle > 0.999))
    for (const p of full) {
      expect((p.lean.x * 180) / Math.PI).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(50)
      expect((p.lean.x * 180) / Math.PI).toBeLessThanOrEqual(60)
    }
  })
  it('moves smoothly: at 60 fps no part of the pose jumps in a frame', () => {
    const most: [string, (p: PoseState) => number, number][] = [
      ['lean', p => p.lean.x, 0.1], ['reach', p => p.reach, 0.15], ['depth', p => p.reachDepth, 0.005],
      ['paddle', p => p.paddle, 0.1], ['neck', p => p.neckExtend, 0.1], ['tail', p => p.tailTilt, 0.05],
    ]
    const { frames } = fine
    for (let i = 1; i < frames.length; i++) {
      for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) {
        for (const [name, get, limit] of most) {
          const d = Math.abs(get(frames[i]!.poses[k]!) - get(frames[i - 1]!.poses[k]!))
          if (d > limit) expect.fail(`bird ${k} ${name} changed by ${d.toFixed(4)} at ${frames[i]!.t.toFixed(2)} s (${frames[i]!.states[k]})`)
        }
      }
    }
  })
  it('rings the water: a skimming bill every 1.5 s at most, the pair once a revolution, a tip-up at each end', () => {
    const sizes = new Set(hours.flatMap(h => h.rings.map(r => r.size)))
    expect(sizes).toContain(SHOVELER.skim.ring.size)
    expect(sizes).toContain(SHOVELER.circle.ring)
    expect(sizes).toContain(SHOVELER.tipUp.ring)
    expect([...sizes].every(x => x <= 1)).toBe(true)
  })

})
```

```ts
/** A flock's shared record: its target, and when the target next moves on. Flockmates hold the same object. */
export interface FlockGroup {
  targetX: number; targetZ: number; retargetAt: number
  /** two shovelers of the flock spinning together, if any are */
  spin?: Spin
}

/**
 * Two flockmates turning about their midpoint, nose to tail, `apart` metres between their centres. Both birds read it,
 * so the pair turns as one: the first advances the turn, and the second takes its place opposite.
 */
export interface Spin {
  readonly pair: readonly [Neighbour, Neighbour]
  /** the midpoint they turn about */
  readonly cx: number
  readonly cz: number
  readonly apart: number
  /** +1 counter-clockwise seen from above, -1 clockwise, for the whole bout */
  readonly dir: 1 | -1
  /** radians a second */
  readonly rate: number
  /** the first bird's bearing from the midpoint (0 = +x, counter-clockwise from above), and its clock when it set it */
  angle: number
  at: number
  /** the first bird's clock when the bout ends, or, while the two gather, when it gives up waiting */
  ends: number
  /** false while the two swim to their places on the circle; true once both are there and turning */
  spinning: boolean
}

/** Another swimmer, as a swimmer sees it: where it is and faces, what it is doing, how close it lets others come, its flock. */

export interface BehaviorContext {
```

Create `src/behavior/shoveler.ts`:

```ts
import type { BehaviorContext, BehaviorController, Spin } from './types'
import { approach, between, ease, turnToward, wrap } from './types'
import { createSwimmer, pickByShare, mid, type SwimSettings } from './swimmer'
import { pointInPolygon } from '../location/zones'
import type { PoseState } from '../birds/model'

type Range = readonly [number, number]

/** The Northern Shoveler's settings. */
export interface ShovelerSettings extends SwimSettings {
  cruising: { share: number }
  loaf: { share: number; seconds: Range }
  preen: { share: number; seconds: Range }
  alert: { share: number; seconds: Range }
  /**
   * skimming, half of it travelling (head down the whole time, then up for `up` seconds) and half pottering nearly
   * on the spot (the face dipping for `down` seconds and raised for `raised`); the bill held `reachAngle` below level,
   * in the surface film (`film` metres under) or the face under to the eye (`face` metres); the heading wandering
   * `wander` radians either way at `wanderHz`, no side-to-side sweep; a small ring every `ringEvery` seconds
   */
  skim: {
    share: number; travelling: number
    travel: { speed: number; seconds: Range; up: number }
    potter: { speed: number; seconds: Range; down: Range; raised: Range }
    reachAngle: number; film: number; face: number; ease: number
    wander: number; wanderHz: number
    ring: { every: number; size: number; ahead: number }
  }
  /**
   * a pair spinning nose to tail about their midpoint, `apart` metres between centres, once round every `revolution`
   * seconds, one direction a bout; a bout usually `seconds`, now and then `long`; a partner must be a flockmate within
   * `within` metres, feeding or resting; the pair swims `gather` m/s to its places first, and gives up after `wait` s
   */
  circle: { share: number; apart: number; revolution: number; seconds: Range; long: Range; longChance: number; within: number; gather: number; wait: number; ring: number }
  /** rare tip-ups while feeding: `perSecond` of feeding, `seconds` long, only `lean` radians nose-down */
  tipUp: { perSecond: number; seconds: Range; lean: number; tail: number; strokes: number; down: number; up: number; ring: number }
}

/**
 * From docs/research/2026-09-25-northern-shoveler.md, section 11 (section 7.3 for the video it rests on). Every number
 * is an estimate from video of a few birds, mostly in winter and spring, except the time budget, which is Webb &
 * Brotherson 1988's wintering drakes with feeding raised for a morning.
 */
export const SHOVELER: ShovelerSettings = {
  // cruising 0.45 m/s (0.36-0.6), as the old swim; bouts as the dabbler's
  cruise: { speed: 0.45, seconds: [4, 14] },
  turn: 1.2,
  // three birds within about 3 m: radius 1.5
  flock: { radius: 1.5, retargetSeconds: 60, drift: 3, whenFarther: 3 },
  // feeding 0.2-0.35 m centre to centre, the spinning pair 0.25; birds of other flocks as the dabbler's
  spacing: { flock: 0.25, other: 0.8 },
  // the full wake only above a travelling skim's 0.32 m/s: skimming leaves its own smaller ring
  wake: { every: 0.9, size: 1, above: 0.35 },
  // skim 38%, circle 5%, cruise 22%, loaf 34%; the loaf split between resting and preening as Webb & Brotherson's
  // 21.9% and 16.9%, and a little of it alert (scans of 1-2 s, Guillemain et al. 2001, in mallard section 11)
  cruising: { share: 0.22 },
  loaf: { share: 0.18, seconds: [10, 60] },
  preen: { share: 0.14, seconds: [5, 20] },
  alert: { share: 0.02, seconds: [1, 2] },
  skim: {
    share: 0.38, travelling: 0.5,
    // 0.32 m/s (0.28-0.37), the head down 30-60 s, then up about 2 s
    travel: { speed: 0.32, seconds: [30, 60], up: 2 },
    // 0.03 m/s (0.01-0.12), the face down 0.75-2.75 s and up 0.25-1.0 s; a bout of 20-60 s is ours
    potter: { speed: 0.03, seconds: [20, 60], down: [0.75, 2.75], raised: [0.25, 1] },
    // the bill 20-35 degrees to the surface, typically 25-30 (27 here); the travelling bird's bill in the surface film
    // (0.008 m, the contract's 0.005-0.01), the potterer's face under to the eye (the eye is 3-4 cm up in a skim)
    reachAngle: 0.47, film: 0.008, face: 0.035, ease: 0.2,
    // a yaw wander of 5-8 degrees either way (6.5 here) at 0.3-0.6 Hz (0.45)
    wander: 0.113, wanderHz: 0.45,
    // the ring a skimming bill trails is ours: half a wake, every 1.5 s
    ring: { every: 1.5, size: 0.5, ahead: 0.15 },
  },
  // two birds 0.25 m apart, 3.4 s a revolution (3.0-4.75), bouts of 4-8 s and now and then a minute or two (1 in 10
  // here is ours); a partner anywhere in the flock's 3 m, gathering at 0.15 m/s and giving up after 12 s, are ours
  circle: { share: 0.05, apart: 0.25, revolution: 3.4, seconds: [4, 8], long: [60, 120], longChance: 0.1, within: 3, gather: 0.15, wait: 12, ring: 0.6 },
  // about one per 10 minutes of feeding, 1-2.5 s, 50-60 degrees nose-down (55 here); the feet and tilt times as the
  // dabbler's
  tipUp: { perSecond: 1 / 600, seconds: [1, 2.5], lean: 0.96, tail: 0.4, strokes: 2.4, down: 0.35, up: 0.25, ring: 0.6 },
}

type State = 'cruise' | 'loaf' | 'preen' | 'alert' | 'skim' | 'circle' | 'tipUp'
const NECK: Record<State, number> = { cruise: 0.3, loaf: 0.15, preen: 0.3, alert: 1, skim: 1, circle: 1, tipUp: 1 }
/** what a flockmate may be doing when asked to spin: feeding or resting, not under way or already spinning */
const FREE: readonly string[] = ['skim', 'loaf', 'preen', 'alert']
const step = (v: number, want: number, by: number) => (v < want ? Math.min(want, v + by) : Math.max(want, v - by))

/**
 * The `shoveler` style: skim with the bill in the water (travelling, or pottering with the face dipping), spin in a
 * pair, now and then tip up a little way, cruise, loaf, preen, and look about.
 */
export function createShoveler(ctx: BehaviorContext, startX: number, startZ: number, S: ShovelerSettings = SHOVELER): BehaviorController {
  const b = createSwimmer(ctx, startX, startZ, S)
  const group = b.flock.group
  let state: State = 'loaf'
  let timer = 0
  let clock = 0
  let following = false
  let travelling = false
  /** pottering: the face is down; travelling: the head is down (not yet in its closing look up) */
  let down = false
  let [tx, tz] = [b.x, b.z]
  let bill = 0, face = 0, tilt = 0, skimming = 0, preen = 0
  let sinceWake = 0, sinceRing = 0, paddlePhase = 0, lastRev = 0
  /** how long a pottering bout has left to run */
  let skimLeft = 0

  const TABLE = {
    cruise: { share: S.cruising.share, mean: mid(S.cruise.seconds) },
    loaf: { share: S.loaf.share, mean: mid(S.loaf.seconds) },
    preen: { share: S.preen.share, mean: mid(S.preen.seconds) },
    alert: { share: S.alert.share, mean: mid(S.alert.seconds) },
    skim: { share: S.skim.share, mean: S.skim.travelling * (mid(S.skim.travel.seconds) + S.skim.travel.up) + (1 - S.skim.travelling) * mid(S.skim.potter.seconds) },
    // each spin gives two birds their circling, so a bird starts one at half the share
    circle: { share: S.circle.share / 2, mean: (1 - S.circle.longChance) * mid(S.circle.seconds) + S.circle.longChance * mid(S.circle.long) },
  }
  const ring = (size: number, ahead: number) => ctx.onRipple?.(b.x + Math.cos(b.heading) * ahead, b.z - Math.sin(b.heading) * ahead, size)

  function next(): void {
    following = b.flock.distance(b.x, b.z) > S.flock.whenFarther
    if (following) {
      state = 'cruise'
      ;[tx, tz] = b.spot(clock)
      timer = Math.hypot(tx - b.x, tz - b.z) / S.cruise.speed + 10
      return
    }
    state = pickByShare(ctx.rng, TABLE)
    if (state === 'circle' && !invite()) state = 'skim'
    if (state === 'skim') skim(ctx.rng() < S.skim.travelling)
    else if (state !== 'circle') {
      timer = between(ctx.rng, state === 'cruise' ? S.cruise.seconds : S[state].seconds)
      if (state === 'cruise') [tx, tz] = b.spot(clock)
    }
  }
  function skim(travel: boolean): void {
    state = 'skim'
    travelling = travel
    down = true
    timer = travel ? between(ctx.rng, S.skim.travel.seconds) : between(ctx.rng, S.skim.potter.down)
    ;[tx, tz] = b.spot(clock)
    skimLeft = travel ? 0 : between(ctx.rng, S.skim.potter.seconds)
  }

  /** Start a spin with the nearest flockmate that is feeding or resting close by, if the circle has room; true if one starts. */
  function invite(): boolean {
    if (group.spin) return false
    const partner = b.others()
      .filter(n => n.group === group && FREE.includes(n.state) && Math.hypot(n.x - b.x, n.z - b.z) <= S.circle.within)
      .sort((p, q) => Math.hypot(p.x - b.x, p.z - b.z) - Math.hypot(q.x - b.x, q.z - b.z))[0]
    if (!partner) return false
    const [cx, cz] = [(b.x + partner.x) / 2, (b.z + partner.z) / 2]
    const r = S.circle.apart / 2
    // room to turn: the circle in the zone, and no other swimmer inside what it keeps from either bird
    for (let k = 0; k < 8; k++) if (!pointInPolygon(cx + Math.cos((k * Math.PI) / 4) * (r + 0.05), cz + Math.sin((k * Math.PI) / 4) * (r + 0.05), b.zone.points)) return false
    if (!b.others().every(n => n === partner || Math.hypot(n.x - cx, n.z - cz) >= b.spacingTo(n) + r + 0.1)) return false
    bout = between(ctx.rng, ctx.rng() < S.circle.longChance ? S.circle.long : S.circle.seconds)
    group.spin = {
      pair: [self, partner], cx, cz, apart: S.circle.apart, dir: ctx.rng() < 0.5 ? 1 : -1, rate: (2 * Math.PI) / S.circle.revolution,
      angle: Math.atan2(-(b.z - cz), b.x - cx), at: clock, ends: clock + S.circle.wait, spinning: false,
    }
    state = 'circle'
    b.partner = partner
    lastRev = 0
    return true
  }
  /** the first bird's drawn length for the spin it has asked for */
  let bout = 0
  /** where this bird of the pair belongs on the circle now: its point, and the heading along it */
  function place(s: Spin): [number, number, number] {
    const first = s.pair[0] === self
    // the second bird takes the first's angle forward to its own clock, so the pair turns as one whichever updates first
    const a = s.angle + (s.spinning ? s.dir * s.rate * (clock - s.at) : 0) * (first ? 0 : 1) + (first ? 0 : Math.PI)
    const r = s.apart / 2
    return [s.cx + Math.cos(a) * r, s.cz - Math.sin(a) * r, a + (s.dir * Math.PI) / 2]
  }
  /** One frame of a spin, for either bird: swim to its place, then turn with the other; false once the bout is over. */
  function spin(s: Spin, dt: number): boolean {
    const first = s.pair[0] === self
    if (first && s.spinning) {
      s.angle += s.dir * s.rate * dt
      s.at = clock
      if (Math.floor(Math.abs(s.angle) / (2 * Math.PI)) !== lastRev) { lastRev = Math.floor(Math.abs(s.angle) / (2 * Math.PI)); ring(S.circle.ring, 0) }
    }
    const [px, pz, along] = place(s)
    if (!s.spinning) {
      // swim to its place, then turn on it to face along the circle
      if (Math.hypot(px - b.x, pz - b.z) >= 0.01) b.swimTo(px, pz, S.circle.gather, dt)
      else b.swim(dt, 0, along)
      if (first) {
        const [ox, oz] = [s.cx * 2 - px, s.cz * 2 - pz]
        const other = s.pair[1]
        const facing = (h: number) => Math.abs(wrap(h - along)) < 0.05
        const there = Math.hypot(px - b.x, pz - b.z) < 0.01 && Math.hypot(ox - other.x, oz - other.z) < 0.01 && facing(b.heading) && Math.abs(wrap(other.heading - along - Math.PI)) < 0.05
        if (there) { s.spinning = true; s.at = clock; s.ends = clock + bout; ring(S.circle.ring, 0) }
        // a pair that can't get to their places in time: no spin after all
        else if (clock > s.ends) return false
      }
      return true
    }
    b.x = px
    b.z = pz
    b.speed = (s.rate * s.apart) / 2
    // the heading comes round to the circle's tangent, then turns with it
    b.heading = turnToward(b.heading, along, (s.rate + S.turn) * dt)
    return !(first && clock >= s.ends)
  }

  const self: BehaviorController = {
    name: 'swim',
    get state() { return state },
    get x() { return b.x }, get y() { return 0 }, get z() { return b.z },
    // a skimming bird's head wanders a few degrees either way as it paddles
    get heading() { return b.heading + ease(skimming) * S.skim.wander * Math.sin(2 * Math.PI * S.skim.wanderHz * clock) },
    spacing: S.spacing.other,
    group,
    update(dt: number, pose: PoseState) {
      clock += dt
      timer -= dt
      sinceRing += dt
      // a flockmate that has asked this bird to spin: it leaves whatever it was doing and joins
      if (group.spin && group.spin.pair[1] === self && state !== 'circle') { state = 'circle'; b.partner = group.spin.pair[0] }
      if (state === 'circle') {
        const s = group.spin
        if (!s || !spin(s, dt)) {
          if (s && s.pair[0] === self) group.spin = undefined
          // after a spin the pair feeds side by side, pottering, and keeps its distance again
          b.partner = null
          skim(false)
        }
      } else if (state === 'cruise') {
        if (b.swimTo(tx, tz, S.cruise.speed, dt) < 0.05) {
          if (following || timer < 1) next()
          else [tx, tz] = b.spot(clock)
        } else if (timer <= 0) next()
      } else if (state === 'skim') {
        const speed = travelling ? S.skim.travel.speed : S.skim.potter.speed
        if (b.swimTo(tx, tz, down || !travelling ? speed : 0, dt) < 0.05) [tx, tz] = b.spot(clock)
        if (travelling && down && sinceRing >= S.skim.ring.every && b.speed > speed / 2) { sinceRing = 0; ring(S.skim.ring.size, S.skim.ring.ahead) }
        skimLeft -= dt
        if (timer <= 0) {
          if (travelling) {
            if (down) { down = false; timer = S.skim.travel.up }
            else next()
          } else if (down) { down = false; timer = between(ctx.rng, S.skim.potter.raised) }
          else if (skimLeft <= 0) next()
          else { down = true; timer = between(ctx.rng, S.skim.potter.down); ring(S.skim.ring.size * 0.6, S.skim.ring.ahead) }
        } else if (ctx.rng() < S.tipUp.perSecond * dt) {
          state = 'tipUp'
          timer = between(ctx.rng, S.tipUp.seconds) + (S.tipUp.down - S.tipUp.up) / 2
          ring(S.tipUp.ring, 0)
        }
      } else if (state === 'tipUp') {
        b.float(dt)
        if (timer <= 0) { ring(S.tipUp.ring, 0); skim(false) }
      } else {
        b.float(dt)
        if (timer <= 0) next()
      }
      sinceWake += dt
      if (b.speed > S.wake.above && sinceWake >= S.wake.every) { sinceWake = 0; ctx.onRipple?.(b.x, b.z, S.wake.size) }

      // posture, each part eased at a steady rate and shaped with smoothstep
      const feeding = state === 'skim' || state === 'circle'
      const wantBill = feeding && (down || state === 'circle') ? 1 : 0
      const wantFace = (state === 'skim' && !travelling && down) || state === 'circle' ? 1 : 0
      const wantTilt = state === 'tipUp' ? 1 : 0
      bill = step(bill, wantBill, dt / S.skim.ease)
      face = step(face, wantFace, dt / S.skim.ease)
      tilt = step(tilt, wantTilt, dt / (wantTilt > tilt ? S.tipUp.down : S.tipUp.up))
      skimming = step(skimming, state === 'skim' ? 1 : 0, dt)
      const kBill = ease(bill), kFace = ease(face), kTilt = ease(tilt)
      pose.reach = kBill * (1 - kTilt)
      pose.reachDepth = S.skim.film + (S.skim.face - S.skim.film) * kFace
      pose.reachAngle = S.skim.reachAngle
      pose.lean.x = S.tipUp.lean * kTilt
      pose.tailTilt = S.tipUp.tail * kTilt
      pose.paddle = kTilt
      paddlePhase = (paddlePhase + S.tipUp.strokes * dt) % 1
      pose.paddlePhase = paddlePhase
      preen = approach(preen, state === 'preen' ? 1 : 0, 2, dt)
      pose.lean.z = preen * 0.1 * Math.sin(clock * Math.PI * 0.8)
      pose.neckExtend = approach(pose.neckExtend, NECK[state], 3, dt)
      pose.headBob = approach(pose.headBob, 0, 4, dt)
    },
  }
  b.self = self
  next()
  return self
}
```

### Task 10: `coot`

**Files:**
- Create: `src/behavior/coot.ts`, `src/behavior/coot.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the shared body and `pickByShare` (Task 7).
- Produces:

```ts
// src/behavior/coot.ts
export interface CootSettings extends SwimSettings { travel; loaf; preen; pause; alert; pump; peck; headDip; tipUp; dive }
export const COOT: CootSettings
/** the hold-then-thrust head pump at phase f: 1 falling steadily to -1 over the hold, then back to 1 along smoothstep */
export function pumpWave(f: number, hold: number): number
export function createCoot(ctx, x, z, S = COOT): BehaviorController
// states: 'travel' | 'cruise' | 'peck' | 'headDip' | 'tipUp' | 'dive' | 'loaf' | 'preen' | 'alert'
// 'cruise' is the feeding swim between peck bouts (0.2 m/s), 'travel' crossing the zone (0.45 m/s), 'alert' looking up
// between peck bouts with a flick of the tail
// y: 0 afloat, down to -COOT.dive.depth (-0.4) all the way under

// src/behavior/swim.ts: 'coot' dispatches to createCoot; companyOf('coot') reads COOT
```

How a coot feeds and dives:
- **Feeding cycle.** A peck bout (2–8 s at 2.5 pecks a second), one time in 3.5 a head-dip, then a pause of 1–4 s:
  swimming to feed (`cruise`) three times in four, looking up (`alert`) otherwise.
- **The head pump** runs only while it swims. `headBob` is half the pump's end-to-end travel times `pumpWave`: held
  still against the water while the body moves on for the first half of each pump, then thrust forward. It grows with
  the speed up to the feeding swim's 0.2 m/s.
- **Dives come in bouts.** A bout starts about once in 7.5 minutes; its first dive comes when the bird next chooses
  what to do, and each later one 10–25 s after the last surfacing.
- **A dive:** the head down 0.2–0.3 s; a lunge that arcs the body over with the feet out, under in 0.5 s; 1.3–5.5 s
  under (mean 2.5), swimming evenly to where it will come up, 0–1 m away; halfway, it moves that spot if another bird
  is floating on it; up back first in 0.3 s; then 2–5 s eating what it brought up.

Create `src/behavior/coot.test.ts`:

```ts
import { createCoot, COOT, pumpWave, type CootSettings } from './coot'
import { createSwim, companyOf } from './swim'
import { startSpot, AVOID, DRIFT } from './swimmer'
import { defaultPose } from '../birds/model'
import { makeRng } from '../catalog/rng'
import { pointInPolygon } from '../location/zones'
import type { BehaviorContext, BehaviorController, FlockGroup } from './types'

type Pt = [number, number]
const pond = { kind: 'polygon' as const, points: [[0, 0], [20, 0], [20, 12], [0, 12]] as Pt[] }
const WIND = { dirX: 1, dirZ: 0, effective: 0.3 }
const ctx = (seed: number, more: Partial<BehaviorContext> = {}): BehaviorContext => ({
  zone: pond, rng: makeRng(seed), wind: () => WIND, ground: () => -2, foraging: 'coot', ...more,
})

interface Frame { t: number; state: string; states: string[]; x: number; y: number; z: number; lean: number; tail: number; reach: number; depth: number; paddle: number; bob: number; neck: number }
interface Ring { t: number; size: number }

/** A flock of six coots, started as the spawner starts them; bird 0 recorded every frame. */
function flock(seed: number, seconds: number, fps: number, S: CootSettings = COOT) {
  const rng = makeRng(seed), group: FlockGroup = { targetX: 10, targetZ: 6, retargetAt: 60 }
  const birds: BehaviorController[] = [], rings: Ring[] = []
  let t = 0
  for (let k = 0; k < 6; k++) {
    const [x, z] = startSpot(pond, rng, group, companyOf('coot'), birds)
    birds.push(createCoot(ctx(seed * 10 + k, { group, neighbours: () => birds, onRipple: k === 0 ? (_x, _z, size) => rings.push({ t, size: size ?? 1 }) : undefined }), x, z, S))
  }
  const poses = birds.map(() => defaultPose()), frames: Frame[] = []
  for (let i = 0; i < seconds * fps; i++) {
    t = i / fps
    const states = birds.map(b => b.state), state = states[0]!
    birds.forEach((b, k) => b.update(1 / fps, poses[k]!))
    const [b, p] = [birds[0]!, poses[0]!]
    frames.push({ t, state, states, x: b.x, y: b.y, z: b.z, lean: p.lean.x, tail: p.tailTilt, reach: p.reach, depth: p.reachDepth, paddle: p.paddle, bob: p.headBob, neck: p.neckExtend })
  }
  return { frames, rings, birds }
}

/** Runs of consecutive frames where `test` holds. */
function runs(frames: Frame[], test: (f: Frame, i: number) => boolean) {
  const out: { from: number; to: number; frames: Frame[]; start: number }[] = []
  let cur: Frame[] = [], start = 0
  frames.forEach((f, i) => {
    if (test(f, i)) { if (!cur.length) start = i; cur.push(f) }
    else if (cur.length) { out.push({ from: cur[0]!.t, to: f.t, frames: cur, start }); cur = [] }
  })
  return out
}
const mean = (xs: number[]) => xs.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / xs.length

describe('coot', () => {
  // six coots for an hour, four times, at 10 frames a second; and for the motion itself, one flock for twenty minutes
  // at 60, diving and tipping up far more often than coots do, so that each shows up many times
  const hours = [1, 2, 3, 4].map(seed => flock(seed, 3600, 10))
  const busy: CootSettings = { ...COOT, dive: { ...COOT.dive, boutsPerSecond: 1 / 60 }, tipUp: { ...COOT.tipUp, perSecond: 1 / 30 } }
  const fine = flock(9, 1200, 60, busy)
  const under = (f: Frame) => f.y <= -COOT.dive.depth + 1e-9

  it('is what swim gives a coot', () => {
    const c = createSwim(ctx(1), 5, 5), pose = defaultPose(), states = new Set<string>()
    for (let i = 0; i < 600 * 10; i++) { c.update(0.1, pose); states.add(c.state) }
    expect(states).toContain('peck')
  })
  it('spends its time as the research says: pecking 40%, swimming to feed 15%, travelling 15%, loafing 10%, preening 10%', () => {
    // all six birds of each flock, 24 coot-hours
    const all = hours.flatMap(h => h.frames.flatMap(f => f.states))
    const share = (s: string) => all.filter(x => x === s).length / all.length
    for (const [s, w] of [['peck', 0.4], ['cruise', 0.15], ['travel', 0.15], ['loaf', 0.1], ['preen', 0.1]] as const) {
      expect(Math.abs(share(s) - w), s).toBeLessThan(0.04)
    }
    // head-dips, tip-ups, and dives together: a few percent, the research's 5% for dives and tip-ups and 5% for dips
    expect(share('headDip') + share('tipUp') + share('dive')).toBeGreaterThan(0.04)
    expect(share('headDip') + share('tipUp') + share('dive')).toBeLessThan(0.12)
  })
  it('pumps its head 2.2 times a second while it swims: held still against the water, then thrust', () => {
    const swims = runs(fine.frames, (f, i) => f.state === 'cruise' && i > 0 && Math.hypot(f.x - fine.frames[i - 1]!.x, f.z - fine.frames[i - 1]!.z) * 60 > 0.19)
      .filter(r => r.frames.length > 90)
    expect(swims.length).toBeGreaterThan(3)
    let thrusts = 0, falling = 0, frames = 0
    const drops: number[] = []
    for (const r of swims) {
      // clear of the run's ends, where the bird gathers way or slows
      const bob = r.frames.slice(15, -15).map(f => f.bob)
      // thrusts: the head turning from falling to rising, once a pump
      for (let i = 2; i < bob.length; i++) {
        if (bob[i - 1]! < bob[i - 2]! && bob[i]! >= bob[i - 1]!) thrusts++
        if (bob[i]! < bob[i - 1]!) { falling++; drops.push(bob[i - 1]! - bob[i]!) }
        frames++
      }
      expect(Math.max(...bob) - Math.min(...bob)).toBeCloseTo(COOT.pump.amplitude, 2)
    }
    expect(thrusts / (frames / 60)).toBeGreaterThan(COOT.pump.perSecond - 0.15)
    expect(thrusts / (frames / 60)).toBeLessThan(COOT.pump.perSecond + 0.15)
    // The hold is half of each pump (at 60 fps, 13 or 14 of its 27 frames), and in it the head goes back steadily.
    // The pure wave falls in 53% of frames (the frame that wraps from thrust to hold falls too); a bird still
    // gathering way or turning shrinks the pump a little, and a few more frames of its thrust count as falling.
    expect(falling / frames).toBeGreaterThan(0.45)
    expect(falling / frames).toBeLessThan(0.62)
    // held still against the water, the head goes back at the body's own steady pace: the drop from frame to frame
    // barely varies (a sine's would run from nothing to its most)
    drops.sort((a, b) => a - b)
    expect(drops[Math.floor(drops.length * 0.2)]! / drops[Math.floor(drops.length * 0.8)]!).toBeGreaterThan(0.8)
  })
  it('keeps its head still when it is not swimming', () => {
    for (const r of runs(fine.frames, f => f.state === 'loaf' || f.state === 'preen').filter(r => r.frames.length > 60)) {
      for (const f of r.frames.slice(30)) expect(Math.abs(f.bob)).toBeLessThan(0.002)
    }
  })
  it('has a pump that holds, then thrusts: falling steadily for the hold, rising smoothly after', () => {
    for (let k = 0; k < 9; k++) expect(pumpWave((k + 1) / 20, 0.5) - pumpWave(k / 20, 0.5)).toBeCloseTo(-0.2, 9)
    expect(pumpWave(0, 0.5)).toBe(1)
    expect(pumpWave(0.5, 0.5)).toBeCloseTo(-1, 9)
    expect(pumpWave(1 - 1e-9, 0.5)).toBeCloseTo(1, 6)
    for (let k = 50; k < 99; k++) expect(pumpWave((k + 1) / 100, 0.5)).toBeGreaterThan(pumpWave(k / 100, 0.5))
  })
  it('pecks at the surface 2.5 times a second in bouts of 2-8 s', () => {
    const bouts = runs(fine.frames, f => f.state === 'peck').filter(r => r.frames.length > 60)
    expect(bouts.length).toBeGreaterThan(5)
    for (const r of bouts) {
      // after the bill comes down, and before the frame that ends the bout (which may start a head-dip)
      const settled = r.frames.slice(20, -1)
      const lifts = settled.filter((f, i) => i > 0 && settled[i - 1]!.reach === 1 && f.reach < 1).length
      expect(lifts / (settled.at(-1)!.t - settled[0]!.t)).toBeCloseTo(COOT.peck.perSecond, 0)
      for (const f of settled) expect(f.depth).toBe(0)
      expect(r.to - r.from).toBeLessThanOrEqual(COOT.peck.seconds[1] + 0.05)
    }
  })
  it('dives for 1.3-5.5 s, 2.5 on average, and comes up within 1 m of where it went down', () => {
    const dives: number[] = [], moved: number[] = []
    for (const h of [...hours, fine]) {
      for (const r of runs(h.frames, under)) {
        if (r.start === 0 || r.to === h.frames.at(-1)!.t) continue
        dives.push(r.to - r.from)
        // from where it went under to where it is back at the surface
        const down = h.frames[r.start - 1]!
        const up = h.frames.slice(r.start).find(f => f.y === 0)!
        moved.push(Math.hypot(up.x - down.x, up.z - down.z))
      }
    }
    expect(dives.length).toBeGreaterThan(50)
    // at 10 frames a second a spell is measured to within 0.15 s
    for (const d of dives) { expect(d).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1.3 - 0.15); expect(d).toBeLessThanOrEqual(5.5 + 0.5) }
    expect(mean(dives)).toBeGreaterThan(2.2)
    expect(mean(dives)).toBeLessThan(2.9)
    for (const m of moved) expect(m).toBeLessThanOrEqual(COOT.dive.move + 1e-9)
    expect(mean(moved)).toBeLessThan(0.5)
  })
  it('dives in bouts, 10-25 s between surfacing and the next dive, a bout every 5-10 minutes', () => {
    let bouts = 0, total = 0
    for (const h of hours) {
      const downs = runs(h.frames, under).map(r => r.from)
      total += downs.length
      for (let i = 1; i < downs.length; i++) {
        const gap = downs[i]! - downs[i - 1]!
        expect(gap).toBeGreaterThan(10)
        if (gap > 120) bouts++
      }
      bouts++
    }
    // six coots' worth of hours would be 6 x 4 x 8 bouts; bird 0 alone had 4 hours: about 32 bouts, give or take
    expect(bouts).toBeGreaterThan(16)
    expect(bouts).toBeLessThan(56)
    expect(total / bouts).toBeGreaterThan(2)
    expect(total / bouts).toBeLessThan(5)
  })
  it('jumps in: the head down 0.2-0.3 s, then a lunge that arcs the body over, feet out, and under in 0.5 s', () => {
    const { frames: f } = fine
    let dives = 0
    for (const r of runs(f, x => x.state === 'dive')) {
      const i = r.start
      const lunge = f.slice(i).findIndex(x => x.y < 0)
      const gone = f.slice(i).findIndex(under)
      if (lunge < 0 || gone < 0) continue
      dives++
      expect((lunge - 1) / 60).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(COOT.dive.dip[0] - 0.02)
      expect((lunge - 1) / 60).toBeLessThanOrEqual(COOT.dive.dip[1] + 0.02)
      expect(Math.max(...f.slice(i, i + lunge).map(x => x.reach))).toBeGreaterThan(0.9)
      expect((gone - lunge + 1) / 60).toBeCloseTo(COOT.dive.lunge, 1)
      const arc = f.slice(i + lunge, i + gone + 1)
      expect(Math.max(...arc.map(x => x.lean))).toBeGreaterThan(1.2)
      expect(Math.max(...arc.map(x => x.paddle))).toBeGreaterThan(0.9)
    }
    expect(dives).toBeGreaterThan(3)
  })
  it('moves smoothly: at 60 fps no part of the pose, nor its depth, jumps in a frame', () => {
    const most = { lean: 0.15, tail: 0.1, reach: 0.15, depth: 0.02, paddle: 0.1, bob: 0.03, neck: 0.1, y: 0.035 }
    const { frames: f } = fine
    for (let i = 1; i < f.length; i++) {
      for (const [k, limit] of Object.entries(most) as [keyof typeof most, number][]) {
        const d = Math.abs(f[i]![k] - f[i - 1]![k])
        if (d > limit) expect.fail(`${k} changed by ${d.toFixed(4)} at ${f[i]!.t.toFixed(2)} s (${f[i]!.state})`)
      }
    }
  })
  it('stays in its zone, and no frame moves it further than its pace, under water or on it', () => {
    const pace = (Math.max(COOT.travel.speed + AVOID, COOT.dive.swim) + DRIFT * WIND.effective) / 60 + 1e-9
    const { frames: f } = fine
    for (let i = 1; i < f.length; i++) {
      if (!pointInPolygon(f[i]!.x, f[i]!.z, pond.points)) expect.fail(`left the zone at ${f[i]!.t}`)
      const moved = Math.hypot(f[i]!.x - f[i - 1]!.x, f[i]!.z - f[i - 1]!.z)
      if (moved > pace) expect.fail(`moved ${moved.toFixed(4)} m at ${f[i]!.t.toFixed(2)} s (${f[i]!.state})`)
    }
  })
  it('rings the water as it dives and surfaces, as it dips its head, and faintly as it swims', () => {
    const sizes = hours.flatMap(h => h.rings.map(r => r.size))
    for (const size of [COOT.dive.ring, COOT.headDip.ring, COOT.pump.ring.size]) expect(sizes).toContain(size)
    const dives = hours.reduce((n, h) => n + runs(h.frames, under).length, 0)
    // one going down and one coming up for each dive (a dive cut by the end of the hour may miss its second)
    expect(sizes.filter(s => s === COOT.dive.ring).length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(2 * dives - 4)
  })
})
```
