- [ ] **Step 3: The coot**

Create `src/behavior/coot.ts`:

```ts
import type { BehaviorContext, BehaviorController } from './types'
import { approach, between, ease } from './types'
import { createSwimmer, pickByShare, mid, type SwimSettings } from './swimmer'
import { pointInPolygon } from '../location/zones'
import type { PoseState } from '../birds/model'

type Range = readonly [number, number]

/** The American Coot's settings. */
export interface CootSettings extends SwimSettings {
  /** swimming across the zone, the head pumping, a wake behind: its share, speed, and bout */
  travel: { share: number; speed: number; seconds: Range }
  loaf: { share: number; seconds: Range }
  preen: { share: number; seconds: Range }
  /**
   * Between peck bouts: a pause of `seconds`, swimming to feed (`cruise.speed`, the head pumping) `swim` of the time,
   * and otherwise looking up (the `alert` state), the tail flicked up `tail` radians as the head comes up, showing
   * the white undertail, and down again over `flick` seconds.
   */
  pause: { seconds: Range; swim: number }
  alert: { tail: number; flick: number }
  /**
   * the head pump while under way: `perSecond` pumps, the head held still against the water for the first `hold` of
   * each, then thrust forward; `amplitude` is the whole fore-and-aft travel in body lengths at `cruise.speed` and over
   */
  pump: { perSecond: number; hold: number; amplitude: number; ring: { every: number; size: number } }
  /** peck bouts: `perSecond` pecks, the bill at the water for the first `bottom` of each and lifted `lift` of the reach between */
  peck: { share: number; seconds: Range; perSecond: number; bottom: number; lift: number }
  /** one peck bout in `every` ends with the head and neck `depth` metres under for `seconds`, the body tipped `lean` */
  headDip: { every: number; seconds: Range; depth: number; lean: number; ring: number }
  /** rare tip-ups while feeding: `perSecond` of pecking or swimming to feed */
  tipUp: { perSecond: number; seconds: Range; lean: number; tail: number; strokes: number; down: number; up: number }
  /**
   * jump-dives, in bouts of `count`, a bout starting `boutsPerSecond`: the head down for `dip` seconds, a lunge that
   * arcs the body to `lean` and takes it `depth` metres under in `lunge` seconds, `under` seconds under (drawn skewed
   * toward the short end, `skew`), `move` metres from where it went down, back up in `rise` seconds back first, then
   * eating on the surface for `eat` seconds; the next dive of the bout `gap` seconds after surfacing. Under water it
   * swims at most `swim` m/s, and stays down a little longer if it must go round to surface clear of another bird.
   */
  dive: {
    boutsPerSecond: number; count: Range; gap: Range
    dip: Range; lunge: number; lean: number; depth: number
    under: Range; skew: number; move: number; swim: number; rise: number; eat: Range; ring: number
  }
}

/**
 * From docs/research/2026-09-25-american-coot.md, section 11 (behaviour in section 7). Sourced: the dive durations
 * (Batulis & Bongiorno 1972, 1.3-3.5 s by depth; video 1.7-5.5 s), the surface intervals between dives (the same, 10-25 s),
 * and the travelling speed (Trenchard 2012, 0.5 m/s). The rest are estimates from video of a few birds, spring and
 * summer, and the time budget from the breeding budget of Ryan & Dinsmore 1979 and a winter snippet (McKnight 1998).
 */
export const COOT: CootSettings = {
  // the feeding swim at 0.2 m/s (0.15-0.3), 1-5 s between peck bouts; `seconds` is unused (a pause's length is `pause`)
  cruise: { speed: 0.2, seconds: [1, 5] },
  turn: 1.2,
  // six birds, nearest neighbours 0.4-0.9 m: a radius of 2 m gives about 0.7 m; spacing 0.4 m, over its 0.30-0.33 m length
  flock: { radius: 2, retargetSeconds: 60, drift: 3, whenFarther: 4 },
  spacing: { flock: 0.4, other: 0.8 },
  wake: { every: 0.9, size: 1, above: 0.3 },
  // feeding 0.65 (pecks 0.40, swimming to feed 0.15, head-dips and dives and tip-ups the rest), travel 0.15, loaf
  // 0.10, preen 0.10; travelling at 0.4-0.5 m/s (0.45 here) for the dabbler's cruise bouts
  travel: { share: 0.15, speed: 0.45, seconds: [4, 14] },
  loaf: { share: 0.1, seconds: [10, 60] },
  preen: { share: 0.1, seconds: [5, 20] },
  // Peck bouts separated by 1-5 s of swimming or looking up: 1-4 s here, swimming three times in four. With the
  // travelling, loafing, and preening and the 5% diving, the feeding cycle has 60% of the bird's time: pecking 5 s of
  // each 7.8 s cycle gives 38% (the budget's 40%), and swimming, 0.60 x 2.5 / 7.8 x 0.75 = 14% (the budget's 15%).
  pause: { seconds: [1, 4], swim: 0.75 },
  // an alarmed bird lifts its tail 30-45 degrees (37.5 here); a looking-up bird only flicks it, as a coot does when it
  // turns (the flick's 0.5 s is ours)
  alert: { tail: 0.65, flick: 0.5 },
  // 2.2 a second (1.8-2.6), hold then thrust, the head still for about half of each; the bill travels about 5 cm fore
  // and aft, 17% of the bill-to-tail length; a faint ring every 1-2 s while swimming (1.5 here)
  pump: { perSecond: 2.2, hold: 0.5, amplitude: 0.17, ring: { every: 1.5, size: 0.3 } },
  // bouts of 2-8 s at 2.5 pecks a second, each held 0.15-0.4 s at the bottom (0.2 s here, half the cycle), the head
  // rising 2-3 cm between: about a third of its 60-95 mm reach from rest to the water
  peck: { share: 0.4, seconds: [2, 8], perSecond: 2.5, bottom: 0.5, lift: 0.3 },
  // about one peck bout in 3-4 ends in a dip (3.5 here) of 0.8-1.5 s, the body 5-10 degrees nose-down (7.5); the head
  // and neck 0.1 m under is ours
  headDip: { every: 3.5, seconds: [0.8, 1.5], depth: 0.1, lean: 0.13, ring: 0.4 },
  // 1-2 s, 45-90 degrees nose-down (67.5 here), the tail up, the feet paddling; about once per bird in 5-10 minutes,
  // which is once in 5 minutes of the 55% of its time it spends pecking or swimming to feed; the paddle rate is the
  // Mallard's, and the tilt times the dabbler's
  tipUp: { perSecond: 1 / 300, seconds: [1, 2], lean: 1.18, tail: 0.5, strokes: 2.4, down: 0.35, up: 0.25 },
  dive: {
    // a bout about once in 5-10 minutes (7.5 here), 2-5 dives 10-25 s apart
    boutsPerSecond: 1 / 450, count: [2, 5], gap: [10, 25],
    // the head down 0.2-0.3 s, then a lunge that arcs the body near vertical (80 degrees here) and has it under in about
    // 0.5 s; 0.4 m down takes the whole bird, feet and tail, under the water (ours)
    dip: [0.2, 0.3], lunge: 0.5, lean: 1.4, depth: 0.4,
    // under 2.5 s on average (1.3-5.5): drawn as 1.3 + 4.2 u^2.5, whose mean is 2.5; moving 0-1 m, mostly under 0.5
    // (u^2 m, three in four under 0.5); up back first in 0.3 s (ours); eating what it brought up for 2-5 s
    under: [1.3, 5.5], skew: 2.5, move: 1, swim: 1, rise: 0.3, eat: [2, 5],
    // a ring of about 0.5-1 m on every dive, going down and coming up
    ring: 0.55,
  },
}

type State = 'travel' | 'cruise' | 'peck' | 'headDip' | 'tipUp' | 'dive' | 'loaf' | 'preen' | 'alert'
type DivePhase = 'dip' | 'lunge' | 'under' | 'rise' | 'eat'
const NECK: Record<State, number> = { travel: 0.6, cruise: 0.6, peck: 0.8, headDip: 1, tipUp: 1, dive: 1, loaf: 0.15, preen: 0.3, alert: 1 }
const step = (v: number, want: number, by: number) => (v < want ? Math.min(want, v + by) : Math.max(want, v - by))

/** The hold-then-thrust head pump at phase f (0..1): held still against the water while the body moves on, then thrust. */
export function pumpWave(f: number, hold: number): number {
  return f < hold ? 1 - (2 * f) / hold : -1 + 2 * ease((f - hold) / (1 - hold))
}

/**
 * The `coot` style: peck at the surface in bouts, between them swim to feed with the head pumping or look up with a
 * flick of the tail, now and then dip the head or tip up, jump-dive in bouts, travel, loaf, and preen.
 */
export function createCoot(ctx: BehaviorContext, startX: number, startZ: number, S: CootSettings = COOT): BehaviorController {
  const b = createSwimmer(ctx, startX, startZ, S)
  let state: State = 'loaf'
  let timer = 0
  let clock = 0
  let following = false
  let [tx, tz] = [b.x, b.z]
  // the head pump's and the peck's cycles, and the paddling feet's
  let pumpPhase = 0, peckPhase = 0, paddlePhase = 0
  // eased posture: the peck (0 head at rest .. 1 pecking), the head under, the tip-up, the dive's arc over and the
  // kick of its feet, and the head pump's reach
  let pecking = 0, under = 0, tilt = 0, arc = 0, kick = 0, pumpK = 0, preen = 0
  let sinceWake = 0, sinceRing = 0
  // the dive: its phase, how deep the bird is (0 afloat .. 1 all the way under), where it went down and comes up
  let phase: DivePhase = 'dip'
  let sink = 0
  let [dx0, dz0, ux, uz] = [0, 0, 0, 0]
  let underFor = 0
  let bout = { left: 0, nextAt: Infinity }

  // a feeding cycle is a peck bout, one time in `every` a head-dip, and a pause; it takes the pecks' share scaled up
  const cycle = mid(S.peck.seconds) + mid(S.headDip.seconds) / S.headDip.every + mid(S.pause.seconds)
  const TABLE = {
    travel: { share: S.travel.share, mean: mid(S.travel.seconds) },
    peck: { share: (S.peck.share * cycle) / mid(S.peck.seconds), mean: cycle },
    loaf: { share: S.loaf.share, mean: mid(S.loaf.seconds) },
    preen: { share: S.preen.share, mean: mid(S.preen.seconds) },
  }
  /** after a peck bout (and its dip): swim to feed, or look up */
  function pause(): void {
    state = ctx.rng() < S.pause.swim ? 'cruise' : 'alert'
    timer = between(ctx.rng, S.pause.seconds)
    looked = 0
    if (state === 'cruise') [tx, tz] = b.spot(clock)
  }
  let looked = 0
  const ring = (size: number, ahead = 0) => ctx.onRipple?.(b.x + Math.cos(b.heading) * ahead, b.z - Math.sin(b.heading) * ahead, size)

  function next(): void {
    following = b.flock.distance(b.x, b.z) > S.flock.whenFarther
    if (following) {
      state = 'travel'
      ;[tx, tz] = b.spot(clock)
      timer = Math.hypot(tx - b.x, tz - b.z) / S.travel.speed + 10
      return
    }
    // a dive bout under way: the next dive once its gap is up, from the surface
    if (bout.left > 0 && clock >= bout.nextAt) { dive(); return }
    state = pickByShare(ctx.rng, TABLE)
    timer = between(ctx.rng, state === 'travel' ? S.travel.seconds : S[state].seconds)
    if (state === 'travel') [tx, tz] = b.spot(clock)
  }
  function dive(): void {
    state = 'dive'
    phase = 'dip'
    timer = between(ctx.rng, S.dive.dip)
    bout.left--
  }
  /** Where it comes up: up to `move` metres from where it went down, about the way it faces, in the zone and clear of the others. */
  function surfacing(): void {
    ;[dx0, dz0] = [b.x, b.z]
    const d = S.dive.move * ctx.rng() ** 2
    const a = b.heading + (ctx.rng() - 0.5) * (Math.PI / 2)
    let len = d
    while (len > 0.01 && !pointInPolygon(b.x + Math.cos(a) * len, b.z - Math.sin(a) * len, b.zone.points)) len /= 2
    if (len <= 0.01) len = 0
    ;[ux, uz] = [b.x + Math.cos(a) * len, b.z - Math.sin(a) * len]
  }
  /** A surfacing spot clear of the other swimmers: the nearest to the planned one within `move` of the dive, if needed. */
  function clearSpot(): void {
    if (b.clearAt(ux, uz)) return
    let best: [number, number] | null = null
    for (let r = 0.25; r <= 1; r += 0.25) {
      for (let k = 0; k < 16; k++) {
        const a = (k * Math.PI) / 8
        const [px, pz] = [dx0 + Math.cos(a) * r * S.dive.move, dz0 - Math.sin(a) * r * S.dive.move]
        if (!pointInPolygon(px, pz, b.zone.points) || !b.clearAt(px, pz)) continue
        if (!best || Math.hypot(px - ux, pz - uz) < Math.hypot(best[0] - ux, best[1] - uz)) best = [px, pz]
      }
    }
    if (best) [ux, uz] = best
  }
  let checked = false

  const self: BehaviorController = {
    name: 'swim',
    get state() { return state },
    get x() { return b.x }, get z() { return b.z }, get heading() { return b.heading },
    // afloat at the waterline; under water, the dive's depth
    get y() { return -S.dive.depth * ease(sink) },
    spacing: S.spacing.other,
    group: b.flock.group,
    update(dt: number, pose: PoseState) {
      clock += dt
      timer -= dt
      sinceRing += dt
      // a dive bout starts now and then, whatever the bird is doing on the surface; its first dive comes when it next
      // chooses what to do
      if (bout.left <= 0 && state !== 'dive' && ctx.rng() < S.dive.boutsPerSecond * dt) bout = { left: Math.floor(between(ctx.rng, [S.dive.count[0], S.dive.count[1] + 1])), nextAt: clock }
      const feeding = state === 'peck' || state === 'cruise'
      if (state === 'dive') {
        if (phase === 'dip' || phase === 'eat') b.float(dt)
        if (phase === 'dip' && timer <= 0) { phase = 'lunge'; timer = S.dive.lunge; ring(S.dive.ring, 0.1) }
        else if (phase === 'lunge') {
          sink = step(sink, 1, dt / S.dive.lunge)
          if (timer <= 0) {
            phase = 'under'
            underFor = S.dive.under[0] + (S.dive.under[1] - S.dive.under[0]) * ctx.rng() ** S.dive.skew
            timer = underFor
            checked = false
            surfacing()
          }
        } else if (phase === 'under') {
          // swimming under water toward where it will come up, evenly over the time it stays down; halfway, it makes
          // sure no one is floating on the spot, and goes round to a clear one if they are
          if (!checked && timer <= underFor / 2) { checked = true; clearSpot() }
          const left = Math.hypot(ux - b.x, uz - b.z)
          const by = Math.min(left, (left / Math.max(timer, dt)) * dt, S.dive.swim * dt)
          if (left > 1e-9) { b.x += ((ux - b.x) / left) * by; b.z += ((uz - b.z) / left) * by }
          if (timer <= 0 && left - by < 1e-9) { phase = 'rise'; timer = S.dive.rise; b.speed = 0 }
        } else if (phase === 'rise') {
          sink = step(sink, 0, dt / S.dive.rise)
          if (timer <= 0) { sink = 0; phase = 'eat'; timer = between(ctx.rng, S.dive.eat); ring(S.dive.ring) }
        } else if (phase === 'eat' && timer <= 0) {
          bout.nextAt = clock + between(ctx.rng, S.dive.gap)
          next()
        }
      } else if (state === 'travel' || state === 'cruise') {
        const speed = state === 'travel' ? S.travel.speed : S.cruise.speed
        if (b.swimTo(tx, tz, speed, dt) < 0.05) {
          if (following || timer < 1) next()
          else [tx, tz] = b.spot(clock)
        } else if (timer <= 0) next()
      } else if (state === 'peck') {
        b.float(dt)
        if (timer <= 0) {
          if (ctx.rng() < 1 / S.headDip.every) { state = 'headDip'; timer = between(ctx.rng, S.headDip.seconds); ring(S.headDip.ring, 0.1) }
          else pause()
        }
      } else if (state === 'headDip') {
        b.float(dt)
        if (timer <= 0) { ring(S.headDip.ring, 0.1); pause() }
      } else if (state === 'tipUp') {
        b.float(dt)
        if (timer <= 0) next()
      } else {
        b.float(dt)
        if (timer <= 0) next()
      }
      // a rare tip-up while feeding
      if (feeding && state !== 'dive' && ctx.rng() < S.tipUp.perSecond * dt) {
        state = 'tipUp'
        timer = between(ctx.rng, S.tipUp.seconds) + (S.tipUp.down - S.tipUp.up) / 2
      }
      sinceWake += dt
      if (b.speed > S.wake.above && sinceWake >= S.wake.every) { sinceWake = 0; ring(S.wake.size) }
      if (b.speed > S.cruise.speed / 2 && sink === 0 && sinceRing >= S.pump.ring.every) { sinceRing = 0; ring(S.pump.ring.size, 0.1) }

      // the head pump, only while it swims: its reach grows with the speed, up to the feeding swim's
      pumpPhase = (pumpPhase + S.pump.perSecond * dt) % 1
      pumpK = step(pumpK, state === 'dive' ? 0 : Math.min(1, b.speed / S.cruise.speed), dt / 0.2)
      pose.headBob = (pumpK * S.pump.amplitude * pumpWave(pumpPhase, S.pump.hold)) / 2
      // the peck: the bill at the water for the first part of each cycle, lifting a little between
      peckPhase += S.peck.perSecond * dt
      const f = peckPhase % 1
      const wave = f < S.peck.bottom ? 1 : 1 - S.peck.lift * Math.sin((Math.PI * (f - S.peck.bottom)) / (1 - S.peck.bottom)) ** 2
      pecking = step(pecking, state === 'peck' ? 1 : 0, dt / 0.3)
      under = step(under, state === 'headDip' || (state === 'dive' && phase === 'dip') ? 1 : 0, dt / 0.25)
      const wantTilt = state === 'tipUp' ? 1 : 0
      tilt = step(tilt, wantTilt, dt / (wantTilt > tilt ? S.tipUp.down : S.tipUp.up))
      const kPeck = ease(pecking), kUnder = ease(under), kTilt = ease(tilt)
      pose.reach = Math.max(kPeck * wave, kUnder)
      pose.reachDepth = kUnder * S.headDip.depth
      pose.reachAngle = Math.PI / 2
      // the lunge arcs the body over as it goes under, feet kicking; under water it levels to come up back first,
      // the nose a fifth of the way down
      const diving = state === 'dive'
      arc = step(arc, diving && phase === 'lunge' ? 1 : diving && (phase === 'under' || phase === 'rise') ? 0.2 : 0, dt / S.dive.lunge)
      kick = step(kick, diving && phase === 'lunge' ? 1 : 0, dt / 0.25)
      pose.lean.x = Math.max(kUnder * S.headDip.lean, kTilt * S.tipUp.lean, ease(arc) * S.dive.lean)
      // looking up, the tail flicks up and down again: a sin^2 over the flick's length
      looked += dt
      const flick = state === 'alert' && looked < S.alert.flick ? Math.sin((Math.PI * looked) / S.alert.flick) ** 2 : 0
      pose.tailTilt = approach(pose.tailTilt, Math.max(flick * S.alert.tail, kTilt * S.tipUp.tail, ease(kick) * S.tipUp.tail), 12, dt)
      // the feet show in a tip-up and as the lunge takes the bird under
      pose.paddle = Math.max(kTilt, ease(kick))
      paddlePhase = (paddlePhase + S.tipUp.strokes * dt) % 1
      pose.paddlePhase = paddlePhase
      preen = approach(preen, state === 'preen' ? 1 : 0, 2, dt)
      pose.lean.z = preen * 0.1 * Math.sin(clock * Math.PI * 0.8)
      pose.neckExtend = approach(pose.neckExtend, NECK[state], 3, dt)
    },
  }
  b.self = self
  next()
  return self
}
```

### Task 11: `grebe`

**Files:**
- Create: `src/behavior/grebe.ts`, `src/behavior/grebe.test.ts`
- Modify: `src/behavior/swimmer.ts` (flockmates may start further apart than their spacing), `src/behavior/swim.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the shared body, `pickByShare`, `startSpot` (Task 7).
- Produces:

```ts
// src/behavior/swimmer.ts
SwimSettings.flock.apart?: number        // flockmates start at least this far apart (unset: their spacing)
Company.apart?: number                   // startSpot uses it between flockmates

// src/behavior/grebe.ts
export interface GrebeSettings extends SwimSettings { bouts; patch; cruising; loaf; peer; start; entry; under; resurface; sink; rings }
export const GREBE: GrebeSettings
/** how far down the grebe goes to back-awash, to watch or to start a sinking dive: the back from +0.028 m to -0.045 m */
export const AWASH = 0.073
/** a gamma draw with this mean and standard deviation (Marsaglia and Tsang), kept to the range */
export function gamma(rng, mean: number, sd: number, range: readonly [number, number]): number
export function createGrebe(ctx, x, z, S = GREBE): BehaviorController
// states: 'cruise' | 'peer' | 'dive' | 'under' | 'resurface' | 'sink' | 'loaf'
// y: 0 afloat, -AWASH at the periscope, down to -GREBE.entry.depth (-0.4) all the way under

// src/behavior/swim.ts: 'grebe' dispatches to createGrebe; companyOf passes the grebe's start spacing (`apart`, 20 m)
```

How a grebe works its patch:
- **Grebes keep apart.** Each grebe of the cast entry is a flockmate with a 10 m spacing. They start at least 20 m
  apart, and each works a patch 6 m either way from where it started; strayed further, it cruises and dives
  homeward.
- **A dive** starts with a roll-under (70%), a splash (15%), or a sink (15%). It stays under a gamma-distributed
  7.6 s (2–15) and travels a gamma-distributed 3.7 m (0–12), about its heading, the whole way if the zone lets it,
  and clear of the other swimmers with a metre to spare. A long way takes the time it takes, at 1.5 m/s.
- **Coming up.** When its time is up, it checks the spot every frame until it is up. If another bird is near, it
  swims on under water to the nearest clear spot, for up to 5 s more, and comes up with a ring.
- **Between dives** it looks about for 2–10 s (mean 4), and peers first before a third of dives.

Create `src/behavior/grebe.test.ts`:

```ts
import { createGrebe, GREBE, AWASH, gamma } from './grebe'
import { createSwim, companyOf } from './swim'
import { startSpot, AVOID, DRIFT } from './swimmer'
import { defaultPose } from '../birds/model'
import { makeRng } from '../catalog/rng'
import { pointInPolygon } from '../location/zones'
import type { BehaviorContext, BehaviorController, FlockGroup } from './types'

type Pt = [number, number]
// the lake's open water, where the grebes swim
const water = { kind: 'polygon' as const, points: [[-22, -15], [26, -15], [32, -52], [-28, -52]] as Pt[] }
const WIND = { dirX: 1, dirZ: 0, effective: 0.3 }
const ctx = (seed: number, more: Partial<BehaviorContext> = {}): BehaviorContext => ({
  zone: water, rng: makeRng(seed), wind: () => WIND, ground: () => -2, foraging: 'grebe', ...more,
})

interface Frame { t: number; states: string[]; xs: number[]; ys: number[]; zs: number[]; lean: number; tail: number; reach: number; paddle: number; neck: number }
interface Ring { t: number; size: number }

/** Three grebes, started as the spawner starts them; every bird's place, and bird 0's pose, every frame. */
function three(seed: number, seconds: number, fps: number) {
  const rng = makeRng(seed), group: FlockGroup = { targetX: 2, targetZ: -33, retargetAt: Infinity }
  const birds: BehaviorController[] = [], rings: Ring[] = []
  let t = 0
  for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) {
    const [x, z] = startSpot(water, rng, group, companyOf('grebe'), birds)
    birds.push(createGrebe(ctx(seed * 10 + k, { group, neighbours: () => birds, onRipple: k === 0 ? (_x, _z, size) => rings.push({ t, size: size ?? 1 }) : undefined }), x, z))
  }
  const poses = birds.map(() => defaultPose()), frames: Frame[] = []
  for (let i = 0; i < seconds * fps; i++) {
    t = i / fps
    const states = birds.map(b => b.state)
    birds.forEach((b, k) => b.update(1 / fps, poses[k]!))
    const p = poses[0]!
    frames.push({ t, states, xs: birds.map(b => b.x), ys: birds.map(b => b.y), zs: birds.map(b => b.z), lean: p.lean.x, tail: p.tailTilt, reach: p.reach, paddle: p.paddle, neck: p.neckExtend })
  }
  return { frames, rings, birds }
}

/** Bird k's runs of consecutive frames where `test` holds, with the frame index each starts at. */
function runs(frames: Frame[], test: (f: Frame, i: number) => boolean) {
  const out: { from: number; to: number; start: number; end: number }[] = []
  let start = -1
  frames.forEach((f, i) => {
    if (test(f, i)) { if (start < 0) start = i }
    else if (start >= 0) { out.push({ from: frames[start]!.t, to: f.t, start, end: i }); start = -1 }
  })
  return out
}
const mean = (xs: number[]) => xs.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / xs.length
const DEPTH = GREBE.entry.depth
const underwater = (k: number) => (f: Frame) => f.ys[k]! <= -DEPTH + 1e-9

describe('grebe', () => {
  // three grebes for an hour, four times, at 10 frames a second; and three for twenty minutes at 60
  const hours = [1, 2, 3, 4].map(seed => three(seed, 3600, 10))
  const fine = three(9, 1200, 60)
  /** every dive by every bird: how long under, how far it went, how it started */
  const dives = hours.flatMap(h => [0, 1, 2].flatMap(k => runs(h.frames, underwater(k)).filter(r => r.start > 0 && r.end < h.frames.length - 1).map(r => {
    // from its first frame all the way under to its last, where it starts up (after that the wind has it again)
    const down = h.frames[r.start]!, up = h.frames[r.end - 1]!
    return { k, h, seconds: r.to - r.from, moved: Math.hypot(up.xs[k]! - down.xs[k]!, up.zs[k]! - down.zs[k]!), start: r.start }
  })))

  it('is what swim gives a grebe', () => {
    const c = createSwim(ctx(1), 2, -33), pose = defaultPose(), states = new Set<string>()
    for (let i = 0; i < 1200 * 10; i++) { c.update(0.1, pose); states.add(c.state) }
    expect(states).toContain('under')
  })
  it('stays under 7.6 s on average (2-15), and comes up 3.7 m away on average (0-12)', () => {
    expect(dives.length).toBeGreaterThan(300)
    // At 10 frames a second a spell is measured to within 0.25 s (it takes in the frames it goes under and starts up).
    // A bird that must swim on to surface clear of another stays down up to `extra` seconds longer.
    for (const d of dives) {
      expect(d.seconds).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(2 - 0.25)
      expect(d.seconds).toBeLessThanOrEqual(15 + GREBE.under.extra + 0.25)
      expect(d.moved).toBeLessThanOrEqual(12 + 1e-6)
    }
    expect(mean(dives.map(d => d.seconds))).toBeGreaterThan(7.0)
    expect(mean(dives.map(d => d.seconds))).toBeLessThan(8.2)
    expect(mean(dives.map(d => d.moved))).toBeGreaterThan(3.1)
    expect(mean(dives.map(d => d.moved))).toBeLessThan(4.3)
  })
  it('draws gamma times with the mean and spread asked for', () => {
    const rng = makeRng(3), xs = Array.from({ length: 20000 }, () => gamma(rng, 7.6, 3.1, [0, Infinity]))
    const m = mean(xs), sd = Math.sqrt(mean(xs.map(x => (x - m) ** 2)))
    expect(m).toBeCloseTo(7.6, 1)
    expect(sd).toBeCloseTo(3.1, 1)
  })
  it('starts its dives 70% rolling under, 15% with a splash, 15% sinking first', () => {
    // told apart by how it goes: a sink holds at back-awash first; a splash goes straight down, level, in a flash; a
    // roll tips the stern up as it goes
    const kinds = { roll: 0, splash: 0, sink: 0 }
    for (const d of dives.filter(d => d.k === 0)) {
      const before = d.h.frames.slice(Math.max(0, d.start - 30), d.start)
      if (before.filter(f => Math.abs(f.ys[0]! + AWASH) < 1e-6).length >= 10) kinds.sink++
      else if (Math.max(...before.map(f => f.lean)) > 0.3) kinds.roll++
      else kinds.splash++
    }
    const n = kinds.roll + kinds.splash + kinds.sink
    expect(n).toBeGreaterThan(100)
    expect(kinds.roll / n).toBeGreaterThan(0.6)
    expect(kinds.roll / n).toBeLessThan(0.8)
    expect(kinds.splash / n).toBeGreaterThan(0.08)
    expect(kinds.splash / n).toBeLessThan(0.22)
    expect(kinds.sink / n).toBeGreaterThan(0.08)
    expect(kinds.sink / n).toBeLessThan(0.25)
  })
  it('rolls under in 0.4-0.65 s, the stern tipped up 20-30 degrees, a kick of the feet; a splash goes down in 0.1 s', () => {
    const { frames: f } = fine
    let rolls = 0, splashes = 0
    for (const r of runs(f, x => x.states[0] === 'dive')) {
      const gone = f.slice(r.start).findIndex(underwater(0))
      if (gone < 0) continue
      const entry = f.slice(r.start, r.start + gone + 1)
      const lean = Math.max(...entry.map(x => x.lean))
      if (lean > 0.3) {
        rolls++
        expect((lean * 180) / Math.PI).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(20)
        expect((lean * 180) / Math.PI).toBeLessThanOrEqual(30)
        expect(Math.max(...entry.map(x => x.paddle))).toBeGreaterThan(0.5)
        expect((gone + 1) / 60).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0.4 - 0.02)
        expect((gone + 1) / 60).toBeLessThanOrEqual(0.65 + 0.02)
      } else if (entry[0]!.ys[0] === 0 && !entry.some(x => Math.abs(x.ys[0]! + AWASH) < 1e-6)) {
        splashes++
        // from the first frame off the surface to gone: 0.1 s, the neck upright
        const off = entry.findIndex(x => x.ys[0]! < 0)
        expect((gone - off + 1) / 60).toBeCloseTo(GREBE.entry.splashTime, 1)
        expect(entry[off]!.neck).toBeGreaterThan(0.7)
      }
    }
    expect(rolls).toBeGreaterThan(20)
    expect(splashes).toBeGreaterThan(2)
  })
  it('sinks to watch with only its head and neck out, the back 45 mm under, for 5-30 s, every few minutes', () => {
    let watches = 0
    for (const h of hours) {
      for (const k of [0, 1, 2]) {
        for (const r of runs(h.frames, f => f.states[k] === 'sink' && Math.abs(f.ys[k]! + AWASH) < 1e-9)) {
          // a watch held at the periscope, as against the brief hold of a sinking dive
          if (r.to - r.from < 4) continue
          watches++
          expect(r.to - r.from).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(5 - 0.15)
          expect(r.to - r.from).toBeLessThanOrEqual(30 + 0.15)
        }
      }
    }
    // once in 3-6 minutes of the time it isn't in a bout: 12 grebe-hours, half of them out of bouts
    expect(watches).toBeGreaterThan(40)
    expect(watches).toBeLessThan(160)
  })
  it('spends about a third of its time under water', () => {
    let under = 0, all = 0
    for (const h of hours) for (const f of h.frames) for (const y of f.ys) { all++; if (y <= -DEPTH + 1e-9) under++ }
    expect(under / all).toBeGreaterThan(0.25)
    expect(under / all).toBeLessThan(0.4)
  })
  it('keeps at least 10 m from the other grebes on the water, coming up clear of them too', () => {
    // one passing under another's patch is out of sight, and doesn't count
    let closest = Infinity
    for (const h of [...hours, fine]) {
      for (const f of h.frames) {
        for (const [a, c] of [[0, 1], [0, 2], [1, 2]] as const) {
          if (f.ys[a]! <= -DEPTH + 1e-9 || f.ys[c]! <= -DEPTH + 1e-9) continue
          closest = Math.min(closest, Math.hypot(f.xs[a]! - f.xs[c]!, f.zs[a]! - f.zs[c]!))
        }
      }
    }
    expect(closest).toBeGreaterThan(GREBE.spacing.flock - 0.01)
  })
  it('moves smoothly: at 60 fps no part of the pose jumps in a frame, and only a splash drops it fast', () => {
    const most = { lean: 0.1, tail: 0.1, reach: 0.15, paddle: 0.15, neck: 0.1 }
    const { frames: f } = fine
    for (let i = 1; i < f.length; i++) {
      for (const [k, limit] of Object.entries(most) as [keyof typeof most, number][]) {
        const d = Math.abs(f[i]![k] - f[i - 1]![k])
        if (d > limit) expect.fail(`${k} changed by ${d.toFixed(4)} at ${f[i]!.t.toFixed(2)} s (${f[i]!.states[0]})`)
      }
      // the splash's 0.1 s and the 0.1 s rise are the fastest: 0.4 m along smoothstep, 1.5 x 0.4 / 0.1 / 60 = 0.1 m a frame
      const dy = Math.abs(f[i]!.ys[0]! - f[i - 1]!.ys[0]!)
      if (dy > 0.1 + 1e-9) expect.fail(`y changed by ${dy.toFixed(4)} at ${f[i]!.t.toFixed(2)} s`)
    }
  })
  it('stays in its zone, and no frame moves it further than its pace, under water or on it', () => {
    const pace = (GREBE.under.speed + AVOID + DRIFT * WIND.effective) / 60 + 1e-9
    const { frames: f } = fine
    for (let i = 1; i < f.length; i++) {
      for (const k of [0, 1, 2]) {
        if (!pointInPolygon(f[i]!.xs[k]!, f[i]!.zs[k]!, water.points)) expect.fail(`bird ${k} left the zone at ${f[i]!.t}`)
        const moved = Math.hypot(f[i]!.xs[k]! - f[i - 1]!.xs[k]!, f[i]!.zs[k]! - f[i - 1]!.zs[k]!)
        if (moved > pace) expect.fail(`bird ${k} moved ${moved.toFixed(4)} m at ${f[i]!.t.toFixed(2)} s`)
      }
    }
  })
  it('rings the water as it goes under and as it comes up', () => {
    const h = hours[0]!
    const downs = runs(h.frames, underwater(0)).filter(r => r.start > 0 && r.end < h.frames.length - 1)
    const going = h.rings.filter(r => r.size === GREBE.rings.roll || r.size === GREBE.rings.splash || r.size === GREBE.rings.sink)
    const up = h.rings.filter(r => r.size === GREBE.rings.up)
    expect(going.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(downs.length)
    expect(up.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(downs.length)
  })
})
```

Create `src/behavior/grebe.ts`:

```ts
import type { BehaviorContext, BehaviorController } from './types'
import { approach, between, ease } from './types'
import { createSwimmer, pickByShare, mid, type SwimSettings } from './swimmer'
import { pointInPolygon } from '../location/zones'
import type { PoseState } from '../birds/model'
import type { Rng } from '../catalog/types'

type Range = readonly [number, number]
type Start = 'roll' | 'splash' | 'sink'

/** The Pied-billed Grebe's settings. */
export interface GrebeSettings extends SwimSettings {
  /** its time: foraging bouts of `dives` dives, cruising a few metres at a time, loafing with the stern puff cocked */
  bouts: { share: number; dives: Range }
  /** it works a patch of its own: farther than `radius` metres from where it started, it heads back that way */
  patch: { radius: number }
  cruising: { share: number; range: Range; ring: { every: number; size: number } }
  loaf: { share: number; seconds: Range; tail: number }
  /** the face in the water for `seconds`, `depth` metres under: before `before` of dives, and now and then while cruising */
  peer: { seconds: Range; before: number; perSecond: number; depth: number }
  /** how often each start begins a dive */
  start: { roll: number; splash: number; sink: number }
  /**
   * a roll-under: the face in, the body pitched `rollLean` (the stern up), gone in `rollTime` seconds, a kick of the
   * feet at the stern; a splash: the stern puff up for `puff` seconds, then driven straight down in `splashTime`; a
   * sink: down to back-awash in `sinkDown`, held `sinkHold`, then under in `slide`. `depth` metres takes it all under.
   */
  entry: { rollTime: Range; rollLean: number; puff: Range; splashTime: number; sinkDown: number; sinkHold: number; slide: number; depth: number }
  /**
   * under water: a gamma-distributed time with its mean and sd, kept to `range`; a distance the same way, within
   * `turn` radians of the heading, swum at most `speed` m/s (a long way stays down longer); `tries` at a spot clear of
   * the others to come up. If another bird has come up near that spot meanwhile, it swims on under water to a clear
   * one, for up to `extra` seconds more.
   */
  under: { mean: number; sd: number; range: Range; distance: { mean: number; sd: number; range: Range }; speed: number; turn: number; tries: number; extra: number }
  /** up in `rise` seconds, the neck upright in `neck`; then on the surface, looking about, for `pause` seconds (mean `pauseMean`) */
  resurface: { rise: number; neck: number; pause: Range; pauseMean: number }
  /** sink and watch: settle to back-awash in `settle` seconds, hold `hold`, then rise in `up`, or `slide` of the time dive */
  sink: { perSecond: number; settle: Range; hold: Range; up: Range; slide: number }
  rings: { roll: number; splash: number; sink: number; up: number }
}

/** How far the grebe goes down to back-awash, to watch or to start a dive: the back from +0.028 m to -0.045 m (section 6). */
export const AWASH = 0.073

/**
 * From docs/research/2026-09-25-pied-billed-grebe.md, section 11 (sections 6 and 7 for the depths and the video).
 * Sourced: the forage dives, 7.58 s (SD 3.07, 1-15) and 3.69 m (SD 3.02, 0-12) under water (Bleich 1975, n = 154), and
 * the surface pause from Bleich's dive/pause ratio of 2.3 (computed). The dive starts, the sink, and the surfacing are
 * from a few video clips; the shares, bout lengths, and cruising are estimates.
 */
export const GREBE: GrebeSettings = {
  // 0.15 m/s (0.05-0.3), 5-30 s at a time
  cruise: { speed: 0.15, seconds: [5, 30] },
  turn: 1.2,
  // no flock: each works its own patch, anywhere in the zone, the patches tens of metres apart (20 m at the start)
  flock: { radius: Infinity, retargetSeconds: Infinity, drift: 0, whenFarther: Infinity, apart: 20 },
  // at least 10 m from other grebes; from any other swimmer 1.5 m, as it swims away from ducks (ours)
  spacing: { flock: 10, other: 1.5 },
  // no duck's wake: a grebe leaves a small ring every 1.5 s as it swims (`cruising.ring`)
  wake: { every: 0.9, size: 1, above: Infinity },
  // about half its time in foraging bouts of 3-12 dives; the rest cruising and loafing (the split is ours)
  bouts: { share: 0.5, dives: [3, 12] },
  // each works its own patch; 6 m either way is ours: with the patches 20 m apart, 8 m of water lies between them
  patch: { radius: 6 },
  // a small ring about every 1.5 s as it swims; a cruise goes 1-5 m (ours)
  cruising: { share: 0.15, range: [1, 5], ring: { every: 1.5, size: 0.4 } },
  // 30-120 s, the stern puff cocked 20-40 degrees (30 here)
  loaf: { share: 0.27, seconds: [30, 120], tail: 0.52 },
  // 0.8-1.2 s, before about a third of dives, and now and then while cruising (one bird peered about 10 times in 18
  // minutes: once in 90 s of cruising here); the face just under (ours)
  peer: { seconds: [0.8, 1.2], before: 1 / 3, perSecond: 1 / 90, depth: 0.03 },
  // roll-under 70%, splash 15%, sink 15%; never a forward leap
  start: { roll: 0.7, splash: 0.15, sink: 0.15 },
  entry: {
    // gone 0.4-0.65 s after the face touches, the stern tipped up 20-30 degrees (25 here)
    rollTime: [0.4, 0.65], rollLean: 0.44,
    // the stern puff rises 0.2-0.3 s before, then the body goes straight down in under 0.1 s
    puff: [0.2, 0.3], splashTime: 0.1,
    // down to back-awash in 0.4 s, held about 1.5 s, then the head straight down (0.2 s here, ours)
    sinkDown: 0.4, sinkHold: 1.5, slide: 0.2,
    // 0.4 m takes the whole small bird under (ours)
    depth: 0.4,
  },
  under: {
    mean: 7.6, sd: 3.1, range: [2, 15],
    distance: { mean: 3.7, sd: 3.0, range: [0, 12] },
    // at most 1.5 m/s under water (ours: forage dives net 0.5 m/s, escape dives 1.1); within 60 degrees of the heading
    speed: 1.5, turn: Math.PI / 3, tries: 24, extra: 5,
  },
  // head and body up together within 0.1 s, the neck upright by 0.4 s; the pause between dives 2-10 s, mean about 4
  // (Bleich's ratio gives 3.3), drawn skewed toward the short end
  resurface: { rise: 0.1, neck: 0.4, pause: [2, 10], pauseMean: 4 },
  // once every 3-6 minutes (4.5 here); settle over 1-3 s, hold 5-30 s, rise over 2-4 s, or slide under into a dive
  // (half the time, ours)
  sink: { perSecond: 1 / 270, settle: [1, 3], hold: [5, 30], up: [2, 4], slide: 0.5 },
  // ring sizes are ours: a roll's ring, a splash's bigger one, a sink's swirl, and where it comes up
  rings: { roll: 0.5, splash: 0.7, sink: 0.3, up: 0.5 },
}

type State = 'cruise' | 'peer' | 'dive' | 'under' | 'resurface' | 'sink' | 'loaf'
const NECK: Record<State, number> = { cruise: 0.8, peer: 1, dive: 1, under: 1, resurface: 0.9, sink: 1, loaf: 0.15 }
const step = (v: number, want: number, by: number) => (v < want ? Math.min(want, v + by) : Math.max(want, v - by))

/** A gamma draw with this mean and standard deviation (Marsaglia and Tsang's method), kept to the range. */
export function gamma(rng: Rng, mean: number, sd: number, [lo, hi]: Range): number {
  const draw = (shape: number): number => {
    if (shape < 1) return draw(shape + 1) * (1 - rng()) ** (1 / shape)
    const d = shape - 1 / 3, c = 1 / Math.sqrt(9 * d)
    for (;;) {
      let x = 0, v = 0
      do {
        // a standard normal, by Box-Muller
        x = Math.sqrt(-2 * Math.log(1 - rng())) * Math.cos(2 * Math.PI * rng())
        v = 1 + c * x
      } while (v <= 0)
      v = v * v * v
      if (Math.log(1 - rng()) < 0.5 * x * x + d - d * v + d * Math.log(v)) return d * v
    }
  }
  return Math.min(hi, Math.max(lo, draw((mean / sd) ** 2) * ((sd * sd) / mean)))
}

/**
 * The `grebe` style: foraging bouts of dives, each started by a roll-under, a splash, or a sink, under water for
 * seconds and metres, then up with a ring and a look about; cruising and peering between, sinking to watch now and
 * then, and loafing. It keeps at least 10 m from the other grebes.
 */
export function createGrebe(ctx: BehaviorContext, startX: number, startZ: number, S: GrebeSettings = GREBE): BehaviorController {
  const b = createSwimmer(ctx, startX, startZ, S)
  const home: [number, number] = [startX, startZ]
  let state: State = 'loaf'
  let timer = 0
  let clock = 0
  let [tx, tz] = [b.x, b.z]
  let dives = 0
  let start: Start = 'roll'
  // a dive's entry, or a sink-and-watch, goes in stages; how long it has been on this one
  let stage = 0, onStage = 0
  // how far down the bird is (0 afloat, AWASH at the periscope, entry.depth under), easing from `from` to `to`
  let depth = 0, from = 0, to = 0, over = 0, moved = 0
  let [ux, uz] = [0, 0]
  let face = 0, puff = 0, roll = 0, kick = 0, preen = 0
  let sinceRing = 0, paddlePhase = 0

  const TABLE = {
    bout: { share: S.bouts.share, mean: mid(S.bouts.dives) * (S.under.mean + S.resurface.pauseMean + 1) },
    cruise: { share: S.cruising.share, mean: mid(S.cruise.seconds) },
    loaf: { share: S.loaf.share, mean: mid(S.loaf.seconds) },
  }
  const ring = (size: number) => ctx.onRipple?.(b.x, b.z, size)
  /** Go from the depth it is at to `target` over `seconds`, eased. */
  function sinkTo(target: number, seconds: number): void { from = depth; to = target; over = seconds; moved = 0 }
  const settled = () => moved >= over
  const toStage = (n: number) => { stage = n; onStage = 0 }
  const boutLength = () => Math.floor(between(ctx.rng, [S.bouts.dives[0], S.bouts.dives[1] + 1]))

  function next(): void {
    const pick = pickByShare(ctx.rng, TABLE)
    if (pick === 'bout') { dives = boutLength(); beforeDive() }
    else if (pick === 'cruise') cruise()
    else { state = 'loaf'; timer = between(ctx.rng, S.loaf.seconds) }
  }
  function cruise(): void {
    state = 'cruise'
    timer = between(ctx.rng, S.cruise.seconds)
    ;[tx, tz] = patch()
  }
  /** the way it faces, or, strayed from its patch, the way home */
  function bearing(): number {
    return Math.hypot(home[0] - b.x, home[1] - b.z) > S.patch.radius ? Math.atan2(-(home[1] - b.z), home[0] - b.x) : b.heading
  }
  /** a spot a few metres off (homeward, if it has strayed), in the zone, and clear of the other grebes if one of a few tries is */
  function patch(): [number, number] {
    let best: [number, number] = [b.x, b.z]
    const strayed = Math.hypot(home[0] - b.x, home[1] - b.z) > S.patch.radius
    for (let i = 0; i < 12; i++) {
      const a = strayed ? bearing() + (ctx.rng() * 2 - 1) * (Math.PI / 3) : ctx.rng() * Math.PI * 2, r = between(ctx.rng, S.cruising.range)
      const [px, pz] = [b.x + Math.cos(a) * r, b.z - Math.sin(a) * r]
      if (!pointInPolygon(px, pz, b.zone.points)) continue
      best = [px, pz]
      if (b.clearAt(px, pz, 0.5)) break
    }
    return best
  }
  /** settle to back-awash (stage 0), hold there watching (stage 1), then rise (stage 2) or slide into a dive */
  function sinkAndWatch(): void {
    state = 'sink'
    toStage(0)
    sinkTo(AWASH, between(ctx.rng, S.sink.settle))
    hold = between(ctx.rng, S.sink.hold)
  }
  let hold = 0
  /** peer first, a third of the time, then dive */
  function beforeDive(): void {
    if (ctx.rng() < S.peer.before) { state = 'peer'; timer = between(ctx.rng, S.peer.seconds) }
    else dive()
  }
  function dive(): void {
    state = 'dive'
    dives--
    const r = ctx.rng()
    start = r < S.start.roll ? 'roll' : r < S.start.roll + S.start.splash ? 'splash' : 'sink'
    toStage(0)
    if (start === 'roll') { sinkTo(S.entry.depth, between(ctx.rng, S.entry.rollTime)); ring(S.rings.roll) }
    else if (start === 'splash') timer = between(ctx.rng, S.entry.puff)
    else sinkTo(AWASH, S.entry.sinkDown)
  }
  function goUnder(): void {
    state = 'under'
    ;[wentX, wentZ] = [b.x, b.z]
    ;[ux, uz] = surfacing()
    // a long way takes the time it takes: at most 12 m at `speed`, 8 s, inside the 2-15 s a dive may last
    timer = Math.max(gamma(ctx.rng, S.under.mean, S.under.sd, S.under.range), Math.hypot(ux - b.x, uz - b.z) / S.under.speed)
  }
  let [wentX, wentZ] = [0, 0]
  /** A spot clear of the others within `extra` seconds' swim of (ux, uz), the nearest there is, if (ux, uz) isn't clear. */
  function clearOfOthers(): void {
    if (b.clearAt(ux, uz)) return
    let best: [number, number] | null = null
    const reach = S.under.speed * S.under.extra
    for (let r = reach / 12; r <= reach + 1e-9; r += reach / 12) {
      for (let k = 0; k < 16; k++) {
        const [px, pz] = [ux + Math.cos((k * Math.PI) / 8) * r, uz - Math.sin((k * Math.PI) / 8) * r]
        const within = Math.hypot(px - wentX, pz - wentZ) <= S.under.distance.range[1]
        if (within && pointInPolygon(px, pz, b.zone.points) && b.clearAt(px, pz, 0.05) && (!best || Math.hypot(px - ux, pz - uz) < Math.hypot(best[0] - ux, best[1] - uz))) best = [px, pz]
      }
      if (best) break
    }
    if (best) [ux, uz] = best
  }

  /**
   * Where it will come up: about the way it faces (homeward, if it has strayed from its patch), as far as it draws,
   * in the zone, and clear of the other swimmers. Where the zone's edge cuts a way short, or another bird is there,
   * it tries another way.
   */
  function surfacing(): [number, number] {
    let best: [number, number] = [b.x, b.z], bestRoom = -Infinity
    const d = gamma(ctx.rng, S.under.distance.mean, S.under.distance.sd, S.under.distance.range)
    const third = S.under.tries / 3
    for (let i = 0; i < S.under.tries; i++) {
      // a third of the tries about its bearing, the whole way; a third any way, the whole way; the last cut short by
      // the zone's edge as they must be
      const a = i < third ? bearing() + (ctx.rng() * 2 - 1) * S.under.turn : ctx.rng() * Math.PI * 2
      let len = d
      while (len > 0.01 && !pointInPolygon(b.x + Math.cos(a) * len, b.z - Math.sin(a) * len, b.zone.points)) len /= 2
      if (len <= 0.01) len = 0
      if (len < d && i < 2 * third) continue
      const [px, pz] = [b.x + Math.cos(a) * len, b.z - Math.sin(a) * len]
      // a metre to spare, as the others move on while it is down
      if (b.clearAt(px, pz, 1)) return [px, pz]
      // none clear yet: keep the one with the most room
      const room = Math.min(...b.others().map(n => Math.hypot(n.x - px, n.z - pz) - b.spacingTo(n)))
      if (room > bestRoom) { bestRoom = room; best = [px, pz] }
    }
    return best
  }
  function resurface(): void {
    state = 'resurface'
    sinkTo(0, S.resurface.rise)
    // the pause, skewed toward the short end so its mean is `pauseMean`
    const [lo, hi] = S.resurface.pause
    timer = lo + (hi - lo) * ctx.rng() ** ((hi - S.resurface.pauseMean) / (S.resurface.pauseMean - lo))
    ring(S.rings.up)
  }

  const self: BehaviorController = {
    name: 'swim',
    get state() { return state },
    get x() { return b.x }, get z() { return b.z }, get heading() { return b.heading },
    get y() { return -depth },
    spacing: S.spacing.other,
    group: b.flock.group,
    update(dt: number, pose: PoseState) {
      clock += dt
      timer -= dt
      onStage += dt
      sinceRing += dt
      moved += dt
      depth = settled() ? to : from + (to - from) * ease(moved / over)

      if (state === 'cruise') {
        b.swimTo(tx, tz, S.cruise.speed, dt)
        if (b.speed > 0.05 && sinceRing >= S.cruising.ring.every) { sinceRing = 0; ring(S.cruising.ring.size) }
        if (timer <= 0) next()
        else if (ctx.rng() < S.peer.perSecond * dt) { state = 'peer'; timer = between(ctx.rng, S.peer.seconds); dives = 0 }
        else if (ctx.rng() < S.sink.perSecond * dt) sinkAndWatch()
      } else if (state === 'loaf') {
        b.float(dt)
        if (timer <= 0) next()
        else if (ctx.rng() < S.sink.perSecond * dt) sinkAndWatch()
      } else if (state === 'peer') {
        b.float(dt)
        if (timer <= 0) { if (dives > 0) dive(); else cruise() }
      } else if (state === 'dive') {
        b.float(dt)
        if (start === 'roll' && settled()) goUnder()
        // a splash: the stern puff up first, then straight down
        else if (start === 'splash' && stage === 0 && timer <= 0) { toStage(1); sinkTo(S.entry.depth, S.entry.splashTime); ring(S.rings.splash) }
        else if (start === 'splash' && stage === 1 && settled()) goUnder()
        // a sink: down to back-awash, a pause, then the head straight down
        else if (start === 'sink' && stage === 0 && settled()) toStage(1)
        else if (start === 'sink' && stage === 1 && onStage >= S.entry.sinkHold) { toStage(2); sinkTo(S.entry.depth, S.entry.slide); ring(S.rings.sink) }
        else if (start === 'sink' && stage === 2 && settled()) goUnder()
      } else if (state === 'under') {
        // Time to come up: if another bird is near the spot, afloat or about to come up itself, swim on to a clear
        // one first, checking until it is up; after `extra` seconds more it comes up wherever it is.
        const late = -timer < S.under.extra
        if (timer <= 0 && late) clearOfOthers()
        // swimming under water toward where it will come up, evenly over the time it stays down
        const left = Math.hypot(ux - b.x, uz - b.z)
        const by = Math.min(left, (left / Math.max(timer, dt)) * dt, S.under.speed * dt)
        if (left > 1e-9) { b.x += ((ux - b.x) / left) * by; b.z += ((uz - b.z) / left) * by }
        b.speed = 0
        if (timer <= 0 && left - by < 1e-9 && (b.clearAt(ux, uz) || !late)) resurface()
      } else if (state === 'resurface') {
        b.float(dt)
        if (timer <= 0) { if (dives > 0) beforeDive(); else next() }
      } else if (state === 'sink') {
        b.float(dt)
        if (stage === 0 && settled()) toStage(1)
        // watched long enough: slide under into a bout of dives, or rise and carry on
        else if (stage === 1 && onStage >= hold) {
          if (ctx.rng() < S.sink.slide) { dives = boutLength() - 1; state = 'dive'; start = 'sink'; toStage(2); sinkTo(S.entry.depth, S.entry.slide); ring(S.rings.sink) }
          else { toStage(2); sinkTo(0, between(ctx.rng, S.sink.up)) }
        } else if (stage === 2 && settled()) next()
      }

      // posture, each part eased at a steady rate and shaped with smoothstep
      const rolling = state === 'dive' && start === 'roll'
      face = step(face, state === 'peer' || rolling ? 1 : 0, dt / 0.2)
      puff = step(puff, (state === 'dive' && start === 'splash') || state === 'loaf' ? 1 : 0, dt / 0.2)
      roll = step(roll, rolling ? 1 : 0, dt / 0.3)
      kick = step(kick, rolling && onStage > 0.15 ? 1 : 0, dt / 0.2)
      const kFace = ease(face), kPuff = ease(puff), kRoll = ease(roll)
      pose.reach = kFace
      pose.reachDepth = kFace * S.peer.depth
      pose.reachAngle = Math.PI / 2
      // the roll-under pitches the body nose-down, stern up, as a tip-up does
      pose.lean.x = S.entry.rollLean * kRoll
      pose.tailTilt = Math.max(kPuff, kRoll) * S.loaf.tail
      // the feet show only as the kick at the stern that takes it under
      pose.paddle = ease(kick)
      paddlePhase = (paddlePhase + 3 * dt) % 1
      pose.paddlePhase = paddlePhase
      preen = approach(preen, state === 'loaf' ? 1 : 0, 1, dt)
      pose.lean.z = preen * 0.05 * Math.sin(clock * Math.PI * 0.5)
      // the neck comes up quickly to dive, and by `resurface.neck` after coming up
      pose.neckExtend = approach(pose.neckExtend, NECK[state], state === 'resurface' ? 2 / S.resurface.neck : state === 'dive' ? 6 : 3, dt)
      pose.headBob = 0
    },
  }
  b.self = self
  next()
  return self
}
```

### Task 12: Diving, targeting, and the ring budget

**Files:**
- Create: `src/player/candidates.ts`, `src/player/candidates.test.ts`, `src/birds/cast.test.ts`
- Modify: `src/behavior/types.ts`, `src/behavior/swimmer.ts`, `src/behavior/coot.ts`, `src/behavior/grebe.ts`,
  `src/behavior/dabbler.ts`, `src/behavior/shoveler.ts`, `src/birds/spawn.ts`, `src/main.tsx`,
  `src/behavior/swim.test.ts`, `src/birds/spawn.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the four styles (Tasks 8–11); `pickTarget` (existing); `createIdentifyController` (existing, unchanged);
  `RippleBuffer`, `MAX_RIPPLES` (existing, unchanged).
- Produces:

```ts
// src/behavior/types.ts
BehaviorController.submerged?: boolean   // all the way under: the coot from the end of its lunge until it starts up,
                                         // the grebe from the end of its entry until it starts up (y = -0.4 for both)
type Neighbour = Pick<BehaviorController, 'x' | 'z' | 'heading' | 'state' | 'spacing' | 'group' | 'submerged'>

// src/behavior/swimmer.ts
body.others()                        // no longer includes a bird under the water: a duck swims over a diving coot
body.clearAt(px, pz, extra = 0, under = false)   // under: count the birds under the water too, where they are now

// src/birds/spawn.ts: every frame, model.group.visible = !controller.submerged

// src/player/candidates.ts
export function targetCandidates(birds: readonly BirdInstance[]): Candidate[]   // skips a hidden bird

// src/main.tsx: the candidates come from targetCandidates(registry.all())
```

**How an identification drops when its bird dives.**
- **What already happens.** The main loop sets `game.target` to whatever `pickTarget` finds among the candidates,
  or to `null`. `pressIdentify` needs a target, so a bird that has left the reticle can't be identified. The
  `identifying` session (its misses) stays in the store, and carries on only if the player presses E on the same
  bird again; a different bird starts a fresh one.
- **What changes.** A diving bird drops out of the candidates, so it leaves the target exactly as if it had swum out
  of the reticle. No change to `identifyController.ts` is needed.
- **When it can happen.** The world is paused while the guide or a card is open, so a bird can only dive between
  guesses.
- **How it is tested.** `candidates.test.ts` runs the main loop's frame (pick from the candidates, set the target)
  around a coot that dives and comes back.

**The ring budget.** The ringing cast was measured with the buffer unbounded (`RippleBuffer(1000)`): the 24
swimmers (three grebes), each in its species' style, and the six dowitchers. Over two 20-minute runs at 30 frames a
second, with Task 11's rates:
- live rings averaged 19–20, with 32–33 at the 99th percentile and 38 at most;
- the wakes alone (size 1, every 0.9 s) averaged 10–11, 23–24 at the 99th percentile, and up to 27–29;
- with the real 32 slots, about 290 rings were pushed out in 20 minutes, half of them wakes.

So with the old wake rate the budget can't hold, however small the other rings are. The rates this task chooses:
- **the wake every 1.5 s** for the dabblers, the Shoveler, and the Coot: the rhythm the grebe research measured for a
  grebe's wake, and what the buffer holds;
- **a dabbling bill's ring at 0.3**, not 0.4;
- **the Coot's pump ring only while it swims to feed**: a travelling coot leaves a wake instead.

With those rates, `MAX_RIPPLES` stays at 32 and the water shader is unchanged:
- with the Kingfisher added (its dives ring too), over five seeds of 20 minutes at 10 frames a second: live rings
  averaged 14.0–14.6, 22–23 at the 99th percentile, 28–30 at most, and none was pushed out;
- without it, over three seeds at 60 frames a second: an average of 15.7–16.2, 23–26 at the 99th percentile, 28–32
  at most, and none pushed out;
- the peaks come close to 32, so a larger buffer would be the next step if the cast grows; see Decisions.

Create `src/player/candidates.test.ts`:

```ts
import * as THREE from 'three'
import { targetCandidates } from './candidates'
import { pickTarget } from './target'
import { BirdRegistry } from '../birds/registry'
import { createStore } from '../store/store'
import type { GameState } from '../store/game'
import { createIdentifyController } from '../game/identifyController'
import { SPECIES } from '../birds/species'

/** A bird's model as the registry holds it: a group placed in the world, its centre, its size. */
function model(x: number, y: number, z: number) {
  const group = new THREE.Group()
  group.position.set(x, y, z)
  group.updateMatrixWorld(true)
  return { group, center: new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.1, 0), radius: 0.2 }
}
/** The camera through binoculars, looking straight down -z. */
function binoculars() {
  const c = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(7.5, 16 / 9, 0.1, 1000)
  c.lookAt(0, 0, -1)
  c.updateMatrixWorld(true)
  c.updateProjectionMatrix()
  return c
}
const vp = { width: 1600, height: 900 }

describe('targetCandidates', () => {
  it("offers every bird at its model's centre in the world", () => {
    const registry = new BirdRegistry()
    const a = registry.add({ species: 'y00475', model: model(1, 0, -10), behavior: 'swim' })
    const [c] = targetCandidates(registry.all())
    expect(c).toMatchObject({ id: a.id, species: 'y00475', radius: 0.2 })
    expect(c!.position.toArray()).toEqual([1, 0.1, -10])
  })
  it('leaves out a bird the spawner has hidden, one all the way under the water', () => {
    const registry = new BirdRegistry()
    registry.add({ species: 'y00475', model: model(0, 0, -10), behavior: 'swim' })
    const grebe = registry.add({ species: 'pibgre', model: model(0.5, -0.4, -12), behavior: 'swim' })
    grebe.model.group.visible = false
    expect(targetCandidates(registry.all()).map(c => c.species)).toEqual(['y00475'])
  })
  it('drops an identification in progress on a bird that dives, as leaving the reticle does', () => {
    // A coot straight ahead through binoculars. The player presses E, guesses wrong, and dismisses the card; the coot
    // dives before the next guess.
    const registry = new BirdRegistry()
    const coot = registry.add({ species: 'y00475', model: model(0, 0, -15), behavior: 'swim' })
    const store = createStore<GameState>({
      optic: 'binoculars', target: null, guideOpen: false, guideMode: 'browse', identifying: null, card: null, lifeListOpen: false,
      titleOpen: false, settingsOpen: false, creditsOpen: false,
    })
    const controller = createIdentifyController({ store, registry, specs: SPECIES, names: c => c, record: () => {}, exitPointerLock: () => {} })
    /** main.tsx's frame: pick from the candidates, and set the target to it or to nothing */
    const frame = () => {
      const t = pickTarget(targetCandidates(registry.all()), binoculars(), vp)
      store.set({ target: t ? { id: t.id, species: t.species, tooSmall: t.tooSmall } : null })
    }
    frame()
    expect(store.get().target?.id).toBe(coot.id)
    controller.pressIdentify()
    controller.guess('mallar3')
    controller.dismissCard()
    expect(store.get().identifying).toEqual({ targetId: coot.id, species: 'y00475', misses: 1 })

    // it dives: the spawner hides it, it drops out of the candidates, and the target goes as if it had left the reticle
    coot.model.group.visible = false
    frame()
    expect(store.get().target).toBeNull()
    controller.pressIdentify()
    expect(store.get().guideOpen).toBe(false)

    // it comes up in the reticle again: the identification carries on where it was, as it would for a bird that had
    // swum out of the reticle and back
    coot.model.group.visible = true
    frame()
    controller.pressIdentify()
    expect(store.get().guideOpen).toBe(true)
    expect(store.get().identifying).toEqual({ targetId: coot.id, species: 'y00475', misses: 1 })
  })
})
```

Create `src/birds/cast.test.ts`:

```ts
import { spawnCast } from './spawn'
import { BirdRegistry } from './registry'
import { Wind } from '../world/wind'
import { RippleBuffer, MAX_RIPPLES } from '../world/ripples'
import { loadLocation } from '../location'
import { targetCandidates } from '../player/candidates'
import type { SpeciesSpec, Foraging } from './spec'

// Each swimmer in the style its species file will name when the species is detailed (the Mallard in Task 14, the
// rest in Part 2). Until then the spec has no style and every swimmer would be a dabbler; a spec that already names
// one keeps it.
vi.mock('./species', async importOriginal => {
  const real = await importOriginal<typeof import('./species')>()
  const STYLE: Record<string, Foraging> = { mallar3: 'dabbler', gadwal: 'dabbler', amewig: 'dabbler', norsho: 'shoveler', y00475: 'coot', pibgre: 'grebe' }
  const styled = (s: SpeciesSpec): SpeciesSpec => (STYLE[s.code] && !s.foraging ? { ...s, foraging: STYLE[s.code] } : s)
  return { ...real, getSpec: (code: string) => styled(real.getSpec(code)) }
})

/**
 * Everything on the lake that rings the water, as the round's cast will have it: the 24 swimmers (three grebes, not
 * two), the six dowitchers, and the Kingfisher, whose dives ring too.
 */
function ringers() {
  const lake = loadLocation()
  const RINGS = ['y00475', 'mallar3', 'gadwal', 'amewig', 'norsho', 'pibgre', 'lobdow', 'belkin1']
  const cast = lake.cast.filter(e => RINGS.includes(e.species)).map(e => (e.species === 'pibgre' ? { ...e, count: 3 } : e))
  return { ...lake, cast }
}

describe('the whole cast on the water', () => {
  it('rings the water within the buffer: over ten minutes no ring is pushed out before it fades, about 15 alive at once', () => {
    // 24 swimmers' wakes, dips, and dives, six dowitchers' bills, and the Kingfisher: at 10 frames a second, which
    // rings the water as the game does, for two seeds. In the scratch run (and over 5 seeds of 20 minutes each) the
    // live rings averaged 14.0-14.6, 23 at the 99th percentile, and 28-30 at most; at 60 frames a second, 15.7-16.2
    // on average and 28-32 at most, with nothing pushed out.
    const counts: number[] = []
    let dropped = 0
    for (const seed of [42, 7]) {
      const wind = new Wind({ direction: 240, strength: 0.35, gustiness: 0.5, seed: 1 })
      const ripples = new RippleBuffer()
      const spawn = ripples.spawn.bind(ripples)
      ripples.spawn = (x: number, z: number, size?: number) => { if (ripples.rings.length >= MAX_RIPPLES) dropped++; spawn(x, z, size) }
      const world = spawnCast(ringers(), { registry: new BirdRegistry(), wind, ground: () => -1, ripples, seed, perches: { snag: [22, 3, -48] } })
      for (let i = 0; i < 600 * 10; i++) {
        world.update(0.1, wind)
        ripples.update(0.1)
        counts.push(ripples.rings.length)
      }
    }
    expect(dropped).toBe(0)
    const mean = counts.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / counts.length
    expect(mean).toBeGreaterThan(10)
    expect(mean).toBeLessThan(18)
  }, 30_000)   // two seeds x 600 s of the whole cast: about 2 s alone, more in a busy full run
  it('hides each diving bird exactly while it is under the water, and never offers a hidden bird as a target', () => {
    const wind = new Wind({ direction: 240, strength: 0.35, gustiness: 0.5, seed: 1 })
    const registry = new BirdRegistry()
    const world = spawnCast(ringers(), { registry, wind, ground: () => -1, ripples: new RippleBuffer(), seed: 42, perches: { snag: [22, 3, -48] } })
    let hidden = 0
    for (let i = 0; i < 300 * 10; i++) {
      world.update(0.1, wind)
      for (const b of registry.all()) {
        if (b.behavior !== 'swim') continue
        // a coot or grebe all the way down sits 0.4 m under; anything less is still in sight
        const down = b.model.group.position.y <= -0.4 + 1e-9
        if (b.model.group.visible === down) expect.fail(`${b.species} ${b.id} at y ${b.model.group.position.y} is ${b.model.group.visible ? 'shown' : 'hidden'}`)
        if (down) hidden++
      }
      const offered = new Set(targetCandidates(registry.all()).map(c => c.id))
      for (const b of registry.all()) if (!b.model.group.visible && offered.has(b.id)) expect.fail(`hidden ${b.species} offered as a target`)
    }
    expect(hidden).toBeGreaterThan(100)
  }, 30_000)   // 300 s of the whole cast, checking every bird each frame
})
```

Create `src/player/candidates.ts`:

```ts
import type { BirdInstance } from '../birds/registry'
import type { Candidate } from './target'

/**
 * What the reticle can pick from: every registered bird the player can see, at its model's centre. A bird the spawner
 * has hidden, one all the way under the water, is not a candidate: otherwise the player could identify a bird they
 * cannot see.
 */
export function targetCandidates(birds: readonly BirdInstance[]): Candidate[] {
  return birds
    .filter(b => b.model.group.visible)
    .map(b => ({ id: b.id, species: b.species, position: b.model.group.localToWorld(b.model.center.clone()), radius: b.model.radius }))
}
```

### Task 13: The painter in a Web Worker, and Begin waiting for it

The atlases the cast needs paint in a Web Worker behind the title card, which shows how far along it is. The cast is built, and Begin enabled, only once every atlas is ready. A detailed bird is therefore never built before its atlas is ready, and building one paints nothing. If a Worker can't be made, or fails, the rest of the atlases paint on the main thread, one per task. The dev overlay still reports each atlas's paint time, now the worker's.

**Files:**
- Create: `src/birds/paint/job.ts`, `src/birds/paint/paint.worker.ts`, `src/birds/paint/prepaint.ts`, `src/birds/paint/prepaint.test.ts`, `src/ui/Title.test.tsx`
- Modify: `src/birds/paint/painter.ts`, `src/main.tsx` (startup and Begin only), `src/ui/Title.tsx`, `src/ui/App.tsx`, `src/ui/ui.css`
- `vite.config.ts` does not change. Vite 8 bundles `new Worker(new URL('./birds/paint/paint.worker.ts', import.meta.url), { type: 'module' })` by itself, as `dist/assets/paint.worker-*.js`.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - `expandCast(loc: Location, rng: Rng): Individual[]` from `src/birds/spawn.ts`, with `Individual.species` and `Individual.variant`. Task 1 leaves its signature unchanged; checked against section B's Task 1 commit.

```ts
// src/birds/paint/painter.ts (materialFor's signature is unchanged)
/** One atlas: a species, and one of its versions (none: the species' own plumage). */
export interface AtlasJob { code: string; variant?: string }
/** An atlas painted off the main thread: its RGBA pixels, and how long the painting took in ms. */
export interface PaintedAtlas extends AtlasJob { pixels: Uint8ClampedArray; ms: number }
export function paintSpecies(spec: SpeciesSpec, variant?: string): Uint8ClampedArray   // paint here and now
export const atlasKey: (job: AtlasJob) => string          // `${code}:${variant ?? ''}`, materialFor's cache key
export function atlasReady(job: AtlasJob): boolean        // cached, so materialFor won't paint it
export function storeAtlas(a: PaintedAtlas): void         // cache it and count its paint in paintStats

// src/birds/paint/job.ts: the worker's handler, a plain function
export function paintJob(job: AtlasJob): PaintedAtlas

// src/birds/paint/prepaint.ts
export function castAtlases(loc: Location, specs?: Record<string, SpeciesSpec>): AtlasJob[]
export interface PaintWorker {
  onmessage: ((e: MessageEvent<PaintedAtlas>) => void) | null
  onerror: ((e: ErrorEvent) => void) | null
  postMessage(job: AtlasJob): void
  terminate(): void
}
export interface PrepaintOptions { worker?: () => PaintWorker; onProgress?: (done: number, total: number) => void }
export async function prepaint(jobs: readonly AtlasJob[], opts?: PrepaintOptions): Promise<void>

// src/ui/Title.tsx
export interface PaintProgress { done: number; total: number; ready: boolean }
TitleProps.painting: PaintProgress
// src/ui/App.tsx
AppDeps.painting: Store<PaintProgress>
```

- For Task 14 and Part 2:
  - The worker imports the species registry, so every detailed species is painted in the worker without being registered anywhere.
  - Species files, and everything `painter.ts` imports, now also load in a worker. They must not touch `document` or `window` when their module loads.
  - The atlas count on the title card grows by itself, reaching "… of 20" once the six swimmers are detailed.

```ts
import * as THREE from 'three'
import { atlasKey as key, atlasReady, materialFor, paintSpecies, paintStats, type AtlasJob, type PaintedAtlas } from './painter'
import { paintJob } from './job'
import { castAtlases, prepaint, type PaintWorker } from './prepaint'
import { ATLAS_SIZE } from './atlas'
import { TEST_DETAIL, TEST_SPEC } from '../detail/fixture'
import { SPECIES, getSpec } from '../species'
import { parseSpeciesSpec } from '../spec'
import { expandCast } from '../spawn'
import { loadLocation } from '../../location'
import { makeRng } from '../../catalog/rng'

const pixelsOf = (m: THREE.MeshStandardMaterial) => (m.map as THREE.DataTexture).image.data
const flat = (v: number) => new Uint8ClampedArray(ATLAS_SIZE * ATLAS_SIZE * 4).fill(v)
// identity as a boolean: a failed toBe on two million-byte arrays makes vitest diff them, which never finishes
const same = (a: unknown, b: unknown) => a === b

/**
 * A stand-in for the paint worker: answers each job a tick later, as a real worker would, with `answer`'s pixels, or
 * fires an error instead where `answer` returns 'fail'. It records what was posted and whether it was terminated.
 */
function fakeWorker(answer: (job: AtlasJob) => Uint8ClampedArray | 'fail') {
  const posted: AtlasJob[] = []
  const state = { terminated: false }
  const w: PaintWorker = {
    onmessage: null,
    onerror: null,
    postMessage(job) {
      posted.push(job)
      setTimeout(() => {
        if (state.terminated) return
        const px = answer(job)
        if (px === 'fail') w.onerror?.({ message: 'boom' } as ErrorEvent)
        else w.onmessage?.({ data: { ...job, pixels: px, ms: 40 } } as MessageEvent<PaintedAtlas>)
      }, 0)
    },
    terminate() { state.terminated = true },
  }
  return { w, posted, state }
}

describe('paintJob, the worker\'s handler', () => {
  it('paints the atlas materialFor would, and says how long it took', () => {
    const r = paintJob({ code: 'leasan' })
    expect(r.code).toBe('leasan')
    expect(r.variant).toBeUndefined()
    expect(r.ms).toBeGreaterThan(0)
    expect(Buffer.from(r.pixels).equals(Buffer.from(paintSpecies(getSpec('leasan'))))).toBe(true)
  })
  it('owns its whole buffer, so posting it back transfers exactly the atlas', () => {
    const r = paintJob({ code: 'lobdow' })
    expect(r.pixels.byteOffset).toBe(0)
    expect(r.pixels.buffer.byteLength).toBe(ATLAS_SIZE * ATLAS_SIZE * 4)
  })
  it('fails loudly on a species with no spec', () => {
    expect(() => paintJob({ code: 'nosuchbird' })).toThrow(/nosuchbird/)
  })
})

describe('castAtlases', () => {
  const lake = loadLocation()
  it('covers every detailed bird the cast can make, whatever the seed, once each', () => {
    const jobs = castAtlases(lake)
    const keys = new Set(jobs.map(key))
    expect(keys.size).toBe(jobs.length)
    for (let seed = 1; seed <= 20; seed++) {
      for (const ind of expandCast(lake, makeRng(seed))) {
        if (getSpec(ind.species).detail) expect(keys.has(key({ code: ind.species, variant: ind.variant })), `${ind.species}:${ind.variant}`).toBe(true)
      }
    }
  })
  it('paints every version of a detailed species with versions, and nothing for block birds or flyovers', () => {
    // a Mallard with a detail block and exactly two versions (Task 14 gives the real one three), the shorebirds as they are
    const specs = { ...SPECIES, mallar3: parseSpeciesSpec({ ...getSpec('mallar3'), variants: { male: {}, female: {} }, detail: TEST_DETAIL }) }
    const loc = {
      ...lake,
      cast: [
        { species: 'mallar3', behavior: 'swim' as const, zone: 'near-water', count: 1, plumage: 'mixed' as const },
        { species: 'leasan', behavior: 'probe' as const, zone: 'mudflat', count: 5 },
        { species: 'grbher3', behavior: 'wade' as const, zone: 'far-shore', count: 1 },
        { species: 'cangoo', behavior: 'flyover' as const, zone: 'sky', count: 14 },
      ],
    }
    expect(castAtlases(loc, specs).map(key).sort()).toEqual(['leasan:', 'mallar3:female', 'mallar3:male'])
  })
})

describe('prepaint', () => {
  it('paints every atlas in the worker, reports progress, and counts each paint for the dev overlay', async () => {
    const before = { ...paintStats }
    const { w, posted, state } = fakeWorker(() => flat(200))
    const progress: [number, number][] = []
    const jobs: AtlasJob[] = [{ code: 'fake1' }, { code: 'fake2', variant: 'female' }, { code: 'fake2', variant: 'male' }, { code: 'fake1' }]
    await prepaint(jobs, { worker: () => w, onProgress: (d, t) => progress.push([d, t]) })
    expect(posted.map(key)).toEqual(['fake1:', 'fake2:female', 'fake2:male'])       // the duplicate is painted once
    expect(progress).toEqual([[0, 3], [1, 3], [2, 3], [3, 3]])
    for (const j of jobs) expect(atlasReady(j)).toBe(true)
    expect(state.terminated).toBe(true)
    expect(paintStats.atlases - before.atlases).toBe(3)
    expect(paintStats.ms - before.ms).toBeCloseTo(120, 9)                             // the worker's 40 ms each
    expect(paintStats.bytes - before.bytes).toBe(3 * Math.round(ATLAS_SIZE * ATLAS_SIZE * 4 * 4 / 3))
  })
  it('leaves materialFor nothing to paint: it finds the worker\'s pixels ready', async () => {
    const sent = flat(90)
    const { w } = fakeWorker(() => sent)
    await prepaint([{ code: TEST_SPEC.code }], { worker: () => w })
    const before = { ...paintStats }
    const mat = materialFor(TEST_SPEC)
    expect(paintStats).toEqual(before)
    expect(same(pixelsOf(mat), sent), 'pixels from the worker').toBe(true)
    expect(materialFor(TEST_SPEC)).toBe(mat)
  })
  it('skips atlases already painted, and makes no worker when there is nothing to paint', async () => {
    let made = 0
    const progress: [number, number][] = []
    await prepaint([{ code: TEST_SPEC.code }], { worker: () => { made++; return fakeWorker(() => flat(1)).w }, onProgress: (d, t) => progress.push([d, t]) })
    expect(made).toBe(0)
    expect(progress).toEqual([[0, 0]])
  })
  it('paints on the main thread when a worker can\'t be made', async () => {
    const warn = vi.spyOn(console, 'warn').mockImplementation(() => {})
    const before = paintStats.atlases
    const progress: [number, number][] = []
    await prepaint([{ code: 'killde' }], { worker: () => { throw new Error('no workers here') }, onProgress: (d, t) => progress.push([d, t]) })
    expect(atlasReady({ code: 'killde' })).toBe(true)
    expect(progress).toEqual([[0, 1], [1, 1]])
    expect(paintStats.atlases - before).toBe(1)                                       // counted once, by paintAtlas
    expect(warn).toHaveBeenCalledOnce()
    warn.mockRestore()
  })
  it('paints on the main thread when given no worker at all, one atlas per task so the page can redraw between them', async () => {
    const seen: string[] = []
    const tick = setInterval(() => seen.push('tick'), 0)
    await prepaint([{ code: 'pecsan' }, { code: 'lobdow' }], { onProgress: d => seen.push(`done ${d}`) })
    clearInterval(tick)
    expect(atlasReady({ code: 'pecsan' }) && atlasReady({ code: 'lobdow' })).toBe(true)
    const between = seen.slice(seen.indexOf('done 1'), seen.indexOf('done 2'))
    expect(between).toContain('tick')
  })
  it('keeps what the worker painted before it failed, and paints the rest on the main thread', async () => {
    const warn = vi.spyOn(console, 'warn').mockImplementation(() => {})
    const sent = flat(30)
    const { w, state } = fakeWorker(j => j.code === 'lesyel' ? sent : 'fail')
    const progress: [number, number][] = []
    const before = paintStats.atlases
    await prepaint([{ code: 'lesyel' }, { code: 'greyel' }], { worker: () => w, onProgress: (d, t) => progress.push([d, t]) })
    expect(state.terminated).toBe(true)
    expect(progress).toEqual([[0, 2], [1, 2], [2, 2]])
    expect(paintStats.atlases - before).toBe(2)
    expect(same(pixelsOf(materialFor(getSpec('lesyel'))), sent), 'lesyel from the worker').toBe(true)
    expect(Buffer.from(pixelsOf(materialFor(getSpec('greyel'))) as Uint8ClampedArray).equals(Buffer.from(paintSpecies(getSpec('greyel'))))).toBe(true)
    expect(warn).toHaveBeenCalledOnce()
    warn.mockRestore()
  })
})
```

```ts
/** Count one atlas's paint for the dev overlay, wherever it was painted. */
function tally(ms: number, pixels: Uint8ClampedArray): void {
  paintStats.atlases++
  paintStats.ms += ms
  paintStats.bytes += Math.round(pixels.length * 4 / 3)     // with mipmaps
}
```

Then replace

```ts
const materials = new Map<string, THREE.MeshStandardMaterial>()

/** One material (and one painted atlas) per species and plumage, shared by every bird of that kind. */
export function materialFor(spec: SpeciesSpec, variant?: string): THREE.MeshStandardMaterial {
  const key = `${spec.code}:${variant ?? ''}`
  let mat = materials.get(key)
  if (!mat) {
    const detail = spec.detail
    if (!detail) throw new Error(`${spec.code}: no detail block to paint`)
    const pixels = paintAtlas({ code: spec.code, detail, layers: layersFor(detail, variant), profile: createProfile(detail.profile) })
    mat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: atlasTexture(pixels), roughness: 0.85, metalness: 0 })
    materials.set(key, mat)
  }
  return mat
}
```

with

```ts
/** One atlas: a species, and one of its versions (none: the species' own plumage). */
export interface AtlasJob { code: string; variant?: string }

/** An atlas painted off the main thread: its RGBA pixels, and how long the painting took in ms. */
export interface PaintedAtlas extends AtlasJob { pixels: Uint8ClampedArray; ms: number }

/** Paint one species' atlas for one version, here and now. */
export function paintSpecies(spec: SpeciesSpec, variant?: string): Uint8ClampedArray {
  const detail = spec.detail
  if (!detail) throw new Error(`${spec.code}: no detail block to paint`)
  return paintAtlas({ code: spec.code, detail, layers: layersFor(detail, variant), profile: createProfile(detail.profile) })
}

/** The key an atlas is cached under: one per species and version. */
export const atlasKey = ({ code, variant }: AtlasJob): string => `${code}:${variant ?? ''}`

const materials = new Map<string, THREE.MeshStandardMaterial>()
const material = (pixels: Uint8ClampedArray) => new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: atlasTexture(pixels), roughness: 0.85, metalness: 0 })

/** True once this species and version has its atlas, so materialFor won't paint it. */
export function atlasReady(job: AtlasJob): boolean {
  return materials.has(atlasKey(job))
}

/** Keep an atlas painted elsewhere (the paint worker) for materialFor, and count its paint time for the dev overlay. */
export function storeAtlas(a: PaintedAtlas): void {
  if (atlasReady(a)) return
  materials.set(atlasKey(a), material(a.pixels))
  tally(a.ms, a.pixels)
}

/**
 * One material (and one painted atlas) per species and plumage, shared by every bird of that kind. At startup
 * `prepaint` has painted every atlas the cast needs; anything else (the viewer, tests) is painted here on first use.
 */
export function materialFor(spec: SpeciesSpec, variant?: string): THREE.MeshStandardMaterial {
  const key = atlasKey({ code: spec.code, variant })
  let mat = materials.get(key)
  if (!mat) {
    mat = material(paintSpecies(spec, variant))
    materials.set(key, mat)
  }
  return mat
}
```
